**Risks:** UE Python material-graph node enumeration is shallow: MaterialEditingLibrary has no first-class 'get all expressions with connections' call. material_node_list may need MaterialEditingLibrary internals or to extend the existing C++ material_get_info, not pure Python.; Static-switch changes (material_set_static_switch) trigger full shader recompiles, which can stall the game thread; must go through the deferred-compile/auto-save pattern (PR #250) and never inline-recompile inside the python_run call.; texture_channel_pack via draw_material_to_render_target needs a helper pack material asset to exist; if absent the tool must create/ship one. GPU read-back to save as Texture2D (CreateStaticTexture from RT) can lose precision on HDR sources.; texture_import through Interchange vs legacy AssetImportTask differs across 5.7/5.8; pinning to one path risks version drift (Theme 6).; material_thumbnail / preview_on_mesh must NOT sync-capture the live world (known crash); must show-only-list the preview mesh, pre-warm shaders, split into short calls (memory: scenecapture isolate-mesh).; Reverse-reference lookups (find_references) over a large project can be slow and must paginate + use AssetRegistry, not a filesystem scan.; Bulk writes (batch_set_param, apply_to_selection) without a transaction wrap leave half-applied state on failure; depends on the Theme 4C transaction seam landing to be a single Ctrl+Z.; Reading raw texture pixels (audit non-pow2/oversize is fine from metadata, but histogram/pixel-sample needs source-art or platform data) is unreliable on cooked/streamed textures; flag as best-effort.

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live editor with the HaybaMCP plugin loaded and a small content folder (a base material with scalar+vector+texture+static-switch params, a few MICs, and a /Game/Tex folder of PNG/TGA with _D/_N/_ORM suffixes): (1) Inspection-first: material_param_list + material_instance_inspect on a known MIC and assert returned param names/defaults match the editor's MI editor; material_node_list against the base material vs the editor graph. (2) Round-trip: material_set_params_bulk then material_param_list to confirm overrides; material_param_reset and confirm values fall back to parent default. (3) Static switch: material_set_static_switch, wait_for_shaders, then render_camera/material_thumbnail to confirm the branch visibly changed. (4) Batch: spawn N actors, material_apply_to_selection + material_apply_batch by tag, then verify each component slot via material_instance_inspect; texture_batch_set_settings + texture_fix_settings_by_convention(dry_run=true) and confirm the plan flags the wrong-sRGB normal map before committing. (5) Import: texture_import a PNG, texture_get_info to confirm settings; texture_channel_pack three grayscale maps into an ORM and texture_export it to disk for eyeball check. (6) MPC: mpc_create, mpc_set a global scalar, confirm a material reading the MPC changes via material_thumbnail before/after diff. (7) Safety: run every mutating tool once with dry_run=true and assert no asset is dirtied (EditorAssetLibrary.do_assets_exist / package dirty flag), then commit and confirm a single undo reverts. (8) Audit: texture_audit + material_audit over the folder and assert violation counts match seeded bad assets. Validate visually, not just metric counts.


<a id="domain-4"></a>
## 4. pcg-and-pcgex

*34 tools — P0:7 P1:14 P2:10 P3:3*

The current PCG/PCGEx surface (~17 first-class tools) covers JSON-graph build/validate/export plus four reflection primitives (add_node/set_prop/wire/inspect_instances) and the async-safe pcg_cook_and_wait — but it has glaring authoring holes: no node/edge delete, no live topology read-back, no class enumeration, no way to instantiate a PCGComponent on an actor, no attribute-data debug readback, no dry-run, no cleanup, and no asset-level undo. Strategy: lean on python_run-backed pyTemplate wrappers (zero plugin rebuild) to fill the full author→cook→inspect→verify loop, and press the worldbuilding moat with PLUMB-validated graph linting, recipe instantiation from the sliver library, and a cook+auto-frame+screenshot vision loop that no competitor has for PCG. Every write tool ships a paired live read/inspect tool (Flop pattern), wraps mutations in a graph-asset snapshot (FScopedTransaction can't capture python asset edits), and routes through validate-before/after. Reserve C++ only for a hot-path declarative cook-stats probe if python proves too slow; everything else stays no-rebuild.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pcg_graph_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); reuses PY_RESOLVE_NODE from pcg-primitives.ts | GenOrca has material-graph readback; net-new for PCG | Read-back the live topology of a PCGGraph asset (nodes with index+label+settings-class, real pin labels, edges, exposed params) so the agent can reason about a graph it didn't build — closes the round-trip gap. — `(graph_path, include_props?:bool) -> {nodes:[{index,label,settings_class,props?}], edges:[{from,from_pin,to,to_pin}], params, input_pins, output_pins}` |
| `pcg_node_list_classes` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); unreal class iteration | net-new moat (Houdini docs-as-a-tool analog) | Enumerate installed PCG + PCGEx settings classes via UE reflection (filter by keyword/category), so the agent picks real class names instead of guessing — fixes the postmortem 'no reflection-introspection' gap. — `(filter?:str, category?:Literal, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {classes:[{settings_class,display_name,category}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `pcg_remove_node` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); g.remove_node + snapshot wrap | net-new (chongdashu has BP node delete; none for PCG) | Delete a node from a PCGGraph asset and drop its incident edges in one transaction — there is no delete today, blocking iterative authoring. — `(graph_path, node:str\|int, prune_dangling_edges?=true) -> {ok, removed_label, removed_edges:int}` |
| `pcg_unwire` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); g.remove_edge | net-new | Remove a single edge between two node pins on a live graph — the inverse of pcg_wire, missing today. — `(graph_path, from_node, from_pin?='Out', to_node, to_pin?='In') -> {ok, removed:bool}` |
| `pcg_component_bind` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); add_component_by_class + set graph | net-new moat | Add a PCGComponent to an existing actor (or create a PCG volume) and bind a graph asset to it — without this you can author a graph but never run it on a real actor. — `(actor, graph_path, generation_trigger?:Literal, runtime_generated?=false) -> {ok, actor_path, component, graph_bound}` |
| `pcg_graph_lint` | P0 | python_run-backed TS; live introspect + reuse validate-attribute-flow logic + plumb_validate | Houdini graph-intelligence validate; net-new for PCG (deeper than Flop auto-repair) | Opinionated structural lint of a LIVE graph asset: orphan nodes, unconnected required input pins, missing/zero seed, obvious pin-type mismatches — each with an actionable fix string and PLUMB constraint check. — `(graph_path, strict?=false) -> {ok, issues:[{severity,node,pin,detail,suggested_fix}], plumb:{sat,unsat_core?}}` |
| `pcg_cook_and_wait` | P0 | existing TS (pcg-cook-and-wait.ts); wait_for_idle pcg subsystem | net-new moat (async-correct readback) | EXISTING — keep. Regenerate an actor's PCGComponent and read back ISM instance counts in one async-safe call (plain generate reads 0 same-frame). — `(actor, timeout_s?=120) -> {ok, cook, idle, result:{ism:[{mesh,count}], total}}` |
| `pcg_set_props_batch` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); extends pcg_set_prop | Roblox mass_set_property / Unity batch wiring; net-new for PCG | Set many editor-properties on one node in a single transaction-wrapped call (supports nested struct paths) — collapses N round-trips and the struct write-back footgun. — `(graph_path, node, props:[{path,value}]) -> {ok, set:int, failed:[{path,error}]}` |
| `pcg_build_graph_steps` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); orchestrates primitives under one snapshot | Unity batch wiring; net-new for PCG | Outcome verb: add a batch of nodes, set their props, and wire them in ONE snapshot/undo unit — the high-level author primitive vs hand-rolling add_node/set_prop/wire loops. — `(graph_path, nodes:[{key,settings_class,props?}], edges:[{from_key,from_pin?,to_key,to_pin?}]) -> {ok, added:{key->index}, wired:int, errors}` |
| `pcg_attribute_readback` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); PCGComponent generated data inspection | net-new moat (UE attribute-viewer as a tool) | Dump the attribute set + a sampled slice of point data (position/density/named attrs) from a cooked component's output — the agent's only window into WHAT the graph produced, for debugging attribute flow. — `(actor, sample?=10, attributes?:[str]) -> {ok, point_count, attributes:[{name,type}], sample:[{...}]}` |
| `pcg_component_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Flop inspection-first pattern; net-new | Read a PCGComponent's state: bound graph, generation trigger, runtime/partitioned flags, last cook duration + output point count — paired read for every cook/bind write tool. — `(actor) -> {ok, components:[{graph,trigger,runtime,partitioned,last_cook_ms?,point_count?,generated:bool}]}` |
| `pcg_spawn_and_scatter` | P1 | python_run-backed TS; composes spawn+bind+cook_and_wait+preview_capture | net-new moat | Worldbuilding outcome verb: spawn a PCG volume over a target/landscape, bind a scatter graph, cook, read back counts, and auto-frame screenshot — one call from nothing to verified scatter. — `(target_actor, graph_path\|recipe, bounds?, seed?) -> {ok, actor, total_instances, image}` |
| `pcg_clear_generated` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); PCGComponent.cleanup | net-new | Clear an actor/component's generated PCG output (instances + transient data) — cleanup before regen or to reclaim editor memory; no cleanup verb exists today. — `(actor, all_components?=true) -> {ok, cleared:int}` |
| `pcg_cook_dry_run` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); inspect graph + sampler density math, no commit | Flop bp_dry_run / our world_generate dry_run; net-new for PCG | Estimate the result of cooking WITHOUT committing instances: report execution node order and a point-count estimate per output, so the agent previews cost before a heavy generate. — `(actor\|graph_path, bounds?) -> {ok, plan:[{node,est_points}], total_est, warnings}` |
| `pcg_snapshot` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); EditorAssetLibrary.duplicate_asset | Unity undo/redo; net-new moat (named-checkpoint multi-step undo) | Duplicate a PCGGraph asset into a named checkpoint slot before edits — asset-level undo, because C++ FScopedTransaction cannot capture python-driven asset mutations. — `(graph_path, label?) -> {ok, snapshot_id, backup_path}` |
| `pcg_restore` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Restore a PCGGraph asset from a prior pcg_snapshot checkpoint — the rollback half of the snapshot pair. — `(snapshot_id) -> {ok, restored_path}` |
| `pcg_components_generate_all` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); EditorActorSubsystem loop + wait_for_idle | Roblox mass_duplicate / Unity batch; net-new for PCG | Batch-cook every (or selected, or graph-filtered) PCGComponent in the level and aggregate counts — bulk regen after a shared subgraph edit. — `(scope:Literal[all,selection,by_graph], graph_path?, timeout_s?=300) -> {ok, cooked:int, total_instances, per_actor:[...]}` |
| `pcg_runtime_gen_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Toggle runtime/partitioned generation and the generation trigger on a component — controls whether PCG cooks at runtime, on-demand, or on-load. — `(actor, runtime_generated?:bool, partitioned?:bool, trigger?:Literal) -> {ok, applied}` |
| `pcg_recipe_apply` | P1 | TS sliver registry (extends scatter/pcg_biome.ts) + pyTemplate build | Blender asset-library / Maya parametric prefab; net-new moat | Instantiate a known-good, parameterized PCG graph recipe (scatter / spline-mesh / fence / forest-biome) from the sliver library into a new asset — worldbuilding depth competitors lack. — `(recipe:Literal, name, params:{density,mesh,seed,...}) -> {ok, graph_path, recipe, resolved_params}` |
| `pcg_preview_capture` | P1 | python_run-backed TS; composes cook_and_wait + editor_capture_viewport/render_camera | net-new moat (vision feedback for PCG) | Cook a PCG actor, auto-frame the camera on its generated bounds, and return a multi-angle screenshot for visual verification — extends the vision moat to PCG output. — `(actor, angles?=['front','top','iso'], cook?=true) -> {ok, images:[ImageContent], total_instances}` |
| `pcg_pin_resolve` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); node pin properties | net-new (chongdashu has BP pin listing; none for PCG) | List a node's REAL pin labels (internal vs display) on a live asset, exposing traps like PCGEx Staging's visible 'Map' = internal 'CollectionSource' — the live, authoritative version of the offline match_pin_names heuristic. — `(graph_path, node) -> {ok, input_pins:[{display,internal,type}], output_pins:[...]}` |
| `pcg_node_describe_live` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); class default object property walk | GenOrca get_node_details; net-new live variant | Reflection dump of a settings class's editor-properties (names, types, enum options, defaults) from the running engine — complements the offline query_pcgex_docs DB when a class isn't catalogued. — `(settings_class) -> {ok, properties:[{name,type,default,enum_options?}], pins:[...]}` |
| `pcg_graph_set_param_default` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); user-parameters editing | net-new | Set the default value of an exposed (overridable) graph parameter on a PCGGraph asset, so instances inherit sane defaults. — `(graph_path, param_name, value) -> {ok, param, value}` |
| `pcg_graph_add_io_pin` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); PCGGraphInputOutputSettings | net-new | Add or remove an exposed graph input/output pin (the boundary interface used when a graph is called as a subgraph). — `(graph_path, direction:Literal[input,output], pin_name, type, remove?=false) -> {ok, pins}` |
| `pcg_component_set_override` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); PCGComponent parameter overrides | net-new | Set a per-component graph-parameter override (graph instance) so two actors share one graph asset with different density/seed/mesh — instancing without asset duplication. — `(actor, overrides:[{param,value}]) -> {ok, applied:int}` |
| `pcg_subgraph_inline` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Inline/expand a Subgraph (ExecuteSubgraph) node back into its parent graph — the runtime inverse of the existing offline abstract_to_subgraph. — `(graph_path, subgraph_node) -> {ok, inlined_nodes:int, rewired_edges:int}` |
| `pcgex_cluster_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); PCGEx cluster data attributes | net-new moat (PCGEx depth) | Read back PCGEx Vtx/Edges cluster data (vertex count, edge count, sample positions, island count) from a cooked output — PCGEx-specific debug visibility nothing else offers. — `(actor, sample?=10) -> {ok, vtx_count, edge_count, islands?, sample_vtx:[...]}` |
| `pcg_node_set_position` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); node.set_node_position | net-new utility | Set a node's graph-editor position for human-readable layout (manual counterpart to the auto-layout format_graph_topology). — `(graph_path, node, x, y) -> {ok}` |
| `pcg_graph_diff_live` | P2 | python_run-backed TS; two live introspects + structural diff | net-new (Unity compare_instances analog) | Diff two live PCGGraph assets (or asset vs JSON spec): node/edge/property adds, removes, changes — extends the JSON-only diff_against_working_asset to real assets for safe review before overwrite. — `(graph_a, graph_b\|spec_json) -> {added:[...], removed:[...], changed:[...]}` |
| `pcg_find_components` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); actor scan | Roblox search_by_property; net-new for PCG | Query the level for actors with PCGComponents bound to a given graph asset — impact analysis before editing a shared graph (what will I break?). — `(graph_path) -> {actors:[{label,path,partitioned,generated}], count}` |
| `pcg_validate_before_after` | P2 | python_run-backed TS; composes snapshot + plumb_validate + attribute_readback | net-new moat (PLUMB closed-loop) | Wrap a mutation: snapshot point-counts + PLUMB/validator state before AND after a cook, returning the delta and any new violations — productizes the validate-before/after-mutate invariant for PCG. — `(actor, mutate_tool, mutate_args) -> {before:{points,plumb}, after:{points,plumb}, delta, regressions:[...]}` |
| `pcg_seed_sweep` | P3 | python_run-backed TS; loops component override seed + preview_capture | net-new (our plumb_study analog) | Cook a component across N seeds and return a thumbnail + instance count per seed for fast variation review — study-style exploration of procedural variance. — `(actor, seeds:[int]\|count, capture?=true) -> {results:[{seed,total,image?}]}` |
| `pcg_graph_export_doc` | P3 | python_run-backed TS; live introspect -> markdown | Houdini docs-as-a-tool; net-new | Generate a Markdown topology + parameter reference for a graph asset (nodes, wiring, exposed params, recipe lineage) — docs-as-a-tool for handoff and review. — `(graph_path) -> {markdown, node_count, param_count}` |
| `pcgex_pathfind_preview` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate); cook + path data readback | net-new moat (PCGEx depth) | Run a PCGEx pathfinding/graph subgraph and read back path point counts, total length, and a sampled polyline — verify graph/path generation without leaving the agent loop. — `(actor, sample?=20) -> {ok, paths:[{points,length}], sample_polyline:[...]}` |

**Dependencies:** pcg-primitives.ts PY_RESOLVE_NODE/_load_graph/_node_label/_resolve_node shared helpers (UE 5.7 verified: graph.nodes, node.node_title, pin.properties.label); Hardened python_run handler (SEH + dangling-delegate gate #283/#284) — all pyTemplate tools route through it; never open TCP from inside python_run; wait_for_idle with subsystems:['pcg'] + pcg_actors for async cook settling (pcg_cook_and_wait already depends on it); Theme-1 add-a-tool refactor: descriptors + pyTemplate factory (Section 5 #4) so these ~33 tools land as data, no per-tool 4-file churn; PLUMB validator (plumb_validate, validator_run) for lint / validate_before_after / spawn_and_scatter green-red gating; Sliver registry + scatter/pcg_biome.ts for pcg_recipe_apply; existing catalog.ts + pcgex docs sqlite DB for describe/list fallbacks; Vision tools editor_capture_viewport / render_camera for preview_capture / seed_sweep; Memory fact: SurfaceSampler needs a REAL imported Landscape (landscape_import handler), not the LandscapePlaceholder spawn stub — recipes/spawn_and_scatter must target a real Landscape; Transaction/snapshot relies on EditorAssetLibrary.duplicate_asset (asset-level), NOT FScopedTransaction, which cannot capture python asset edits

**Risks:** PCG Python reflection is unstable across UE versions: add_node_of_type returns a tuple, node_title is often empty/'None', and edge APIs (add_edge/remove_edge) vary — tools must probe-and-fallback like existing primitives and be re-verified on engine bumps; PCGComponent.generate is async; any readback (attribute_readback, pcgex_cluster_inspect, instance counts) must go through wait_for_idle or it reads stale/zero same-frame; Generated PCG point/attribute data is transient (not persisted): attribute_readback/cluster_inspect only work right after a cook while data is resident; cleared by pcg_clear_generated or GC; Partitioned/runtime-generated PCG needs World Partition cells loaded; counts and readback are unreliable on unloaded cells — flag partitioned state in component_inspect; PCGEx classes only exist if the PcgEx plugin is installed; pcgex_* tools and node_list_classes must degrade gracefully (installed=false) rather than throw; Heavy cooks can exceed executor timeouts and (pre-Theme-0 fix) freeze the game thread — keep dry_run/estimate as the default preview, gate generate_all behind explicit confirm + job-envelope timeouts; cook_dry_run point estimates are approximate (sampler density math, not a real cook) — must be labelled estimate to avoid false confidence; Snapshot via asset duplication can clutter content if not GC'd — restore should clean the backup; namespace snapshots under /Game/_pcg_snapshots/

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live UE 5.7 editor with PCG + PCGExtendedToolkit plugins and a REAL imported Landscape (not the placeholder stub): 1) pcg_node_list_classes(filter='Sampler') confirms PCGSurfaceSamplerSettings + PCGEx classes enumerate. 2) pcg_recipe_apply('scatter', name='T_Scatter', {mesh, density:0.5, seed:1337}) builds an asset; pcg_graph_inspect round-trips the same nodes/edges; pcg_graph_lint reports 0 errors. 3) pcg_snapshot the asset, then pcg_remove_node + pcg_unwire + pcg_set_props_batch, re-inspect to confirm deltas, then pcg_restore and confirm the graph matches the snapshot. 4) Spawn a volume over the Landscape via pcg_component_bind (or pcg_spawn_and_scatter), pcg_cook_dry_run gives a point estimate, then cook_and_wait and confirm instance total is non-zero and within ~order of the estimate. 5) pcg_attribute_readback returns position/density samples; pcg_component_inspect shows bound graph + last_cook stats. 6) pcg_preview_capture returns front/top/iso PNGs that visibly show scattered meshes on terrain (validate visually, not just counts — per memory). 7) pcg_seed_sweep over 4 seeds shows 4 visibly different scatters. 8) pcg_components_generate_all(scope='by_graph') re-cooks all bound actors. 9) Negative paths: bind a graph with an unconnected required pin -> pcg_graph_lint flags it with a fix; pcgex_cluster_inspect on a non-PCGEx output returns a clean 'no cluster data' rather than throwing. Cross-check hayba_journal_tail shows no SEH catches or dangling-delegate blocks during the run.


<a id="domain-5"></a>
## 5. landscape-and-terrain

*31 tools — P0:6 P1:11 P2:10 P3:4*

Today the domain is a single tool (hayba_import_landscape → C++ Cmd_ImportLandscape in HaybaMCPLandscapeImporter.cpp); the brief targets ~10, and competitors (Epic 5.8, Aura, GenOrca 253, Flop) have essentially zero real landscape sculpt/paint depth — this is open ground for a net-new moat. Strategy: (1) make the existing import path honest and inspectable first (list/inspect/validate/dry-run read tools — Flop inspection-first), (2) lean the flagship into a Gaea→UE bridge (the repo already carries a deep Gaea transcript+docs corpus that no rival has) so landscape_import_gaea_build ingests heightmap+named masks+material in ONE undo, (3) reserve C++ (a new HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler over FLandscapeEditDataInterface) only for the operations UE Python genuinely cannot do — height/weight data edits, multi-step single-Ctrl+Z transactions, and buffer-viz/export readback — while doing material/layer-info/Nanite/grass setup as no-rebuild python_run wrappers. Every write tool pairs with a read/verify tool, transaction-wraps, dry-runs, and round-trips through PLUMB/validator + the vision loop (top-down auto-frame + slope/height buffer-viz + before/after diff). This wins on the moats (crash-resilience, transactional one-undo, quantified validation, vision feedback, worldbuilding depth) rather than raw count, while still out-tooling every competitor in this specific subsystem.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `landscape_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/list.ts; EditorActorSubsystem + unreal.Landscape/LandscapeProxy) | net-new moat (no UE MCP enumerates landscapes; analog to Roblox get_descendants / Unity paginated hierarchy) | Enumerate all landscape actors + streaming proxies with resolution, component layout, bounds and material — the read entry point so agents stop guessing actor names. — `(limit?, offset?, name_filter?) -> {landscapes:[{actor_label, guid, size_x, size_y, components, sections_per_component, quads_per_section, world_bounds, material, proxy_count, is_world_partition}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `landscape_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/inspect.ts) | Flop inspection-first pattern; net-new for landscape specifics | Deep brief of one landscape: exact section/quad/component config, XYZ scale, min/max sampled height, paint layers + weightmap usage, proxy coverage, material layer-info completeness — paired read tool for every write op. — `(actor_label \| guid) -> {size, component_layout, scale, height_range_m, layers:[{name, layer_info, weightmap_present}], proxies:[{label, loaded}], material, holes, warnings[]}` |
| `landscape_import_heightmap` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeImporter.cpp (existing Cmd_ImportLandscape; extend params + structured return) | GenOrca/Gaea bridges have import; we make it transactional + inspectable | Import a PNG/R16 heightmap as an ALandscape (harden + keep the one tool that exists today; add explicit component-sizing + offset/rotation params and structured readback). — `(heightmap_path, world_size_km=8, max_height_m=600, actor_label, landscape_material?, location?, rotation?) -> {actor_label, size_x, size_y, component_layout, scale, ok}` |
| `landscape_import_gaea_build` | P0 | MIX — TS orchestrator (src/tools/landscape/gaea-build.ts) over C++ importer + weightmap handler, transaction-wrapped | net-new moat (Gaea bridge; steals Blender 'import build' ergonomics, exploits our Gaea knowledge base) | Flagship outcome verb: ingest a Gaea export folder (heightmap R16 + named mask PNGs + optional material) into ONE landscape with auto-created LayerInfo assets, weightmaps wired, in a single undo — the Gaea→UE workflow the repo's whole transcript corpus documents and no rival ships. — `(folder_path, world_size_km, max_height_m, mask_to_layer_map?, material?, actor_label) -> {actor_label, layers_created[], weightmaps_imported[], unmapped_masks[], ok}` |
| `landscape_validate` | P0 | python_run-backed TS + validator hooks (src/tools/landscape/validate.ts, validator_run) | net-new moat (PLUMB quantified validation > Flop auto-repair / Houdini graph-intel) | Quantified PLUMB/validator pass over a landscape: no holes, heights in range, per-pixel weights sum to ~1, no NaN, full proxy coverage, every material layer slot has a LayerInfo — green/red before/after mutate. — `(actor_label, rules?) -> {ok, violations:[{rule, count, sample_locations, severity}]}` |
| `landscape_transaction` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (FScopedTransaction over edit handlers) | net-new moat (multi-step single-undo; Epic/GenOrca do per-call only) | Begin/commit/abort a landscape edit bundle so a whole sculpt+paint sequence collapses to ONE Ctrl+Z — the named differentiator vs Epic's per-call undo. — `(action: 'begin'\|'commit'\|'abort', label?) -> {transaction_id, depth, ok}` |
| `landscape_create_blank` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeImporter.cpp (reuse Import path with zeroed heightmap) | net-new (real Landscape, not the placeholder stub spawn) | Spawn a flat ALandscape with explicit section/quad/component sizing and target height range without needing a heightmap file — fast scaffold for procedural pipelines. — `(quads_per_section, sections_per_component, component_count_x, component_count_y, world_size_km, actor_label, material?) -> {actor_label, size_x, size_y, ok}` |
| `landscape_import_weightmaps` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (FLandscapeEditDataInterface::SetAlphaData) | net-new moat (Gaea bridge) | Import one or many grayscale masks as paint-layer weightmaps onto an existing landscape (the standalone Gaea-mask step), creating LayerInfo as needed. — `(actor_label, masks:[{path, layer_name}], normalize=true) -> {imported[], created_layers[], ok}` |
| `landscape_export_heightmap` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (FLandscapeEditDataInterface::GetHeightData) | net-new moat (readback/round-trip) | Read the live heightmap back to PNG/R16 for round-trip verify, external editing, or before/after diff — the verify half of the loop nobody else offers. — `(actor_label, out_path, format='r16'\|'png16', region?) -> {out_path, size_x, size_y, min_h, max_h, ok}` |
| `landscape_sculpt_apply_heightmap` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (FLandscapeEditDataInterface SetHeightData) | net-new (no competitor sculpts landscape height data) | Stamp/blend a heightmap image into a region with replace/add/min/max mode + edge falloff — procedural detail injection without re-importing the whole landscape. — `(actor_label, heightmap_path, region_rect, mode='add'\|'replace'\|'min'\|'max', strength=1, falloff_px=0) -> {pixels_changed, height_range_after, ok}` |
| `landscape_add_layer` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/add-layer.ts; LandscapeLayerInfoObject + material slot reconcile) | net-new (layer-info authoring) | Create a LandscapeLayerInfoObject asset and bind it to a named layer slot of the landscape material — prerequisite for any painting, done without a rebuild. — `(actor_label, layer_name, weight_blend=true, physical_material?, layer_info_path?) -> {layer_info_path, slot_bound, ok}` |
| `landscape_layer_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/layer-list.ts) | net-new | List paint layers on a landscape with their LayerInfo asset, blend mode and whether a weightmap actually exists — read tool that exposes painting state. — `(actor_label) -> {layers:[{name, layer_info, blend, weightmap_present, coverage_pct}]}` |
| `landscape_paint_by_rule` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (height+normal sample → SetAlphaData) | net-new moat (auto-mask/auto-texture) | Auto-paint a layer by slope/altitude rules (rock on steep, snow above Z, grass on flat low) — the Gaea-style auto-texture outcome verb; mask-aware so it never clobbers hand paint. — `(actor_label, layer_name, slope_min?, slope_max?, height_min_m?, height_max_m?, strength=1, restrict_mask?) -> {pixels_painted, ok}` |
| `landscape_set_material` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/set-material.ts) | net-new (material + layer reconcile) | Assign/swap the landscape material and reconcile layer-info slots so painting keeps working after a material change. — `(actor_label, material_path, reconcile_layers=true) -> {assigned, layer_slots, missing_layer_info[], ok}` |
| `landscape_enable_nanite` | P1 | python_run-backed TS over job-envelope (src/tools/landscape/nanite.ts) | steal-Theme3 (Nanite); net-new for landscape | Toggle Nanite on a landscape, set position precision, and trigger the data rebuild through the job-envelope so it does not freeze the editor (Theme-3 Nanite easy win applied to terrain). — `(actor_label, enable=true, position_precision?) -> {nanite_enabled, build_job_id, ok}` |
| `landscape_dry_run` | P1 | MIX — TS planner (src/tools/landscape/dry-run.ts) + C++ no-op estimator + vision capture | net-new moat (dry-run generalization, matches Flop bp_dry_run intent) | Preview an import/sculpt/paint op: predicted component layout, scale, affected region count, and a before/after thumbnail — mutates NOTHING. Generalizes world_generate's dry_run to terrain. — `(op, actor_label?, params) -> {predicted_layout, predicted_scale, affected_pixels, warnings[], preview_png?}` |
| `landscape_capture_topdown` | P1 | MIX — TS (src/tools/landscape/capture.ts) over render_camera + C++ buffer-viz pass | extends our strongest area; net-new buffer-viz for terrain | Auto-framed top-down + perspective capture of a landscape with optional height/slope/layer-weight buffer-viz overlay and before/after diff — returns MIME image blocks for the agent's eyes. — `(actor_label, mode='lit'\|'height'\|'slope'\|'layer_weight', layer_name?, diff_against?) -> {images:[image/png blocks], camera}` |
| `landscape_paint_layer_region` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (SetAlphaData) | net-new | Paint a single layer's weight in a rect with value + falloff + optional restrict-mask — the low-level brush behind paint_by_rule. — `(actor_label, layer_name, region_rect, value=1, falloff_px=0, restrict_mask?) -> {pixels_painted, ok}` |
| `landscape_sculpt_flatten` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp | net-new | Flatten a region to a target Z (explicit or sampled from a point) with falloff — common road/building-pad terrain prep. — `(actor_label, region_rect, target_z_m?, sample_point?, falloff_px=0) -> {pixels_changed, ok}` |
| `landscape_fill_layer` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp | net-new | Fill or clear an entire layer's weight (e.g. base layer to 1, scratch layer to 0) — batch reset for painting workflows. — `(actor_label, layer_name, value=1) -> {ok}` |
| `landscape_export_weightmap` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (GetAlphaData) | net-new (readback) | Export a layer's weightmap to an image for inspection/diff/external editing — the verify half of painting. — `(actor_label, layer_name, out_path, format='png') -> {out_path, coverage_pct, ok}` |
| `landscape_setup_autolayers` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/autolayers.ts) | net-new (outcome verb, Maya parametric-prefab spirit) | Given a layer-blend material, create a LayerInfo asset for every layer expression name and bind them in one call — eliminates the tedious manual layer-info dance. — `(actor_label, material_path, weight_blend=true) -> {layers_created[], slots_bound, ok}` |
| `landscape_spline_add` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (ULandscapeSplinesComponent) | net-new (landscape splines) | Add a landscape spline (roads/rivers) from a list of world-space control points with width/falloff — terrain-aware path authoring. — `(actor_label, points:[{location, width?}], spline_width=400, falloff=200) -> {spline_segments, control_points, ok}` |
| `landscape_grass_assign` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/grass.ts) | net-new | Assign a LandscapeGrassType to a paint layer so weight drives procedural grass/foliage — the landscape↔foliage seam. — `(actor_label, layer_name, grass_type_path) -> {assigned, ok}` |
| `landscape_batch_set_property` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/batch-property.ts) | matches Roblox mass_set_property / Unity batch wiring | Set a property (LOD bias, cast-shadow, collision, Nanite flag, scalability) across many landscape proxies/actors matched by query — the unglamorous batch op. — `(name_filter \| guids[], property, value) -> {affected[], failed[], ok}` |
| `landscape_build_data` | P2 | MIX — TS job-envelope wrapper over C++ build | net-new for landscape; respects Theme-0 async | Rebuild grass maps, physical-material masks and Nanite data via the job-envelope (no game-thread freeze) — the post-edit bake step. — `(actor_label, targets=['grass','nanite','physmat']) -> {build_job_id, ok}` |
| `landscape_sculpt_set_region` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp (SetHeightData) | net-new | Set raw uint16 height values for a rect from an array/path — low-level primitive for procedural/sim-driven terrain pipelines. — `(actor_label, region_rect, height_data_path) -> {pixels_changed, ok}` |
| `landscape_adjust_zscale` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/landscape/zscale.ts) | net-new | Re-fit the height range / Z-scale of an existing landscape without re-importing — quick exaggerate/flatten of relief. — `(actor_label, max_height_m \| z_scale) -> {old_scale, new_scale, ok}` |
| `landscape_sculpt_ramp` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp | net-new | Region smooth / add-noise / ramp brush ops for finishing passes (de-stairstep imported data, add micro-relief). — `(actor_label, region_rect, op='smooth'\|'noise'\|'ramp', amount=0.5, params?) -> {pixels_changed, ok}` |
| `landscape_spline_apply_deformation` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp | net-new | Deform the landscape to a spline (carve a road bed / river channel and flatten its shoulders). — `(actor_label, spline_id, raise_terrain=false, lower_terrain=true) -> {pixels_changed, ok}` |
| `landscape_resize` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp | net-new | Change a landscape's component/section configuration (UE resize op) for performance or LOD tuning — Plan-Mode gated, validate-after. — `(actor_label, quads_per_section, sections_per_component, resize_mode='resample'\|'clip'\|'expand') -> {new_layout, ok}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 0 gate: build_*/test_* job-envelope conversion and SEH-wrap of ProcessCommand must land before any landscape build/rebuild tool ships unattended (landscape_build_data, enable_nanite trigger game-thread builds that freeze today); Theme 1 gate: add-a-tool descriptor refactor + pyTemplate factory — most python tools here ship as descriptors with zero rebuild only after this lands; Existing C++: HaybaMCPLandscapeImporter.cpp (Cmd_ImportLandscape) — extend, do not fork; New C++ module HaybaMCPLandscapeHandler.cpp wrapping FLandscapeEditDataInterface (engine module: Landscape, LandscapeEditor) for all height/weight data edits — requires plugin rebuild + EngineVersion gating; flag as non-marketplace (engine-private headers); Game-thread marshal seam HaybaGameThread::RunSync (PR #254) — every landscape edit is game-thread-only (check IsInGameThread already enforced in importer); World Partition: ALandscapeStreamingProxy enumeration + force-load of all loaded-region proxies before any data edit; PLUMB validator + validator_run hooks for landscape_validate; vision loop (render_camera/editor_capture_viewport) for capture/dry-run; Gaea knowledge corpus already in repo (src/gaea/transcripts + knowledge/gaea-docs) — source of mask-name → layer-name conventions for the Gaea bridge; 1MB TCP frame cap — heightmap/weightmap blobs must transfer as file paths or be chunked, never inline base64

**Risks:** UE Python landscape API is thin: heightmap/weightmap pixel edits, layer-info creation in-material, and sculpt brushes are effectively C++-only (FLandscapeEditDataInterface). Realistically ~12 of these need a plugin rebuild, which conflicts with 'no-rebuild-by-default' — sequence them behind the new C++ handler and clearly tag which are python vs C++.; World Partition landscapes are split into streaming proxies; editing only loaded proxies silently corrupts/partial-edits the rest (echoes the 2026-05-23 PCG/landscape postmortem: success:true is NOT proof). Every edit tool must enumerate + force-load proxies and report coverage, or refuse.; FLandscapeImportHelper caps ~8193/side and ChooseBestComponentSizeForImport can yield degenerate sizes — dry-run must surface the chosen component layout BEFORE mutate (importer already rejects out-of-range).; Nanite-landscape enable and grass/data rebuilds run synchronous game-thread builds → the Theme-0 freeze bug; must go through the job-envelope or they hang the editor.; Large weightmap/heightmap transfer exceeds the 1MB frame cap — must pass file paths, not inline pixels.; Gaea bridge depends on mask-filename → layer-name conventions; ambiguous/unknown masks must fail loud with a mapping report, not guess (honesty invariant).; Auto-paint-by-slope/altitude can overwrite hand-painted weights — must be transaction-wrapped, dry-runnable, and mask-aware so it never silently destroys existing work.; Spline/deformation and resize ops are heavy and can desync collision/Nanite — gate behind Plan-Mode + validate-after.

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation on a blank UE 5.7 map plus a World-Partition map: (1) Read path first — landscape_list on empty map returns []; spawn via landscape_import_heightmap with a known 1009x1009 R16, then landscape_inspect must report matching SizeX/SizeY, section/quad/component counts, scale, bounds, min/max height that agree with the importer log. (2) Round-trip — landscape_export_heightmap then re-import into a second actor and diff; pixel delta must be ~0 (proves readback fidelity). (3) Gaea bridge — point landscape_import_gaea_build at a real Gaea export folder (height R16 + Rock/Soil/Snow mask PNGs + material); assert landscape spawned, 3 LayerInfo assets created+assigned, weightmaps non-zero (export each + check histograms), and the whole thing is ONE Ctrl+Z (transaction bundle). (4) Sculpt/paint — landscape_sculpt_flatten a rect then inspect min/max in that region == target Z; landscape_paint_by_rule rock-on-slope then export rock weightmap and confirm weight correlates with slope buffer. (5) Vision — landscape_capture_topdown returns MIME image content blocks for height + slope buffer-viz + before/after diff around a sculpt op. (6) Dry-run — landscape_dry_run on an oversized heightmap returns predicted layout + warning and mutates nothing (landscape_list unchanged). (7) Safety — landscape_validate flags an intentionally introduced hole / out-of-range height / weights-not-summing-to-1; undo after a transaction bundle restores the prior inspect snapshot exactly. (8) WP coverage — on a streaming-proxy map, run a region edit with only some proxies loaded and confirm the tool either force-loads all or refuses with a coverage report. (9) No-freeze — landscape_enable_nanite + landscape_build_data return via job-envelope without blocking the game thread past the executor ceiling.


<a id="domain-6"></a>
## 6. foliage-and-scatter

*37 tools — P0:8 P1:15 P2:10 P3:4*

The existing C++ FoliageHandler advertises 4 commands but two of them (foliage_add_instance, foliage_paint_at) are silent stubs that return success without persisting anything — a direct honesty-invariant violation. The strategy is to re-platform the whole domain on python_run-backed pyTemplate descriptors using the working `unreal.FoliageEditorLibrary` / `InstancedFoliageActor` / `ProceduralFoliageComponent` / HISM APIs, fixing the false-advertised tools with zero plugin rebuild. We out-tool every competitor by shipping a single reusable scatter sampling engine (ground-trace, slope/altitude/mask filters, Poisson relaxation, deterministic seeds) exposed as outcome-verbs, paired with inspection/read-back, dry-run preview, transaction-wrapped mutation, and PLUMB density-constraint validation — none of which Epic, Aura, Flop, or GenOrca offer for foliage. Every write tool is chunked into job-envelope-friendly batches so large scatters never freeze the game thread, and verification closes through a top-down density heatmap and before/after vision capture. Net-new moats: quantified density/spacing validation, reproducible scatter manifests, and terrain-reconform after sculpt edits.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `foliage_capability_probe` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (steal: ChiR24/remiphilippe graceful-degradation probe) | Report which UE Python foliage/proc-foliage/HISM bindings are available + whether a Landscape/collision surface exists; gates graceful degradation before any scatter (get_*_status pattern). — `() -> {has_foliage_lib, has_proc_foliage, has_hism, surface_actors[], engine_version}` |
| `foliage_type_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | GenOrca/Flop have asset-create; net-new for typed foliage | Create a FoliageType_InstancedStaticMesh asset from a StaticMesh with sane defaults; the entry point for all foliage authoring. — `(static_mesh_path, dest_path, name, defaults?) -> {ok, foliage_type_path}` |
| `foliage_add_instances` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) — replaces HaybaMCPFoliageHandler stub | net-new moat (existing C++ command is a no-op stub) | Bulk-add explicit world transforms for a foliage type via FoliageEditorLibrary.add_instances (FIXES the silent C++ stub); chunked to avoid game-thread freeze. — `(foliage_type_path, transforms[], chunk_size=2000) -> {added, instance_count}` |
| `foliage_scatter_paint` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (C++ paint is non-persisting + non-deterministic) | Area scatter: ground-trace onto landscape/collision within radius/box at a density, with yaw/scale jitter and a deterministic seed (FIXES foliage_paint_at stub). — `(foliage_type_path, center\|box, radius, density_per_m2, seed, scale_range?, align_normal?) -> {painted, skipped_no_hit, seed}` |
| `foliage_scatter_dry_run` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) + render_camera | net-new moat (Flop bp_dry_run only; none for scatter) | Compute the exact transforms a scatter WOULD place (same engine+seed) without committing; returns count, sample, and a heatmap PNG — generalizes world_generate's dry_run to foliage. — `(scatter_spec) -> {count, sample_transforms[], heatmap_png, seed}` |
| `foliage_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (inspection-first, Flop pattern) | Read-back of all foliage types in the level: per-type instance count, world bounds, and key params; the mandatory paired read tool. — `(limit=50, offset=0) -> {types:[{path,count,bounds,density,cull}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `foliage_remove_in_bounds` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | existing C++ (works) — re-platformed for consistency | Remove instances of a type inside a box or sphere; transaction-wrapped, returns exact removed count. — `(foliage_type_path, box\|sphere) -> {removed}` |
| `foliage_clear_type` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Remove ALL instances of a foliage type from the level (destructive, Plan-Mode gated, one-undo). — `(foliage_type_path, confirm) -> {removed}` |
| `foliage_type_set_params` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (no competitor exposes typed foliage params) | Set density, scale range, align-to-normal, random-yaw, z-offset, collision, cast-shadow, start/end cull distance on a foliage type; the core authoring knobs. — `(foliage_type_path, params{}) -> {ok, changed_keys[]}` |
| `foliage_type_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (inspection-first) | Read every authored param of a single foliage type for verify/diff before mutate. — `(foliage_type_path) -> {params{}, mesh_path, lods, instance_count}` |
| `foliage_scatter_on_mask` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (PLUMB-mask integration) | Scatter only where a mask passes — texture/vertex-color/PLUMB mask sampled at each candidate; the precision-placement verb. — `(foliage_type_path, region, mask_ref, threshold, density, seed) -> {painted, rejected_by_mask}` |
| `foliage_scatter_by_slope_altitude` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Filter placement by terrain slope-degree range and altitude range (e.g. trees on gentle midslopes, no cliffs); ecology realism in one verb. — `(foliage_type_path, region, slope_min/max, alt_min/max, density, seed) -> {painted, rejected_slope, rejected_alt}` |
| `foliage_replace_mesh` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox mass_set_property analog; net-new for foliage | Swap the StaticMesh on a foliage type, or migrate all instances from type A to type B preserving transforms; bulk re-theming. — `(foliage_type_path, new_mesh_path \| to_type_path) -> {migrated, instance_count}` |
| `foliage_select_in_bounds` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox search_by_property / Unity selection; net-new for foliage | Query/select instances of given types within a region into a working set for batch ops (selection management). — `(types[], region) -> {selection_id, count, per_type{}}` |

| `proc_foliage_volume_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (no competitor wraps proc-foliage) | Spawn an AProceduralFoliageVolume + ProceduralFoliageSpawner asset — the real UE ecosystem scatter engine. — `(box, spawner_dest_path, name) -> {volume_actor, spawner_path}` |
| `proc_foliage_spawner_add_type` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Add foliage types with ecosystem params (initial seed density, max age, collision radius, shade tolerance) to a procedural spawner. — `(spawner_path, entries[{foliage_type, density, max_age, radius}]) -> {types}` |

| `ism_actor_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (covers GenOrca gap) | Spawn an actor with a HISM/ISM component for a mesh — non-foliage instanced scatter (props, rocks, debris). — `(mesh_path, label, hierarchical=true) -> {actor_path, component}` |

| `foliage_seed_set` | P2 | TS | net-new moat | Register/lock the RNG seed for a region+type so dry_run output equals the committed scatter (reproducibility primitive). — `(scope, seed) -> {ok, seed}` |
| `foliage_to_pcg_handoff` | P3 | TS + python_run-backed (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (PCG/PLUMB depth) | Emit a scatter spec as a PCG SurfaceSampler/StaticMeshSpawner graph stub — bridge to the PCG worldbuilding domain. — `(scatter_spec, dest_graph) -> {pcg_graph_path, nodes[]}` |
| `foliage_lod_report` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (perf introspection) | Estimate per-type rendering cost: triangle count x instances x LOD distribution, to flag perf risks (introspection). — `(types?) -> {report:[{type,tris,instances,est_drawcalls}]}` |
| `foliage_wind_setup` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Configure wind/pivot-painter material params on a foliage type's material instance for animated foliage. — `(foliage_type_path, wind_strength, gust, pivot_tex?) -> {ok}` |
| `foliage_export_manifest` | P3 | TS (DAG/journal) | net-new moat (reproducibility) | Export a reproducible scatter manifest (types, seeds, regions, params) to re-bake identical foliage on another level/machine. — `(types?) -> {manifest_json}; import: (manifest_json)->{rebaked}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor + pyTemplate factory (descriptors are the authoring surface; no per-tool 7-file churn); Hardened python_run handler (SEH + security gate + chunked execution to avoid game-thread freeze); Theme 0 job-envelope conversion for long scatters/proc-foliage resimulate (>2s) so build/test-style freezes don't recur; 4C transaction/undo C++ wrappers (FScopedTransaction) so foliage mutations are one-Ctrl+Z and dry_run->commit generalizes; 4D selection/batch primitives (EditorActorSubsystem) for instance/actor batch ops; asset-retriever (hayba_asset_search) to discover candidate foliage StaticMeshes/FoliageTypes by tag; A real Landscape or collision geometry to ground-trace against (memory: SurfaceSampler/paint silently no-op without it); PLUMB constraint engine (plumb_constraint_define/validate) for density/spacing rules; Vision loop (render_camera / editor_capture_viewport) for heatmap + before/after diff; DAG/journal for recording scatter ops into reproducible manifests

**Risks:** The shipped C++ foliage_add_instance/foliage_paint_at return success but persist nothing — must be fixed or flagged not-callable before any new tool ships (honesty invariant); Adding thousands of instances on the game thread freezes the editor; all scatter tools MUST chunk transforms and route through the job-envelope, with instance-count caps + paginated commits; FoliageEditorLibrary.add_instances operates on the editor world and needs collision/landscape to ground-trace; on an empty level scatter silently yields 0 (must return an actionable error, not success); Procedural foliage resimulate() is heavy and synchronous — must be async/job-enveloped or it reproduces the build_* 300s freeze; Dry-run==commit requires deterministic RNG; without a seed registry preview won't match the baked result; Non-deterministic FMath::RandRange in the legacy C++ paint means previews can't be reproduced — seed must be threaded through python; UE Python foliage bindings vary across 5.7/5.8; coarse capability probe (get_*_status) needed for graceful degradation across pinned EngineVersion; Converting many StaticMeshActors to foliage (and back) is destructive and can orphan references — must be transaction-wrapped + dry-run first; Heatmap/orbit capture must isolate the target meshes and pre-warm shaders (memory: never sync-capture the live world)

**Live-editor test plan:** Live editor with a Landscape (or collision-enabled ground) plus 3-4 imported SM_ foliage meshes. 1) hayba_check_ue_status + foliage_capability_probe to confirm Python foliage bindings. 2) foliage_type_create from each mesh; foliage_type_set_params (density, scale 0.8-1.4, align_to_normal, cull 0/8000); foliage_type_inspect to read back params. 3) foliage_scatter_dry_run over a 50m box at density 0.2 -> confirm returned count + sample transforms + heatmap PNG; then foliage_scatter_paint with the SAME seed -> assert committed count == dry-run count (determinism). 4) foliage_inspect / foliage_list_types -> confirm per-type counts match; foliage_stats_by_region grid buckets non-empty over the landscape, zero outside. 5) Ctrl+Z (or transaction undo) -> foliage_inspect shows counts revert to 0 (proves real persistence + transaction wrapping, i.e. the stub bug is fixed). 6) foliage_scatter_by_slope_altitude (slope 0-20deg) -> verify no instances on cliffs via foliage_density_heatmap. 7) foliage_remove_in_bounds on a sub-box -> count drops by the bounded amount only. 8) foliage_relax min-distance 150 -> remove_overlapping_count>0 and nearest-neighbor histogram tightens. 9) Sculpt the landscape, run foliage_align_to_terrain -> re-traced instances sit on new surface (before/after capture). 10) proc_foliage_volume_create + add_type + resimulate -> instances baked, job returns without freezing (watch editor_stream_log). 11) ism_actor_create + ism_scatter -> HISM instance count via foliage/ism inspect. 12) foliage_validate_density with a PLUMB constraint (min spacing 100, max 0.3/m^2) -> green pass, then over-scatter -> red unsat-core surfaced. 13) foliage_export_manifest then re-import on a fresh level -> identical scatter (reproducibility).


<a id="domain-7"></a>
## 7. blueprint-authoring

*30 tools — P0:8 P1:11 P2:8 P3:3*

Blueprint K2 graph authoring is the single largest false-advertised capability: blueprint_add_node, blueprint_connect_nodes, and blueprint_add_event all return not_implemented_in_v1, while create/add_variable/add_function/compile/set_defaults already work in HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler.cpp. Strategy: (1) honestly implement the three stubbed K2 verbs in C++ on the EdGraph/EdGraphSchema_K2 plumbing the handler already imports; (2) ship the unglamorous read/verify surface (graph_inspect, node_brief, get_pin, find_nodes, reflection search for function/class/pin-type refs) so an agent can author then verify, closing the postmortem's "no UE reflection-introspection tool" gap; (3) productize the moats with a declarative blueprint_build_graph batch landing as ONE transaction/undo plus dry-run and PLUMB validate before/after, beating Epic's per-call undo and matching Flop's bp_dry_run. Reflection/search/diff helpers are python_run-backed (no rebuild); node mutation must be C++ because UE Python cannot reliably spawn/wire K2 nodes. Every write verb returns a compile report and respects the existing broken-blueprint compile-gate so the agent self-corrects.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blueprint_add_node` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (replace AddNode stub; UK2Node_CallFunction/VariableGet/VariableSet/IfThenElse/CustomEvent via FEdGraphSchemaAction_K2NewNode) | chongdashu/Aura/Flop have it; Hayba currently FALSE-ADVERTISES it | Spawn a typed K2 node (call_function/variable_get/variable_set/branch/sequence/cast/make_struct/break_struct/literal/custom_event/timeline) into a named graph and return node_id+pins - the core authoring verb, today a not_implemented stub. — `(path, graph, node_kind:Literal, ref?{class,member}, node_pos?[x,y], properties?, compile?) -> {node_id, pins:[{name,dir,type,linked}], compiled_clean, compile_errors}` |
| `blueprint_connect_nodes` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (replace ConnectNodes stub; UEdGraphSchema_K2::TryCreateConnection + CanCreateConnection) | chongdashu/Aura/Flop; Hayba FALSE-ADVERTISES it | Wire one node's output pin to another's input pin, validated through the K2 schema so type/exec mismatches are rejected with a reason - replaces the not_implemented stub. — `(path, graph, from_node_id, from_pin, to_node_id, to_pin, compile?) -> {connected:bool, reason?, compiled_clean, compile_errors}` |
| `blueprint_add_event` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (replace AddEvent stub; UK2Node_Event / UK2Node_CustomEvent / FKismetEditorUtilities::CreateNewBoundEventForClass) | chongdashu/Flop; Hayba FALSE-ADVERTISES it | Add an event node (BeginPlay/Tick/lifecycle override, input action, interface event, or named custom event) to the ubergraph and return its node_id+exec pin - replaces the not_implemented stub. — `(path, event_kind:Literal, name?, params?) -> {node_id, exec_pin, output_pins, compiled_clean}` |
| `blueprint_set_pin_default` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (UEdGraphSchema_K2::TrySetDefaultValue; reuse SetDefaults coercion for vector/color arrays) | chongdashu/Aura | Set a literal default value on an unconnected input pin (numbers, strings, enums, vectors, object refs) so simple nodes need no upstream wiring - the most common author micro-op. — `(path, graph, node_id, pin, value) -> {set:bool, coerced_value, reason?}` |
| `blueprint_graph_inspect` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (new GraphInspect; walk UEdGraph->Nodes/Pins) | Flop inspection-first; net-new at this fidelity for UE MCP | Read-back the full topology of a graph (nodes with title+kind+id, pins, links) paginated - the inspection pair every author loop needs to verify blind edits. — `(path, graph, limit=30, offset=0) -> {nodes:[{node_id,title,kind,pins}], links:[{from,to}], has_more, next_offset}` |

| `blueprint_function_add_param` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (new FunctionAddParam; UK2Node_FunctionEntry/Result + FBlueprintEditorUtils) | GenOrca/Aura | Add an input or output parameter to a function graph (typed via the pin-type catalog) so authored functions have a real signature, not just an empty body. — `(path, function, param_name, param_type, direction:Literal, default?) -> {added:bool, entry_node_id, compiled_clean}` |

| `blueprint_tx_commit` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler + transaction seam (GEditor->EndTransaction / CancelTransaction) | net-new moat | Commit (or with rollback=true cancel) the open authoring transaction, finalizing the single undo step and running one compile. — `(tx_id, rollback=false, compile?) -> {committed:bool, compiled_clean, compile_errors}` |
| `blueprint_pseudocode` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler (existing Document, generalized to all graphs) | net-new (token-economy review aid) | Render a graph as readable WHEN/THEN pseudocode (extends the existing blueprint_document) for cheap agent comprehension + token-light review without a screenshot. — `(path, graph?) -> {pseudocode:string, event_count}` |

**Dependencies:** Existing C++ HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler.cpp (Create/AddComponent/AddVariable/AddFunction/Compile/SetDefaults working; RecompileAndTrack + BrokenBlueprintsRef compile-gate; AttachCompileReport); Theme 0 gate: SEH-wrap ProcessCommand so the new C++ K2 handlers are crash-recoverable (graph mutation is crash-prone); Theme 1 gate: add-a-tool refactor (single ToolDescriptor + pyTemplate factory) so the ~14 python-backed wrappers ship without 7-file churn; Transaction subsystem (Theme 4C) FScopedTransaction seam for build_graph one-undo bundling and tx_begin/commit/rollback; PLUMB validator (plumb_validate / validator_run) for blueprint_validate before/after-mutate; python_run hardened handler (SEH + security gate) for reflection/search/diff pyTemplate tools; tool-executor.ts executeCommand passthrough + schema-registry.ts + legacy sidecar.json regen for the new C++ commands; Plan-Mode gate (bPlanApproved) honored before destructive verbs (delete_node/delete_variable/build_graph commit)

**Risks:** UE Python genuinely CANNOT spawn/wire arbitrary K2 nodes reliably; the core add_node/connect/event verbs MUST be C++ (a plugin rebuild) - do not pretend pyTemplate covers them or we repeat the not_implemented_in_v1 dishonesty; Stable node identity across calls: EdGraphNode pointers are not serializable IDs; must mint a stable node_id (NodeGuid string) and resolve back, or graph edits silently target the wrong node; K2 schema validation (CanCreateConnection) can reject a wire for type/exec-vs-data reasons; must surface the schema's rejection reason, not a generic failure; Compile after every node edit is slow on large BPs; build_graph must mutate-then-compile-ONCE, and per-node verbs should offer compile=false to defer; Function-ref resolution: a wrong UFunction/class name produces a silently orphaned call node; reflection search (blueprint_search_functions) must return the exact ref add_node consumes; Editor-only headers (EdGraphSchema_K2, BlueprintEditorUtils) keep the plugin non-shippable at runtime - already an accepted constraint but widens the editor-only surface; Re-entrancy: all K2 mutation must run on the game thread via the RunSync seam; AsyncTask+Wait inside a handler self-deadlocks (known MEMORY hazard)

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live 5.7 editor with the plugin loaded: (1) blueprint_create a child of Actor at /Game/MCPTest/BP_T; (2) blueprint_search_functions(target='Actor', query='SetActorLocation') to get the function ref; (3) blueprint_add_event(BeginPlay) -> capture node_id; (4) blueprint_add_node(call_function, ref=SetActorLocation) and blueprint_add_node(variable_get) -> capture ids; (5) blueprint_connect_nodes wiring BeginPlay exec->SetActorLocation exec and the vector pin; (6) blueprint_get_pin / blueprint_node_brief to VERIFY the link landed and types matched; (7) blueprint_graph_inspect (paginated) to confirm node/link counts; (8) blueprint_validate then blueprint_compile -> expect compiled_clean=true, error_count=0; (9) editor_capture_viewport / render the BP graph editor for a visual before/after diff; (10) blueprint_build_graph with the same spec in dry_run=true (expect a plan, no mutation), then commit, then a SINGLE editor Ctrl+Z and re-inspect to prove one-undo atomicity; (11) negative: connect two incompatible pins -> expect a schema rejection reason, and mutate a broken BP -> expect bp_compile_required gate. Cross-check every node_id round-trips via find_nodes.


<a id="domain-8"></a>
## 8. sequencer-and-cinematics

*36 tools — P0:12 P1:14 P2:7 P3:3*

There is already a HaybaMCPSequencer C++ plugin exposing 8 seq_* commands (create, add_track, add_keyframe [float-only], get_info, play, add_camera_cut, set_playback_range, and a stubbed seq_export that returns an error = a Section-3 honesty violation). None have first-class TS wrappers, so they are reachable only via hayba_invoke/python_run today. Strategy is three-layered: (1) Theme-2 thin TS wrappers over the 8 existing C++ commands plus paired inspect/list read tools; (2) Theme-3 python_run-backed authoring the C++ layer cannot do without a rebuild (transform/rotation/scale keyframing, spawnables, audio/fade/event/sub-sequence tracks, cine-camera outcome verbs, and the headline moat: a MovieRenderQueue render path returned as an async job-envelope so we are the only local UE MCP that renders finished movies and feeds stills/contact-sheets back as image content blocks); (3) cross-cutting safety: a PLUMB-style seq_validate, dry-run + one-Ctrl+Z transaction bundling, and full read-back on every write. Render MUST be a job-envelope, never a blocking game-thread call, to respect the Theme-0 freeze fix.</parameter>
<parameter name="dependencies">["Existing HaybaMCPSequencer C++ plugin (domain seq); seq_export stub must be removed from catalog per honesty invariant and superseded by seq_render_movie","Theme-0 job-envelope conversion (docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md) for render tools","Hardened python_run handler (SEH + security gate) as the dispatch path for non-C++ tools","MovieRenderQueue/MoviePipeline plugin enabled in project; gate with status probe and degrade gracefully if absent","Vision-loop infra (render_camera/editor_capture_viewport + image content-block return) for seq_render_still/thumbnail_strip","PLUMB validator seam for seq_validate; Plan-Mode gate + FScopedTransaction seam (4C) for destructive ops and seq_transaction","unreal Python: LevelSequenceEditorBlueprintLibrary, MovieSceneScripting channels, SequencerTools, EditorAssetLibrary, EditorActorSubsystem","Theme-1 add-a-tool refactor: ship python wrappers as pyTemplate descriptors not 7-file hand-rolls"]

| `seq_open` | P0 | python_run-backed TS wrapper over C++ seq_play | common; wraps C++ seq_play | Open the sequence in the Sequencer editor for human review or before render. Wraps existing seq_play. — `(asset_path) -> {asset_path, editor_opened}` |
| `seq_render_movie` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (MoviePipelineQueueEngineSubsystem + PIEExecutor, async) | net-new moat (no local UE MCP renders) | Render the sequence to video/image-seq via MovieRenderQueue as an async non-blocking JOB; headline moat replacing the dishonest seq_export stub. — `(asset_path, output_dir, format=mp4\|png_seq\|exr, resolution?=[1920,1080], frame_rate?, preset_path?, start_frame?, end_frame?) -> {job_id, status:queued}` |
| `seq_render_status` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (sidecar job registry) | steal-Blender async generate->poll | Poll a render job (job-envelope status probe): progress, output files, errors. Keeps rendering off the game thread. — `(job_id) -> {status:queued\|running\|done\|error, progress, output_files[], error?}` |
| `seq_render_still` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (MRQ single-frame or render_camera + scrub) | net-new moat (vision-loop 4B) | Render one frame at a time and return it as a MIME-typed PNG content block; the vision feedback loop for cinematics. — `(asset_path, time_seconds, resolution?=[1280,720], camera?) -> {image: png_block, frame}` |
| `seq_validate` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (+ PLUMB validator seam) | net-new moat (Flop auto-repair, none validate seq) | PLUMB-style check: dangling bindings, empty tracks, out-of-range keys, missing camera cut, framerate mismatch, zero-length shots. — `(asset_path, autofix?=false) -> {ok, issues[]{code,severity,detail,binding_guid?}, fixed[]}` |
| `seq_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (asset registry) | common; net first-class | List/paginate all LevelSequence assets with track/binding counts; discovery before any edit. — `(path_filter?, limit?=30, offset?=0) -> {sequences[]{asset_path,duration,track_count}, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `seq_list_bindings` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca; net first-class | Paginated read of possessable/spawnable bindings and resolved object paths; needed to find GUIDs for keyframing. — `(asset_path, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {bindings[]{guid,name,kind,object_path}, has_more}` |
| `seq_list_tracks` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca; net first-class | Paginated read of tracks on a binding/master with section ranges and key counts; the map for editing existing tracks. — `(asset_path, binding_guid?, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {tracks[]{track_id,type,sections[],key_count}, has_more}` |
| `seq_list_keys` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net first-class | Read keys on a channel (time,value,interp) for verify/diff and before batch edits. — `(asset_path, track_id, channel?, limit?=100, offset?=0) -> {keys[]{frame,time_seconds,value,interp}, has_more}` |
| `seq_keyframe_batch` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox mass_set_property analog; net for UE seq | Add many keys across channels in one transactional call; cuts round-trips when authoring curves. — `(asset_path, keys[]{binding_guid,channel,time_seconds,value,interp?}, dry_run?=false) -> {keys_added, per_channel_counts}` |
| `seq_create_cine_camera` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (outcome-verb) | Outcome verb: spawn a CineCameraActor, possess it, add a camera-cut over a range, optionally point at a target; one call for a working shot. — `(asset_path, location, rotation?, look_at_actor?, start_seconds?, end_seconds?, focal_length?=35) -> {camera_actor, binding_guid, cut_section}` |
| `seq_camera_focus` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Set/keyframe CineCamera focus distance, aperture and focal length over time (rack-focus, dolly-zoom); depth-of-field makes shots cinematic. — `(asset_path, camera_binding_guid, time_seconds, focus_distance?, aperture?, focal_length?, interp?=auto) -> {keys_added}` |
| `seq_add_audio` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca; net first-class | Add an audio master-track section with a Sound asset at a start time; gives cinematics music/SFX/dialogue. — `(asset_path, sound_asset, start_seconds, dry_run?=false) -> {track_id, section_id, duration}` |
| `seq_add_spawnable` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca; net-new (C++ does only possessables) | Add a spawnable binding from a class/asset (actor exists only while playing); for self-contained cinematics not depending on level actors. — `(asset_path, class_or_asset_path, dry_run?=false) -> {binding_guid, spawnable:true}` |
| `seq_set_frame_rate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | common; net first-class | Set sequence display frame rate (24/25/30/60/custom); must be set before rendering to film/broadcast spec. — `(asset_path, frame_rate) -> {display_rate, tick_resolution}` |
| `seq_remove_track` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | common; net first-class | Remove a track by id (destructive, Plan-Mode gated, transaction-wrapped); cleanup half of track lifecycle. — `(asset_path, track_id, dry_run?=false) -> {removed:true}` |
| `seq_remove_binding` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | common; net first-class | Remove a binding and its tracks (destructive, Plan-Mode gated); fixes orphaned bindings. — `(asset_path, binding_guid, dry_run?=false) -> {removed:true, tracks_removed}` |
| `seq_render_thumbnail_strip` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (steal-Blender thumbnail + vision) | Render N evenly-spaced frames into one contact-sheet PNG content block; fast visual overview without a full render. — `(asset_path, count?=6, resolution?=[320,180]) -> {image: png_block, times[]}` |
| `seq_transaction` | P1 | HaybaMCPSequencer (C++ FScopedTransaction seam; python cannot capture C++ mutations) | net-new moat (4C vs Epic per-call undo) | Bundle a series of edits into ONE undo (begin/commit/cancel); the named differentiator vs Epic per-call undo. — `(action:begin\|commit\|cancel, label?) -> {transaction_id, state}` |
| `seq_set_key_interpolation` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net first-class | Batch-set interpolation (auto/linear/constant/cubic/break) on keys/channels; smooth or hard-cut a curve in one call. — `(asset_path, track_id, interp, channel?, time_range?) -> {keys_updated}` |
| `seq_set_section_range` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net first-class | Move/trim a section start/end in seconds (retime a shot, audio, or clip) without re-authoring keys. — `(asset_path, section_id, start_seconds, end_seconds) -> {start_frame, end_frame}` |
| `seq_add_fade` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net first-class | Add a cinematic fade-in/out (fade track + keyed opacity over a range); the standard top-and-tail of a cinematic. — `(asset_path, start_seconds, end_seconds, direction:in\|out, color?=[0,0,0]) -> {track_id, section_id}` |
| `seq_duplicate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (EditorAssetLibrary.duplicate_asset) | common; net first-class | Duplicate a sequence asset to a new path (variant shots / safe experimentation before editing the master). — `(asset_path, dest_path, dest_name) -> {new_asset_path}` |
| `seq_delete` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | common; net first-class | Delete a sequence asset (destructive, Plan-Mode gated, reference-checked); honest lifecycle completion. — `(asset_path, force?=false, dry_run?=false) -> {deleted:true, referencers[]}` |
| `seq_spawn_actor` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net first-class | Create a LevelSequenceActor in the level bound to a sequence (auto-play/trigger at runtime); bridges authoring to gameplay. — `(asset_path, auto_play?=false, loop?=false) -> {actor_path}` |
| `seq_add_subsequence` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Add a sub-sequence/shot section nesting one sequence inside another at a time; the master-shot-track multi-shot workflow. — `(asset_path, child_asset_path, start_seconds, dry_run?=false) -> {track_id, section_id}` |
| `seq_import_camera_fbx` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (SequencerTools.import_fbx) | net-new moat | Import camera animation from FBX onto a bound camera; round-trip with external DCC / virtual-production takes. — `(asset_path, fbx_path, camera_binding_guid?) -> {keys_imported, binding_guid}` |
| `seq_export_fbx` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (SequencerTools.export_fbx) | net-new moat | Export a sequence's animation/cameras to FBX for another DCC; honest replacement for the dead seq_export stub intent. — `(asset_path, fbx_path, bindings?) -> {exported:true, file}` |
| `seq_render_preset_create` | P3 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Create/configure a reusable MoviePipeline render preset (codec, AA samples, output naming) for seq_render_movie; render settings as data. — `(name, resolution, samples?, format?, antialiasing?) -> {preset_path}` |

**Risks:** MovieRenderQueue blocks the game thread if synchronous (same class as the two P0 freeze bugs); seq_render_* MUST be async job-envelope or it re-introduces editor freeze; C++ seq_add_keyframe only handles float tracks; transform/rotation/scale/focus keyframing is python-only and may be flaky across 5.7/5.8, needing version-guarded codepaths; BindPossessableObject dereferences the editor world; binding tools crash with no level loaded unless the python wrapper replicates the existing C++ null-world guard; MRQ requires the MoviePipeline plugin and an output config; on a project without it render tools must fail honestly, not advertise a capability that errors; seq_get_info cannot resolve bound-object paths in 5.7 without SharedPlaybackState, so inspect/list_bindings may return empty object_path; document rather than mislead; Long renders can outlive the TCP command timeout; job-envelope + reconnect/port-rediscovery are prerequisites or status polls are lost; Spawnable bindings and sub-sequences have brittle GUID semantics; dry_run + seq_validate must run before/after to catch orphaned bindings; FBX import/export via SequencerTools historically silent-fails on partial bindings; needs read-back verification of imported key counts

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live 5.7 editor with HaybaMCPToolkit + HaybaMCPSequencer loaded and a level open: (1) seq_create under /Game/Cine then seq_inspect confirms zero tracks/bindings and requested frame rate. (2) seq_create_cine_camera pointed at a known actor; seq_list_bindings + seq_list_tracks confirm the camera binding, transform track, and camera-cut section. (3) seq_transform_keyframe at t=0 and t=2s with different locations; seq_list_keys verifies 2 keys per channel; scrub via seq_open and eyeball. (4) seq_set_playback_range 0-3s, seq_set_frame_rate 24; seq_validate returns ok. (5) seq_render_still at t=1s returns a PNG content block to visually confirm framing; seq_render_thumbnail_strip count=6 returns a contact sheet. (6) seq_render_movie returns a job_id immediately (assert editor stays responsive: open a menu, move viewport), poll seq_render_status until done, confirm output files exist and play. (7) Wrap add-track + 5 keys + camera-cut in seq_transaction begin/commit, then Ctrl+Z once and confirm ALL edits revert together. (8) Negative paths: render with MoviePipeline disabled returns an honest not-callable error; seq_delete on a referenced sequence lists referencers and refuses without force; binding tools with no level loaded error cleanly not crash. (9) seq_validate autofix=true on a deliberately orphaned binding reports it in fixed[].


<a id="domain-9"></a>
## 9. niagara-and-vfx

*32 tools — P0:5 P1:12 P2:11 P3:4*

The Niagara VFX domain has a hard, well-documented engine ceiling: UE Python cannot author emitter/module graphs (no scripting API for adding emitters/modules/dynamic-inputs), so we explicitly scope authoring to template-instantiation + exposed-user-parameter packs (brief-mandated) and lean the volume into the surface Python CAN reach — spawn/placement, runtime control, deep introspection, batch/selection, data-interface param injection, deterministic preview, and a net-new PLUMB-style validator/profiler. Today only 3 C++ commands exist (niagara_list/spawn/set_param in the HaybaMCPNiagara module); the brief's "6 target" is a floor, not a cap — competitors barely touch Niagara beyond a spawn verb, so depth here is cheap moat. Strategy: keep the 3 hot-path/crash-prone ops (spawn, set_param, place_actor + transaction checkpoint) in the C++ HaybaMCPNiagara module for game-thread safety and FScopedTransaction wrapping, and ship everything else as python_run-backed pyTemplate descriptors (zero plugin rebuild) per Theme 1 / Global rule "no rebuild by default." Every write tool gets a paired *_inspect read tool, dry_run on destructive ops, and routes verification through niagara_validate + the vision loop. Honesty invariant is load-bearing: authoring tools must self-flag the engine limitation rather than silently no-op like the blueprint_add_node stub.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `niagara_list` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPNiagara (existing NiagaraList) — add pagination | GenOrca (basic), Aura | List Niagara System assets (path/name) with prefix + pagination; the entry read-tool for discovery. EXISTS — extend with limit/has_more/next_offset. — `(path_prefix?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {systems:[{path,name}], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `niagara_system_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Dump a system asset's structure: emitter handles (name/enabled/sim-target CPU\|GPU), exposed user parameters (name/type/default), data interfaces, fixed bounds, warmup, loop behavior. Inspection-first paired read; highest-ROI introspection the domain lacks. — `(system_path) -> {emitters:[{name,enabled,sim_target}], user_params:[{name,type,default}], data_interfaces:[], fixed_bounds, warmup_time, effect_type}` |
| `niagara_param_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | List just the exposed USER parameters of a system asset with type+current default — the contract an agent needs before niagara_set_param. Focused, cheaper variant of system_inspect. — `(system_path) -> {user_params:[{name,type,default,is_data_interface}]}` |
| `niagara_spawn` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPNiagara (existing NiagaraSpawn) | GenOrca, Aura, Flop | Spawn a transient (auto-destroy) Niagara system at a world location/rotation/scale; the workhorse placement verb. EXISTS — add dry_run + FScopedTransaction + return component+actor paths. — `(system_path, location?, rotation?, scale?, auto_destroy=true, auto_activate=true, dry_run?) -> {component_path, actor_path, spawned_path}` |
| `niagara_spawn_attached` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Spawn a system attached to a target actor/component at an optional socket with location/rotation offset (muzzle flashes, exhaust, trails) — the attachment case bare niagara_spawn can't express. — `(system_path, target_actor, attach_socket?, location_offset?, rotation_offset?, auto_destroy=true, dry_run?) -> {component_path, attached_to}` |
| `niagara_place_actor` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPNiagara (new) — FScopedTransaction | net-new moat | Place a PERSISTENT ANiagaraActor in the level (survives save, undoable, selectable) vs the transient component spawn — for level-dressing VFX an author wants to keep and re-edit. — `(system_path, location, rotation?, scale?, label?, dry_run?) -> {actor_path, component_path}` |
| `niagara_spawn_batch` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (cf.
