Roblox mass_duplicate) | Scatter many instances of one system across an array of points (or sampled actor surface) in a single call — outcome-verb for crowds/foliage-VFX, avoids N round-trips. — `(system_path, points:[[x,y,z]], rotations?, persistent=false, dry_run?) -> {spawned:[component_path], count}` |
| `niagara_set_param` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPNiagara (existing NiagaraSetParam) | GenOrca, Aura | Set one user variable on a live component (float/int/bool/vec3/color/object). EXISTS — keep C++ for hot-path; broaden value_type coverage (vec2/vec4/quat/position). — `(component_path\|actor_path, param_name, value_type, value) -> {component_path, param_name, value_type}` |
| `niagara_set_params_batch` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Set MANY user params on one component in a single call (typed map), then one MarkRenderStateDirty — the ergonomic bulk variant that out-tools per-param competitors. — `(component_path\|actor_path, params:[{name,type,value}]) -> {component_path, set_count, failed:[]}` |
| `niagara_batch_set_param` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (cf. Roblox mass_set_property) | Set one param across EVERY live component instancing a given system (or matching a tag) — outcome-verb for 'turn all torches blue'. Pairs with niagara_select_in_level. — `(system_path\|actor_tag, param_name, value_type, value) -> {affected:[component_path], count}` |
| `niagara_component_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Read a live component's runtime state: active/paused, current user-param values, age, system asset, bounds, last-render-time — the read-back/verify tool for runtime control ops. — `(component_path\|actor_path) -> {active, paused, age, system_path, user_params:[{name,value}], bounds}` |
| `niagara_list_components` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Enumerate all Niagara components in the editor/PIE world (placed actors + transient spawns) with system path + active flag; paginated. The 'what's currently in the scene' read tool. — `(system_filter?, active_only?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {components:[{path,actor,system,active}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `niagara_activate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Activate (optionally reset) a component to (re)start the effect — needed because many template systems ship auto_activate=false. — `(component_path\|actor_path, reset=true) -> {component_path, active}` |
| `niagara_deactivate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Deactivate a component, optionally killing particles immediately (DeactivateImmediate) vs letting them die out — the clean stop/teardown verb. — `(component_path\|actor_path, immediate=false) -> {component_path, active}` |
| `niagara_set_paused` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Pause/resume a component's simulation in place — for inspecting a frozen frame before a screenshot or stepping a debug. — `(component_path\|actor_path, paused) -> {component_path, paused}` |
| `niagara_advance_simulation` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Deterministically tick a component's sim by N seconds at a fixed dt (AdvanceSimulation) so screenshots are reproducible — the deterministic-preview primitive the vision loop needs (a transient effect is invisible at t=0). — `(component_path\|actor_path, seconds, tick_dt=0.0167) -> {component_path, advanced_seconds}` |
| `niagara_capture_preview` | P1 | MIX — python_run spawn/advance + render_camera/editor_capture_viewport | net-new moat (cf. Blender thumbnail gen) | Spawn a system in isolation, advance its sim to a target time, auto-frame on its bounds, and return a screenshot as an image content block — the end-to-end VFX vision-verify loop (extends Moat #5). — `(system_path, at_time=1.0, orbit_angles?, resolution=512) -> {images:[png], bounds}` |
| `niagara_validate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) + PLUMB validator hooks | net-new moat (PLUMB) | PLUMB-style quantified pre/post-mutate validator: flags unbounded GPU emitters, missing fixed bounds, runaway spawn rates, missing effect-type/scalability, CPU-sim count over budget — closed-loop correctness no competitor offers for VFX. — `(system_path, budget?) -> {ok, violations:[{code,severity,detail,emitter}], score}` |
| `niagara_profile` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Report per-component perf: active particle count, sim target, GPU/CPU sim cost estimate, bounds for live components — read-back for perf tuning and the validator's budget input. — `(component_path?\|system_path?) -> {components:[{path,particle_count,sim_target,bounds,est_cost_ms}]}` |
| `niagara_create_from_template` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | The HONEST authoring path: duplicate a template system asset to a new content path and rename — since UE Python cannot build emitter graphs, template-instantiation is the supported creation verb (brief-mandated, flagged with engine-limit note). — `(template_path, dest_path, name) -> {new_system_path, source_template, note:'graph authoring is a UE Python limit'}` |
| `niagara_set_user_param_default` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Set the DEFAULT value of an exposed user parameter ON the asset (persists to the saved system), not just a live component — the param-pack vehicle for customizing instantiated templates. — `(system_path, param_name, value_type, value, save=false) -> {system_path, param_name, applied}` |
| `niagara_set_asset_property` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Set reflection-exposed system-level properties (warmup time/tick-count, fixed bounds, effect type, loop behavior) on the asset — best-effort over UPROPERTYs that ARE scriptable; self-flags non-settable graph internals. — `(system_path, properties:{warmup_time?,fixed_bounds?,effect_type?}, save=false) -> {system_path, set:[], skipped:[{name,reason}]}` |
| `niagara_set_emitter_enabled` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Toggle an emitter handle's enabled state on a system asset (one of the few graph-adjacent edits exposed) — best-effort, returns engine-limit note if the editor API is unavailable in the build. — `(system_path, emitter_name, enabled, save=false) -> {system_path, emitter_name, enabled, supported}` |
| `niagara_set_data_interface_param` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Inject array/object DATA INTERFACE user params on a live component (positions/vector arrays, static-mesh DI, texture/skeletal DI) — advanced control driving data-driven VFX that scalar set_param can't reach. — `(component_path, param_name, di_type, payload) -> {component_path, param_name, di_type}` |
| `niagara_set_mesh_renderer_mesh` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Swap the static mesh used by a system's mesh renderer via its exposed user param (the common 'debris uses this mesh' pattern) — convenience outcome-verb over set_data_interface_param. — `(component_path\|system_path, param_name, mesh_path) -> {target, mesh_path}` |
| `niagara_assign_system` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Swap the NiagaraSystem asset bound to an existing component/actor (retarget an existing emitter to a different effect) without respawning. — `(component_path\|actor_path, system_path, reactivate=true) -> {component_path, system_path}` |
| `niagara_duplicate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | GenOrca (generic asset dup) | Duplicate a system asset to a new path (variant authoring without touching the template) — paired sibling of create_from_template for fork-and-tweak. — `(system_path, dest_path, name) -> {new_system_path}` |
| `niagara_delete` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | GenOrca (generic), Flop | Delete a system asset with referencer check + dry_run preview; gated by Plan-Mode. The unglamorous destructive op done safely. — `(system_path, dry_run=true) -> {would_delete, referencers:[], deleted}` |
| `niagara_save` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | GenOrca, Flop | Save dirty Niagara system assets (single path or all) — the persist step after asset-level param/default edits; read-back lists what was saved. — `(system_path?, save_all=false) -> {saved:[path], count}` |
| `niagara_select_in_level` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (cf. Roblox search_by_property) | Select all Niagara actors/components in the level matching a system path or tag — feeds batch ops and gives the user a visible selection (Unity/Roblox selection-management pattern). — `(system_path?\|tag?) -> {selected:[actor_path], count}` |
| `niagara_effect_type_set_quality` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Get/set the global Niagara scalability quality level and a system's NiagaraEffectType binding — the scalability lever letting the validator enforce platform budgets. — `(action:'get'\|'set', quality_level?, system_path?, effect_type_path?) -> {current_quality, effect_type}` |
| `niagara_transaction_checkpoint` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPNiagara — FHaybaGameThread::RunSync + FScopedTransaction (4C seam) | net-new moat (vs Epic per-call undo) | Bundle a sequence of Niagara mutations into ONE undo checkpoint (begin/end + restore) — surfaces the cross-cutting 4C transaction seam for VFX so multi-step spawn+param scripts collapse to a single Ctrl+Z (differentiator vs Epic per-call undo). — `(action:'begin'\|'end'\|'restore', label?) -> {transaction_id, status}` |

**Dependencies:** HaybaMCPNiagara C++ module (existing 3 commands: niagara_list/spawn/set_param) — extend for place_actor + transaction_checkpoint; Niagara/NiagaraCore/NiagaraEditor engine modules already linked in HaybaMCPNiagara.Build.cs; Hardened python_run handler (SEH + 3-tier gate + dangling-delegate gate) — the vehicle for all pyTemplate-backed tools; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor: pyTemplate factory in ToolDescriptor (does not yet exist — gates these python-backed tools shipping as first-class); FHaybaGameThread::RunSync seam + FScopedTransaction (Moat 4C transactions) for place_actor/checkpoint; Vision loop: render_camera + editor_capture_viewport + wait_for_shaders for niagara_capture_preview; PLUMB validator hooks (plumb_validate / validator_run) for niagara_validate; FNiagaraUserRedirectionParameterStore / NiagaraComponent::SetVariable* APIs (set_param coverage); ProcessCommand SEH wrap (Theme 0) so C++ niagara handlers are recoverable

**Risks:** HARD ENGINE CEILING: UE Python cannot author Niagara emitter/module/dynamic-input graphs — authoring tools MUST be honest (template-instantiate + exposed-user-params only) or they become a second blueprint_add_node-style false advertisement; set_emitter_enabled/set_asset_property are best-effort and must self-flag unsupported edits; Transient (auto_destroy) spawned components are GC'd / die out — component_path can dangle; read-back tools must handle stale handles gracefully (cf. dangling-delegate crash class); GPU-sim particle counts and per-emitter cost are not cleanly readable from Python; niagara_profile estimates may be coarse vs an actual stat-niagara capture; Editor world vs PIE world: spawns target GetEditorOrGameWorld(); a transient effect is invisible at t=0, so capture_preview is meaningless without advance_simulation — easy to ship a black screenshot; Data-interface user-param API surface (array/mesh/texture DIs) is unstable across 5.7→5.8 and partly editor-only; set_data_interface_param needs version guards; NiagaraComponent SetVariable* APIs do not persist to the asset — set_user_param_default writes the asset's exposed-param store, a different code path that is easy to confuse and silently no-op; Niagara mutations from python_run won't be captured by C++ FScopedTransaction; transaction_checkpoint must route through the C++ seam, not Python's ScopedEditorTransaction (Moat 4C caveat); Spawning/placing actors must respect Plan-Mode gate + dry_run; batch spawn can flood the level and freeze the editor if point arrays are huge — cap + paginate

**Live-editor test plan:** Live editor with the HaybaMCPNiagara plugin loaded and a project containing template systems (Engine/Niagara content or Starter VFX). 1) Read path: niagara_list (verify pagination has_more), then niagara_system_inspect + niagara_param_list on a known system (fountain/fire) — confirm emitter names, sim targets, exposed user params match the Niagara editor. 2) Spawn path: niagara_spawn at origin → niagara_component_inspect confirms active+system_path; niagara_advance_simulation 1.0s then niagara_capture_preview → eyeball the returned PNG shows actual particles (not a black t=0 frame). 3) Control: niagara_set_param (color) + niagara_set_params_batch, re-inspect to read back applied values; niagara_set_paused true then screenshot a frozen frame; niagara_deactivate immediate=true → inspect shows inactive. 4) Placement+undo: niagara_place_actor (persistent) → confirm ANiagaraActor in World Outliner; wrap a spawn+param sequence in niagara_transaction_checkpoint begin/end → single Ctrl+Z removes ALL of it (the differentiator). 5) Authoring (honest-limit): niagara_create_from_template to a new path → niagara_set_user_param_default + save → reopen asset, confirm default persisted; confirm set_emitter_enabled returns a clear engine-limit note if unsupported. 6) Validation: niagara_validate on a deliberately unbounded-GPU / no-fixed-bounds system → expect violations with codes; fix via set_asset_property, re-validate → ok=true. 7) Batch: niagara_spawn_batch over 10 points → niagara_list_components count==10 → niagara_batch_set_param recolors all → re-inspect a sample. 8) Safety: niagara_delete dry_run=true lists referencers without deleting; confirm Plan-Mode blocks the real delete until approved. Cross-check no editor freeze (timeouts align to UE ceilings) and no AV in the journal (hayba_journal_tail) after transient components are GC'd.


<a id="domain-10"></a>
## 10. lighting-postprocess-and-rendering

*33 tools — P0:12 P1:14 P2:7 P3:0*

Almost entirely greenfield: today only render_camera (crash-prone, force-gated C++) and a 4-mode viewport switch exist, while Themes 3/4B name Lighting/Lumen (9), Nanite (3), MoviePipeline render, and vision buffer/orbit capture as quick high-value wins. Strategy: ship the lighting/PP/Lumen/Nanite authoring surface as python_run pyTemplate wrappers (zero rebuild, the Section-5 multiplier), reserving C++ only for genuinely hot/crash-prone paths: G-buffer visualization passes, extended show-flag view modes, and job-enveloped Movie Render Queue (which would otherwise freeze the game thread like build_*). Every write tool ships a paired *_inspect read, a dry_run preview, and outcome-verbs (lighting_preset_apply) collapsed into one Ctrl+Z to press the transaction moat vs Epic's per-call undo. The PostProcessSettings bOverride_* footgun and global rendering-cvar side-effects are wrapped with explicit override-flag handling plus readback/restore so agents can validate before/after mutate through PLUMB. Net result: the deepest dedicated lighting+render-output surface among UE MCP competitors, built mostly without plugin rebuilds.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `light_spawn` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate via runUePythonJson) | GenOrca has generic actor spawn; net-new as a typed lighting verb | Spawn a directional/point/spot/rect/sky light with intensity, color, temperature, mobility in one call — the primary way to lay down scene lighting. — `(light_type:Literal[directional,point,spot,rect,sky], name?, location?, rotation?, intensity?, color_rgb?, temperature_k?, mobility?, attenuation_radius?, source_angle?, cast_shadows?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path, applied}` |
| `light_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (no competitor ships a typed light editor) | Set properties (intensity, color, temperature, attenuation, source size, IES, shadow bias, cast-shadows, mobility) on an existing light by path — the core light-tuning verb. — `(actor_path, intensity?, color_rgb?, temperature_k?, use_temperature?, attenuation_radius?, source_radius?, source_angle?, ies_asset?, cast_shadows?, shadow_bias?, mobility?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path, changed, before, after}` |
| `light_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | Unity peer paginates hierarchy; net-new for lighting props | Read back all lights in the level (or one by path) with their key properties — the paired inspection-first read so agents tune from real values not guesses. — `(actor_path?, light_type?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {ok, lights:[{path,type,intensity,color,temperature,mobility,casts_shadows,attenuation}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `light_batch_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox peer mass_set_property; net-new for UE lighting | Batch-adjust a property across many lights selected by query/class/tag (dim all, re-temperature, toggle shadows) — outcome-verb batch op vs per-light churn. — `(query:{type?,tag?,name_regex?,selection?}, set:{intensity_scale?,intensity?,temperature_k?,color_rgb?,cast_shadows?,mobility?}, dry_run?) -> {ok, matched, changed, sample_diffs}` |
| `skylight_configure` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Configure/recapture a SkyLight (real-time vs captured, source cubemap, intensity, lower hemisphere color) and trigger recapture — ambient/IBL control. — `(actor_path?, source_type:Literal[captured_scene,specified_cubemap], cubemap?, intensity?, recapture?, lower_hemi_is_black?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path, recaptured}` |
| `postprocess_volume_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Create a PostProcessVolume (unbound or box-bounded) with priority/blend-weight — the container every PP/exposure/Lumen tweak targets. — `(name?, unbound=true, location?, extent?, priority?, blend_weight?, blend_radius?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path}` |
| `postprocess_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (handles the bOverride_ footgun no competitor exposes) | Set any PostProcessSettings group (bloom, exposure, GI, reflections, DOF, color-grading, AO, motion-blur, vignette, film-grain, chromatic-aberration) with correct bOverride_* flags on a volume or camera — the workhorse PP verb. — `(target_path, settings:{group->key->value}, clear_overrides?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, override_flags_set, before, after}` |
| `postprocess_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read back PostProcessSettings and which bOverride_* flags are active on a volume/camera — paired read so agents see what is actually overridden. — `(target_path, groups?:[bloom,exposure,gi,reflections,dof,color_grading,ao,motion_blur,misc]) -> {ok, settings, overridden_keys}` |
| `exposure_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Set auto-exposure mode (manual/histogram/basic), EV compensation, min/max EV, metering mask — the single most-asked PP knob, as a focused verb. — `(target_path, method:Literal[manual,auto_histogram,auto_basic], exposure_compensation?, min_ev100?, max_ev100?, metering_mode?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, before, after}` |
| `color_grading_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca touches material graph; net-new for grading | Set white-balance temp/tint and per-range (global/shadows/midtones/highlights) saturation/contrast/gamma/gain/offset — cinematic look control. — `(target_path, temp?, tint?, ranges:{global?,shadows?,midtones?,highlights?:{saturation?,contrast?,gamma?,gain?,offset?}}, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, before, after}` |
| `depth_of_field_set` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Configure cinematic DOF (focal distance, aperture f-stop, focal region) or auto-focus onto an actor's bounds — framing/realism control. — `(target_path, focal_distance?, f_stop?, focal_region?, focus_actor?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, focal_distance_used}` |
| `lumen_configure` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | Epic baseline lacks Lumen depth; net-new moat | Enable/disable Lumen GI and reflections and set quality (final-gather, lighting/reflection quality, scene-detail, max-trace-distance, hardware-RT) on a PPV, with RHI capability probe — the headline UE5 lighting verb. — `(target_path, gi_enabled?, reflections_enabled?, final_gather_quality?, scene_lighting_quality?, reflection_quality?, scene_detail?, max_trace_distance?, hardware_rt?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, rhi_supported, warnings}` |
| `reflection_method_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Set the reflection method (Lumen/SSR/None) and SSR quality on a PPV, and place/build Sphere or Box reflection captures — reflection authoring. — `(target_path?, method?:Literal[lumen,ssr,none], ssr_quality?, place_capture?:{shape,location,radius,brightness}, build_captures?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, captures_built}` |
| `fog_configure` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Configure ExponentialHeightFog (density, height falloff, color, start distance) and volumetric fog (scattering, extinction, albedo, view distance) — atmosphere/mood. — `(actor_path?, density?, height_falloff?, fog_color?, start_distance?, volumetric_enabled?, scattering?, extinction?, albedo?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path, applied}` |
| `sky_atmosphere_configure` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Configure a SkyAtmosphere actor (rayleigh/mie scattering, sun-disk, ground albedo) and link it to the directional sun light — physically-based sky. — `(actor_path?, rayleigh_scattering?, mie_scattering?, sun_disk?, ground_albedo?, link_sun_light?, dry_run?) -> {ok, actor_path, applied}` |
| `shadow_configure` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Set shadow method (Virtual Shadow Maps vs cascaded SM), cascade count/distance, contact shadows, and ray-traced shadow toggle on a directional light — shadow quality. — `(actor_path, method?:Literal[vsm,cascaded], num_cascades?, dynamic_shadow_distance?, contact_shadow_length?, raytraced?, dry_run?) -> {ok, applied, before, after}` |
| `nanite_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (no competitor ships Nanite control) | Enable/disable Nanite on a static-mesh asset and set fallback-triangle-percent / position precision — the easiest perf win in the brief. — `(asset_path, enabled, fallback_percent?, position_precision?, dry_run?) -> {ok, asset_path, rebuilt, tri_counts}` |
| `nanite_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read back Nanite enabled-state, triangle/cluster counts, and fallback settings for one or many meshes — paired read before toggling. — `(asset_path?, folder?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {ok, meshes:[{path,nanite_enabled,nanite_tris,fallback_tris,fallback_percent}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `nanite_batch_enable` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox peer mass ops; net-new for Nanite | Enable Nanite across a folder/selection of static meshes above a triangle threshold, paginated with per-asset status — bulk perf migration. — `(folder?, selection?, min_triangles?, fallback_percent?, dry_run?, limit=25, offset=0) -> {ok, processed:[{path,status}], skipped, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `render_cvar_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | ChiR24 graceful-degradation verbs; scoped/validated variant | Set a whitelisted rendering console variable (r.Lumen.*, r.Nanite.*, r.Shadow.*, sg.*) with prior-value readback and session restore — a scoped, safe escape hatch. — `(cvar, value, persist_for_session?, dry_run?) -> {ok, cvar, old_value, new_value, whitelisted}` |
| `scalability_set` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Apply an engine scalability preset (low..cinematic) or set individual sg.* group levels — quick quality/perf staging for renders. — `(preset?:Literal[low,medium,high,epic,cinematic], groups?:{view_distance?,shadows?,gi?,reflections?,post_process?,textures?,effects?}) -> {ok, applied}` |
| `viewport_set_view_mode` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit / EditorHandler (C++, extends SetViewportMode) | net-new moat (extends current editor_set_viewport_mode) | Extend the existing 4-mode switch to the full show-flag/view-mode set (lighting-only, reflections, lumen-overview, detail-lighting, collision, lightmap-density) for diagnostic captures — vision-loop introspection. — `(mode:Literal[lit,unlit,wireframe,detail_lighting,lighting_only,reflections,lumen_overview,collision,lightmap_density], show_flags?:{key->bool}) -> {ok, mode}` |
| `buffer_visualization_capture` | P0 | HaybaMCPToolkit / RenderHandler (C++; SceneCapture world-pos spike exists) | net-new moat (no UE MCP ships buffer-viz passes) | Capture a G-buffer visualization pass (base-color, world-normal, roughness, metallic, depth, overdraw, shader-complexity, lumen-scene) as a MIME-typed image — the strongest-in-class vision-feedback moat (4B). — `(pass:Literal[base_color,world_normal,roughness,metallic,specular,scene_depth,overdraw,shader_complexity,lumen_scene], width?, height?, camera?) -> {ok, path, image_block}` |
| `orbit_capture` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (set_level_viewport_camera_info + editor_capture_viewport loop) | Blender multi-angle; net-new for UE | Turntable capture: N viewport shots around an actor/selection bounds, returned as inline image blocks plus a contact sheet — multi-angle vision loop (4B) without crash-prone SceneCapture. — `(target:{actor?,selection?}, num_frames=8, elevation_deg?, distance?, width?, height?, contact_sheet?) -> {ok, images:[block], contact_sheet_path}` |
| `auto_frame_actor` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | Blender/Unity focus; net-new for UE MCP | Frame/focus the editor viewport camera on an actor or current selection's bounds then capture — auto-focus vision helper (4B) so captures are never empty. — `(target:{actor?,selection?,bounds?}, padding?, pitch_deg?, yaw_deg?, capture?) -> {ok, camera_location, camera_rotation, image_block?}` |
| `thumbnail_generate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AssetThumbnail render) | Blender asset thumbnails; net-new for UE MCP | Render per-asset thumbnails for a list/folder of assets and return them as image blocks — asset triage vision loop, steal-Blender pattern. — `(asset_paths?, folder?, size?, limit=20, offset=0) -> {ok, thumbnails:[{path,image_block}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `before_after_capture` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (wraps editor_capture_viewport) | net-new moat | Capture the viewport, await a referenced mutation (or tool bundle), capture again, and return a before/after side-by-side diff — closes the validate-before/after-mutate loop visually (4B). — `(camera?, label_a?, label_b?, mutation_token?) -> {ok, before_block, after_block, diff_block}` |
| `high_res_screenshot` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (HighResShot, job-enveloped) | remiphilippe visual tests; net-new authoring depth | Request a high-resolution editor screenshot (resolution multiplier, include-UI, HDR/EXR) via the job envelope and return the written path — marketing/inspection stills without freezing. — `(multiplier?, width?, height?, hdr?, include_ui?, output_path?) -> {ok, path, image_block?}` |
| `movie_render` | P0 | HaybaMCPSequencer (C++ MRQ executor) + python_run TS wrapper | remiphilippe headless render / Flop cloud render; local job-enveloped moat | Render a LevelSequence through Movie Render Queue (resolution/format/AA/output-dir preset) as a job-enveloped async job so the game thread never blocks — the production render verb. — `(sequence_path, output_dir, resolution?, format?:Literal[png,jpg,exr,mp4], frame_range?, preset?, samples?) -> {ok, job_id} ; poll -> {state, frames_done, output_files}` |
| `render_preset_define` | P2 | TS (descriptor-local store, no UE call) | net-new moat | Define/store a reusable render configuration (resolution, format, AA samples, MRQ settings) referenced by name from movie_render/high_res_screenshot — DRY render configs. — `(name, config:{resolution,format,samples,warmup_frames,settings}) -> {ok, preset_name}` |
| `lighting_preset_apply` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (ScopedEditorTransaction) — promote to C++ FScopedTransaction if mixing C++ handler mutations | net-new moat (outcome-verb + single-undo bundling) | Apply a named lighting look (golden_hour, overcast, studio_3point, moonlight) that spawns+configures lights, PPV, sky and fog as ONE outcome verb collapsed into a single Ctrl+Z — the transaction/undo differentiator vs Epic per-call undo (4C). — `(preset:Literal[golden_hour,overcast,studio_3point,moonlight,clear_noon], target_volume?, intensity_scale?, dry_run?) -> {ok, undo_label, actors_created, settings_applied}` |
| `light_build_static` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit / BuildHandler (C++, job envelope) + TS wrapper | remiphilippe headless build; net-new safety framing | Trigger a Lightmass static-lighting build at a quality level as a job-enveloped async job (avoids the build_* 300s game-thread freeze) — baked-lighting workflow, Theme-0 compliant. — `(quality:Literal[preview,medium,high,production], dry_run?) -> {ok, job_id} ; poll -> {state, progress, warnings}` |
| `lighting_validate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS feeding validator_run / plumb_validate | Flop auto-repair rules; PLUMB quantified-constraint moat | Run lighting/PP sanity checks (no over-exposure clipping, exactly one dominant directional, Lumen+RHI consistency, missing skylight, conflicting PPVs) through the PLUMB/validator loop — quantified validate-before/after-mutate. — `(scope?:Literal[level,selection], checks?:[exposure,dominant_light,lumen_rhi,skylight,ppv_conflict]) -> {ok, pass, findings:[{rule,severity,actor,detail,suggested_fix}]}` |

**Dependencies:** UE 5.7 Python editor-scripting (unreal module) via the hardened python_run handler (SEH + 3-tier security gate); runUePythonJson + PY_PREAMBLE HAYBA_JSON contract (src/tools/ue-python.ts) for all pyTemplate wrappers; EditorActorSubsystem / LevelEditorSubsystem / UnrealEditorSubsystem for spawn, selection, and viewport camera; existing editor_capture_viewport (now returns a real inline image block) + editor_set_camera/set_level_viewport_camera_info for orbit/auto-frame loops; Theme-0 job-envelope async conversion (docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md) is a hard prerequisite for movie_render and light_build_static so they do not freeze the game thread; MovieRenderPipeline + MovieRenderPipelineEditor plugins enabled for MRQ rendering; C++ HaybaMCPToolkit RenderHandler/EditorHandler rebuild for buffer_visualization_capture + viewport_set_view_mode (SceneCapture world-pos spike already exists per AI-mask Task 0 findings); FScopedTransaction wrapping in C++ handlers (4C) for true single-undo on preset apply; python ScopedEditorTransaction for PY-only mutations; PLUMB validator + green/red plan-mode overlay for validate-before/after-mutate; ToolDescriptor/register-tool.ts descriptor path + schema-registry + code-mode/deferred routing so the ~30 new tools land behind packs without bloating the eager surface

**Risks:** PostProcessSettings has ~100 paired bOverride_* booleans; setting a value without its override flag silently no-ops — wrappers MUST set bOverride_X=true and inspect must report override state; render_camera/SceneCapture is a documented editor-crasher; orbit_capture and thumbnail_generate must prefer viewport-capture (set camera + editor_capture_viewport) and pre-warm shaders, never sync SceneCapture the live world (per scenecapture-from-python memory); Movie Render Queue and Lightmass static build are long (>120s) game-thread blockers — they MUST go through the Theme-0 job envelope or they reintroduce the editor-freeze P0; global rendering cvars (r.Lumen.*, r.Nanite.*, sg.*) are process-wide side-effects; render_cvar_set needs a whitelist + readback + restore-on-session-end or it silently degrades unrelated work; Lumen/Nanite/Virtual Shadow Maps require correct RHI/feature-level (SM6, DX12); enabling on an unsupported project fails opaquely — lumen_configure must probe and report capability; viewport exposure vs game/PIE exposure differ (auto-exposure metering); inspect/readback values can mislead unless the capture context is reported; buffer_visualization_capture and viewport_set_view_mode extensions require a plugin rebuild (the 4-7 file / rebuild tax) — keep them minimal and behind the Theme-1 descriptor refactor; Nanite enable rebuilds the mesh (slow, asset-dirtying); nanite_batch_enable across a folder can stall the editor — must paginate + report per-asset status; PP color-grading and exposure overlap; without outcome-verbs agents will fight conflicting low-level sets — mitigate with lighting_preset_apply and a single PPV target convention

**Live-editor test plan:** Live editor on a scratch level with one ground plane + a couple of static meshes. (1) light_spawn directional + sky, then light_inspect and assert the returned intensity/temperature/mobility match what was set. (2) postprocess_volume_create unbound, postprocess_set bloom+vignette with dry_run=true (assert no scene change via postprocess_inspect), then dry_run=false and assert bOverride_ flags + values readback. (3) exposure_set manual EV, auto_frame_actor on a mesh + editor_capture_viewport, confirm the inline image brightens/darkens as EV changes. (4) lumen_configure enable GI+reflections, capture before/after via before_after_capture and eyeball indirect light; on an unsupported RHI assert a clean capability error not a crash. (5) orbit_capture N=8 around a mesh — assert 8 image blocks + a contact sheet returned, editor still responsive (no freeze). (6) buffer_visualization_capture world_normal + base_color — assert distinct passes returned as MIME-typed images. (7) nanite_set enable on one mesh, nanite_inspect assert enabled + fallback %, nanite_batch_enable on a 3-mesh folder assert paginated per-asset status. (8) movie_render on a 24-frame LevelSequence to a temp dir — assert job-envelope returns a job id, polls to done, files on disk, editor never blocked. (9) lighting_preset_apply golden_hour then a single Ctrl+Z (undo) — assert the whole preset (lights+PPV+sky) reverts in ONE step. (10) render_cvar_set r.Lumen.* from a whitelist with readback, then session-end restore — assert original value restored. Throughout, run validator_run / plumb_validate before and after mutations to confirm green/red plan-mode overlay fires.


<a id="domain-11"></a>
## 11. physics-and-chaos

*30 tools — P0:6 P1:13 P2:10 P3:1*

Today the surface is 3 raw C++ commands (physics_set_simulate / set_collision_profile / add_impulse) plus a scene_validate_physics read — zero first-class tools, no Chaos destruction, no PhysicsAsset, no constraints, no fields, no sim-bake. The strategy: (1) make the rigid-body author loop honest and outcome-shaped via python_run wrappers (set_body_properties, set_collision, forces, constraints, physical materials, collision-mesh gen) with no plugin rebuild; (2) lean the moat into Chaos destruction (Geometry Collection create/fracture/anchor/field) and PhysicsAsset/ragdoll authoring, which NO competitor (Epic baseline, Aura, Flop, GenOrca) ships — net-new moat, accepted as C++/MIX because UE Python coverage is near-zero there; (3) reinforce the safety moats with inspect-first read tools, a dry_run on every destructive fracture, FScopedTransaction wrapping, a post-sim stability verifier (NaN/explosion/interpenetration), and batch/query verbs. Chaos fracture and simulate-and-bake must ship through the job-envelope/async seam (Theme 0) so they never block the game thread like build_*/test_run do, and through the RunSync seam to avoid task-graph re-entrancy. Honesty invariant: anything Python cannot reach (fracture, anchors, fields, phys-asset body gen) is flagged C++-gated, not advertised as working before the rebuild lands.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `physics_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (Flop has inspect pattern, none for physics) | Read all physics state of an actor/component (simulate on?, mass, lin/ang damping, gravity, collision enabled+profile, CCD, COM, current lin/ang velocity, asleep?) — the inspect-first read that pairs every write verb. — `(actor_id, component_name?) -> {simulating, mass_kg, linear_damping, angular_damping, gravity_enabled, collision_enabled, collision_profile, ccd, com_offset, linear_velocity, angular_velocity, asleep}` |
| `physics_list_simulating` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Paginated list of actors/components with simulate-physics or collision enabled, with mass and profile — lets the agent find rigid bodies before mutating instead of guessing names. — `(filter_class?, tag?, collision_only?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {items:[{actor_id,component,simulating,mass_kg,profile}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `physics_set_simulate` | P0 | HaybaMCPPhysicsHandler (existing) + descriptor | GenOrca/chongdashu (partial) | Toggle simulate-physics on a primitive component (first-class wrapper over the existing C++ command; outcome-shaped with read-back). — `(actor_id, enabled, component_name?) -> {actor_id, simulating}` |
| `physics_set_body_properties` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | One outcome verb to set mass-override-kg, mass scale, linear/angular damping, gravity-enable, max angular velocity, start-awake — collapses the most common rigid-body tuning into one call. — `(actor_id, component_name?, mass_kg?, mass_scale?, linear_damping?, angular_damping?, gravity_enabled?, max_angular_velocity?) -> {applied_fields, readback}` |
| `physics_set_collision` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca/Flop (partial) | Set collision-enabled mode (NoCollision/QueryOnly/PhysicsOnly/QueryAndPhysics) + preset profile + per-channel object type & responses + CCD flag in one call; replaces the bare set_collision_profile. — `(actor_id, component_name?, collision_enabled?, profile?, object_type?, channel_responses?, ccd?) -> {profile, collision_enabled, ccd}` |
| `physics_batch_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox mass_set_property (steal) | Apply simulate/collision/body-property changes across a selection or query (by class/tag/name-glob) in one transaction — the mass-edit ergonomics verb; matches Roblox mass_set_property. — `(query{class?,tag?,name_glob?,selection?}, set{simulating?,profile?,mass_kg?,gravity_enabled?,...}, dry_run=false) -> {matched, changed, sample_ids}` |
| `physics_add_impulse` | P1 | HaybaMCPPhysicsHandler (existing) + descriptor | GenOrca | Apply a one-shot impulse (or velocity-change) at COM or a world point — first-class wrapper over existing C++ command. — `(actor_id, impulse[3], velocity_change?, at_location?, component_name?) -> {applied}` |
| `physics_add_force` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Apply a continuous force/torque, or a radial force/impulse with falloff radius+strength — covers explosions, wind, attractors that impulse alone can't. — `(actor_id, force?[3], torque?[3], radial?{origin[3],radius,strength,falloff,impulse}, component_name?) -> {applied}` |
| `physics_set_velocity` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Directly set linear and/or angular velocity of a body — needed for scripted throws and deterministic test setups that forces can't reproduce. — `(actor_id, linear?[3], angular?[3], add=false, component_name?) -> {linear_velocity, angular_velocity}` |
| `physics_set_mass_center` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Override the center-of-mass offset of a body — controls tipping/toppling realism for stacked or weighted props. — `(actor_id, com_offset[3], component_name?) -> {com_offset}` |
| `physics_wake_sleep` | P3 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Wake or force-sleep a body (and optionally a whole island) — unsticks settled actors after an edit and lets the agent control sim cost. — `(actor_id, action:'wake'\|'sleep', component_name?) -> {asleep}` |
| `physics_add_constraint` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (+ MIX for drives) | net-new moat | Spawn a PhysicsConstraintComponent linking two components with linear (free/limited/locked + limit) and angular swing1/swing2/twist limits, plus drive params — the building block for hinges, doors, ropes, ragdoll joints. — `(actor_id, comp_a, comp_b, linear{x,y,z,limit}?, swing1?, swing2?, twist?, drive?, location?[3]) -> {constraint_name}` |
| `physics_constraint_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read the limits/drives/connected components of a constraint — inspect pairing for add/remove. — `(actor_id, constraint_name) -> {comp_a, comp_b, linear, angular, drive}` |
| `physics_remove_constraint` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Delete a physics constraint component by name — cleanup verb for the constraint loop. — `(actor_id, constraint_name) -> {removed}` |
| `physical_material_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Create a PhysicalMaterial asset with friction, restitution, density, friction-combine mode (and surface type) — the asset behind realistic sliding/bouncing. — `(package_path, name, friction, restitution, density, surface_type?) -> {asset_path}` |
| `physical_material_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read friction/restitution/density/surface-type of a PhysicalMaterial asset — read pairing. — `(asset_path) -> {friction, restitution, density, surface_type}` |
| `physical_material_assign` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Assign a PhysicalMaterial to a static-mesh asset, a material, or a body setup, optionally across a selection — wires the asset into actual collision response. — `(phys_mat_path, target{mesh_path?\|material_path?\|actor_ids?}, dry_run=false) -> {assigned_count}` |
| `mesh_generate_collision` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (StaticMeshEditorSubsystem) | GenOrca (partial) | Generate simple collision on a static mesh: box/sphere/capsule/K-DOP primitives or convex-decomposition (hull count + accuracy) — fixes the #1 cause of objects falling through the world. — `(mesh_path, type:'box'\|'sphere'\|'capsule'\|'kdop'\|'convex', hull_count?, accuracy?, replace=true, dry_run=false) -> {primitive_count}` |
| `mesh_remove_collision` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Strip all simple collision from a static mesh — paired cleanup for collision iteration. — `(mesh_path) -> {removed_count}` |
| `geometry_collection_create` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (GeometryCollectionEngine/Editor) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Create a Chaos Geometry Collection asset+actor from one or more static meshes (the destructible source) — entry point to the destruction moat no competitor has. — `(source_mesh_actors[], package_path, name) -> {gc_asset_path, gc_actor_id, bone_count}` |
| `geometry_collection_fracture` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (FractureEditor) — C++-gated, async job-envelope, transaction-wrapped, Plan-Mode gated | net-new moat | Fracture a Geometry Collection: uniform/Voronoi/clustered/radial/planar with site count, seed, noise, grout, and cluster level; dry_run returns predicted piece count without mutating — core destruction authoring. — `(gc_asset_path, mode:'uniform'\|'voronoi'\|'clustered'\|'radial'\|'planar', sites, seed?, noise?, grout?, cluster_level?, dry_run=false) -> {pieces, levels, job_id}` |
| `geometry_collection_set_damage` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (GeometryCollectionEngine) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Set per-cluster-level damage thresholds, internal/external strain, collision particles, and mass scaling on a GC — controls how easily it shatters. — `(gc_asset_path, damage_thresholds[], collision_type?, mass_scale?) -> {levels, thresholds}` |
| `geometry_collection_add_anchor` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (FieldSystem/GeometryCollection) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Mark bones anchored (or attach a static-anchor field) so the structure stays fixed until impact frees it — the difference between a building and a pile of rubble. — `(gc_actor_id, anchor:'bottom'\|'bone_ids'\|'box', bone_ids?, box?) -> {anchored_count}` |
| `geometry_collection_brief` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (read) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Read a Geometry Collection's fracture levels, bone/cluster counts, damage thresholds, anchored bones, and collision settings — inspect pairing for the destruction loop. — `(gc_asset_path) -> {levels, bone_count, cluster_counts, thresholds, anchored, collision_type}` |
| `physics_field_spawn` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (FieldSystemEngine) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Spawn a FieldSystemActor with a radial/uniform vector / radial-falloff / strain field to push, attract, or break Chaos bodies — programmable forces and triggered destruction. — `(field_type:'radial_vector'\|'uniform_vector'\|'radial_falloff'\|'strain', origin[3], magnitude, radius?, target:'force'\|'break'\|'anchor') -> {field_actor_id}` |
| `physics_asset_create` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (PhysicsAssetUtils) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Generate a PhysicsAsset from a skeletal mesh with auto body generation (min bone size, primitive type, constraint creation) — the ragdoll foundation; Python can't reach the body-builder so this is C++. — `(skeletal_mesh_path, package_path, name, primitive:'capsule'\|'box'\|'sphere', min_bone_size?) -> {phys_asset_path, body_count, constraint_count}` |
| `physics_asset_brief` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read a PhysicsAsset's bodies (bone, shape, mass) and constraints (limits) — inspect pairing for ragdoll work; read-only so can ship as Python reflection ahead of the create tool. — `(phys_asset_path) -> {bodies:[{bone,shape,mass}], constraints:[{name,bone_a,bone_b,limits}]}` |
| `physics_simulate_and_bake` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (RunSync seam, async job-envelope, FScopedTransaction) — C++-gated | net-new moat | Run the editor physics scene for N seconds (or until all bodies sleep), then bake the resting transforms back onto the actors and disable sim — 'drop these props so they settle naturally', baked into ONE undo. — `(actor_ids[]\|selection, seconds?=5, settle_threshold?, dry_run=false) -> {baked_count, settled, final_transforms, job_id}` |
| `physics_verify_stability` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (extends scene_validate_physics) | Post-mutation verifier: scans simulating bodies for NaN/inf transforms, exploding velocities (> threshold), and interpenetration overlaps; returns machine codes per offender — the validate-after-mutate safety check for physics. — `(actor_ids?\|all, velocity_threshold?, check_overlap=true) -> {ok, offenders:[{actor_id,code,detail}]}` |
| `physics_undo` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (FScopedTransaction / 4C seam) | Unity undo/redo (steal); Epic only does per-call undo | Undo/redo/checkpoint wrapper scoped to the physics transaction journal so destructive ops (fracture, bake, collision-gen) revert as a unit — bridges to the cross-cutting 4C transaction seam. — `(action:'undo'\|'redo'\|'checkpoint'\|'restore', label?) -> {applied, label}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 0 gate: ProcessCommand SEH-wrap (so all physics handlers are crash-recoverable) + job-envelope/async conversion (fracture and simulate_and_bake are slow → must not block game thread like build_*/test_run); Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor: single ToolDescriptor with ueCommand/pyTemplate/handler so the ~22 python_run + ~8 C++ tools land without 7-file churn or 4-place schema drift; 4C transaction seam: FScopedTransaction wrapping in HaybaGameThread::RunSync — required for physics_undo, simulate_and_bake, fracture, batch_set to be one-undo; FHaybaGameThread::RunSync seam (PR #254): every C++ handler that steps the scene or touches GeometryCollection must marshal to game thread without AsyncTask(GameThread)+Wait self-deadlock; Build.cs module deps for C++ tools: GeometryCollectionEngine, GeometryCollectionEditor, FieldSystemEngine, Chaos, PhysicsCore, ChaosSolverEngine; honor EngineVersion pin (5.7.0) — these APIs churn across versions; python_run hardened handler (SEH + 3-tier gate + dangling-delegate gate) for all pyTemplate tools; StaticMeshEditorSubsystem for collision-gen; Existing HaybaMCPPhysicsHandler.cpp (extend GetCommands/Handle) for set_simulate/add_impulse first-class wrappers; Plan-Mode gate (bPlanApproved) before destructive ops: fracture, simulate_and_bake, batch_set, mesh_generate_collision(replace); wait_for_idle / editor sim tick access for simulate_and_bake settling; render_camera/editor_capture_viewport for before/after destruction visual verify

**Risks:** Chaos fracture, anchors, fields, GC create, and PhysicsAsset body-gen have near-zero UE Python coverage — they are editor-only C++ and REQUIRE a plugin rebuild; do not advertise as working until compiled, or we repeat the blueprint_add_node 'not_implemented_in_v1' honesty violation; Geometry Collection / Fracture / Field APIs change significantly between UE releases; pinned 5.7.0 build may break on 5.8 — flag as engine-version-sensitive; simulate_and_bake must step the physics scene in the editor world; editor world does not advance physics by default without PIE/simulate — may require a manual Chaos solver tick via RunSync, and a hard time cap to avoid the build_* 300s-freeze class of bug; Fracture on dense meshes is slow and memory-heavy → must run via async job-envelope + dry_run piece-count preview first, or it freezes the game thread; Stepping/baking physics mutates actor transforms irreversibly without transaction wrapping → batch_set and simulate_and_bake MUST be FScopedTransaction-wrapped and Plan-Mode gated; add_force/add_impulse/set_velocity only have visible effect while a body is actually simulating; in a non-simulating editor world they silently no-op — verify_stability and inspect read-backs must report 'not simulating' rather than 'applied:true' to avoid false success; Constraint creation via Python (PhysicsConstraintComponent) can register lifetime delegates — route through the dangling-delegate gate (#283/#284) to avoid the python_run AV crash class; ChaosCloth and ControlRig-driven physics are genuine UE Python limits — explicitly out of scope here; scope ragdoll to template/auto-gen + param packs and flag as engine limitation

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation, ordered cheap-to-expensive: (1) Spawn a few static-mesh actors (cube stack) via actor tools; run physics_list_simulating (expect empty), physics_inspect on one (expect simulating:false). (2) physics_set_simulate enabled=true, then physics_set_body_properties mass_kg=50 gravity_enabled=true; re-inspect to confirm read-back matches. (3) editor_start_pie or simulate, capture viewport before; apply physics_add_force radial explosion at stack origin; capture after — confirm pieces scattered visually, then physics_verify_stability (expect ok:true, no NaN/explosion). (4) mesh_generate_collision convex on a complex mesh; drop it with simulate and confirm it no longer falls through floor. (5) physical_material_create (low friction, high restitution) + assign to a sphere; simulate and confirm visible bounce. (6) physics_add_constraint hinge between a door and frame; simulate and confirm swing limited to the configured angle. (7) physics_batch_set across selection with dry_run=true (confirm matched count, no mutation), then dry_run=false, then physics_undo and confirm reverted in one Ctrl+Z. (8) C++-gated path (after rebuild): geometry_collection_create from a cube, geometry_collection_fracture dry_run=true (confirm predicted pieces), then real fracture, geometry_collection_brief to read levels, add_anchor bottom, spawn physics_field_spawn strain at impact point, simulate and capture viewport to confirm anchored-then-shattering behavior; finish with physics_simulate_and_bake on a pile of debris (seconds=4) and confirm baked resting transforms persist after stopping sim. Verify each handler survives a forced client disconnect mid-command (SEH/late-response crash regression) and that no command exceeds the executor timeout.


<a id="domain-12"></a>
## 12. animation-and-controlrig

*40 tools — P0:7 P1:17 P2:16 P3:0*

UE animation authoring is a deep, under-served surface: only GenOrca covers ControlRig + retarget at all, and Epic's baseline ships none of it. The repo already has a working C++ FHaybaMCPAnimationHandler (5 AnimBP state-machine commands) with zero first-class wrappers — Theme-2 debt we surface cheaply. Strategy: (1) lead with inspection-first read tools (Flop pattern) so the agent never edits blind; (2) wrap the existing C++ state-machine commands; (3) put the bulk of net-new authoring (notifies, curves, sync markers, sockets, virtual bones, blendspace/montage creation, IK Rig/Retargeter, ControlRig bake) on python_run via unreal.AnimationLibrary / IKRigController / IKRetargeterController / ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary — no plugin rebuild; (4) make the batch-retarget pipeline (source→target skeleton) and the bake-to-anim-sequence ops our headline moat tools, both routed through the job-envelope so they cannot freeze the editor. Every write tool is paired with an *_inspect readback and a viewport/preview-render verify, and ControlRig/BlendSpace are honestly scoped to template-instantiation + param packs (rig-graph and sample-graph authoring are genuine UE Python ceilings, flagged not net-new).

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `anim_blueprint_inspect` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (wrap anim_blueprint_get_info) | net-new moat (Epic baseline none; deeper than GenOrca AnimBP read) | Read an AnimBlueprint: target skeleton, parent class, all state machines (states/transitions), variables — so the agent edits with a real map, not guesses. — `(path) -> {name, target_skeleton, parent_class, state_machines[], variables[]}` |
| `anim_sequence_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.AnimationLibrary) | net-new moat | Read an AnimSequence: play length, frame rate, num frames, bone tracks, float curves, notifies, sync markers, additive settings — the prereq for every notify/curve/retarget edit. — `(path) -> {length_s, fps, num_frames, additive, curves[], notifies[], sync_markers[], bone_tracks_count}` |
| `skeleton_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.Skeleton / AnimationLibrary) | net-new moat | Dump a Skeleton's bone hierarchy, sockets, virtual bones, animation slots/groups, curve names, and per-bone retarget options — the introspection base for sockets/retarget/montage slot work. — `(skeleton_path, limit?, offset?) -> {bones[], sockets[], virtual_bones[], slots[], curves[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `anim_asset_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry) | GenOrca (asset listing); we add skeleton filter | Paginated listing of animation assets (AnimSequence/Montage/BlendSpace/AnimBlueprint/IKRig/IKRetargeter/ControlRig) filtered by class and skeleton — discovery without dumping the content browser. — `(class?:Literal, skeleton_path?, path_prefix?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {assets[], total, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `control_rig_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (ControlRigBlueprint.get_hierarchy_controller) | GenOrca (ControlRig); we add honest ceiling labeling | Read a ControlRig blueprint's rig hierarchy (bones/controls/nulls), control settings, and exposed variables — so bake/hierarchy edits are targeted; honestly flags rig-graph contents as opaque (engine limit). — `(path) -> {bones[], controls[], nulls[], variables[], graph_introspection:'limited'}` |
| `ik_rig_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.IKRigController) | GenOrca (retarget) | Read an IK Rig: preview mesh, retarget root, retarget chains (start/end/goal), and solver stack — required before chain edits or retargeter mapping. — `(path) -> {mesh, retarget_root, chains[], solvers[]}` |
| `ik_retargeter_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.IKRetargeterController) | GenOrca (retarget) | Read an IK Retargeter: source/target IK rigs and the chain-to-chain mapping table — verify mapping before a batch retarget run. — `(path) -> {source_ik_rig, target_ik_rig, chain_map[], root_settings}` |
| `anim_blueprint_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AnimBlueprintFactory + AssetTools) | net-new moat (Epic none) | Create an empty AnimBlueprint for a given skeleton/parent class — the entry point that anim_state_add etc. operate on. — `(package_path, name, skeleton_path, parent_class?) -> {path, ok}` |
| `anim_state_add` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (wrap anim_blueprint_add_state) | GenOrca (BT/anim graph edit); we ship it transactional | Add a named state to an AnimBP state machine (auto-creates its bound blend graph) — first-class wrapper for the working C++ command. — `(path, state_machine_name, new_state_name) -> {state_machine, state, node_name}` |
| `anim_transition_add` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (wrap anim_blueprint_add_transition) | GenOrca | Add a transition between two states (searchable across state machines) — first-class wrapper for the working C++ command. — `(path, from_state, to_state, state_machine_name?) -> {transition_id, from, to}` |
| `anim_transition_set_condition` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (wrap anim_blueprint_set_condition) | GenOrca (partial); we are honest about the ceiling | Set a transition rule. v1 supports literal true/false (wraps C++); honestly returns v2_pending for expression graphs — no false advertising. — `(path, condition_expression, transition_id?\|from_state+to_state) -> {transition_id, status:'applied'\|'v2_pending', hint?}` |
| `anim_blueprint_compile` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (wrap anim_blueprint_compile) | Flop (bp lifecycle compile) | Compile an AnimBP and return structured errors/warnings + counts — the verify step after any graph edit. Routed through RunSync seam to avoid game-thread freeze. — `(path) -> {ok, compiled, error_count, warning_count, errors[], warnings[]}` |
| `anim_blueprint_add_variable` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (new) | net-new moat | Add a typed variable (bool/float/int/enum/vector) to an AnimBP so transition conditions and blend params have something to bind to. — `(path, var_name, type:Literal, default?) -> {ok, var_name, type}` |
| `anim_state_set_animation` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/AnimationHandler (new; BoundGraph edit not Python-reachable) | net-new moat | Bind an AnimSequence/BlendSpace into a state's blend graph (drops a sequence/blendspace player and wires it to the state result) — closes the loop so states actually play something. — `(path, state_machine_name, state_name, asset_path, loop?) -> {ok, player_node}` |
| `anim_notify_add` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary.add_animation_notify_event/state) | GenOrca (anim notify) | Add a notify event or notify-state at a time on a sequence/montage (footsteps, VFX, combat windows) — high-value pure-python edit, no rebuild. — `(anim_path, notify_name, time_s, duration_s?, notify_class?, track?) -> {ok, notify_id}` |
| `anim_notify_remove` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary) | net-new moat | Remove notifies by name/track/time-range, or clear all — the batch cleanup counterpart so the agent can re-author idempotently. — `(anim_path, name?, track?, time_range?, all?) -> {removed_count}` |
| `anim_curve_add` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary float curve API) | net-new moat | Add/replace a named float curve with keyframes on a sequence (e.g. IK weight, speed warp, morph-target driver) — drives runtime blending. — `(anim_path, curve_name, keys:[{time,value}], interp?) -> {ok, key_count}` |
| `anim_sync_marker_add` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary sync marker API) | net-new moat | Add a sync marker at a time on a named sync track — enables foot-locked blendspace/montage syncing across locomotion anims. — `(anim_path, track_name, marker_name, time_s) -> {ok}` |
| `montage_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AnimMontageFactory / AnimationLibrary) | GenOrca (montage) | Create an AnimMontage from one or more sequences on a target slot/group — the standard gameplay playback asset; pure-python, no rebuild. — `(package_path, name, source_sequences[], slot_name?, blend_in?, blend_out?) -> {path, length_s}` |
| `montage_add_section` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimMontage section API; flags any C++-only gaps) | net-new moat | Add/define a named montage section at a time with optional next-section link (combo chains, loops) and inspect existing sections. — `(montage_path, section_name, start_time_s, next_section?) -> {ok, sections[]}` |
| `montage_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read a montage: slots, sections + links, total length, notifies — verify combo wiring before gameplay test. — `(montage_path) -> {slots[], sections[], length_s, notifies[]}` |
| `blendspace_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (BlendSpaceFactory1D/BlendSpaceFactory) | GenOrca (blendspace) | Create a 1D or 2D BlendSpace with axis names/ranges/grid for a skeleton (locomotion: speed/direction) — pure-python asset creation. — `(package_path, name, skeleton_path, dims:1\|2, axes:[{name,min,max,grid}]) -> {path}` |
| `blendspace_add_sample` | P2 | python_run-backed TS w/ C++ fallback (AnimationHandler) — flagged engine limitation | GenOrca; we flag the ceiling honestly | Place a sequence sample at a blendspace coordinate. Honestly scoped: sample-graph internals are partly C++-only in UE Python — flags fallback to C++ where the Python setter is unavailable. — `(blendspace_path, coord:[x,y?], sequence_path) -> {ok\|deferred, reason?}` |
| `skeleton_add_socket` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.Skeleton add socket) | GenOrca (socket); we add batch variant | Add a socket on a bone with relative transform (weapon/attach points) — batchable; the bread-and-butter rigging op. — `(skeleton_path, bone_name, socket_name, location?, rotation?, scale?) -> {ok, socket_name}` |
| `skeleton_add_sockets_batch` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox mass_* peer; net-new for UE anim | Add many sockets in one call from a list (one undo) — outcome-verb over the primitive, matching DCC batch best-practice. — `(skeleton_path, sockets:[{bone,name,loc,rot,scale}]) -> {added_count, failures[]}` |
| `skeleton_add_virtual_bone` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary.add_virtual_bone) | net-new moat | Add a virtual bone between source and target bones (retarget/IK helper) — pure-python, common rigging need. — `(skeleton_path, source_bone, target_bone, name?) -> {ok, virtual_bone_name}` |
| `skeleton_set_retarget_options` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AnimationLibrary retarget options) | net-new moat | Set per-bone translation-retargeting mode (Skeleton/Animation/AnimationScaled/Orientation) — fixes the classic root-motion/scale retarget bugs. — `(skeleton_path, rules:[{bone, mode:Literal}]) -> {updated_count}` |
| `ik_rig_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (unreal.IKRigController) | GenOrca (retarget setup) | Create an IK Rig asset for a skeletal mesh — the foundation for retargeting and runtime IK; pure-python via IKRigController. — `(package_path, name, mesh_path) -> {path}` |
| `ik_rig_add_chain` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (IKRigController.add_retarget_chain/set_retarget_root) | GenOrca | Add a retarget chain (start bone, end bone, optional goal) and/or set the retarget root on an IK Rig — defines what gets mapped. — `(ik_rig_path, chain_name, start_bone, end_bone, goal_name?, set_as_root?) -> {ok, chains[]}` |
| `ik_retargeter_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (IKRetargeterController) | GenOrca | Create an IK Retargeter binding a source IK Rig to a target IK Rig — the asset that drives cross-skeleton anim transfer. — `(package_path, name, source_ik_rig, target_ik_rig) -> {path}` |
| `ik_retargeter_auto_map` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (IKRetargeterController auto-map) | net-new moat | Auto-map source→target chains by name/heuristic on a retargeter, returning the resulting map for review — saves dozens of manual mappings. — `(retargeter_path, method?:Literal) -> {chain_map[], unmapped[]}` |
| `anim_retarget_batch` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (IKRetargetBatchOperation) via job-envelope | GenOrca (single); net-new for batch + async + dry-run | Headline moat: batch-retarget a set of AnimSequences from source to target skeleton via a retargeter, writing new assets. Long-running → job-envelope so it never freezes the editor. — `(retargeter_path, source_sequences[], dest_path, suffix?, dry_run?) -> {job_id}\|{created[], failures[]}` |
| `control_rig_create_from_template` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (ControlRigBlueprintFactory) — flagged engine limitation | GenOrca (ControlRig); we are explicit about scope | Create a ControlRig blueprint for a mesh from a template (e.g. FK Control Rig / Basic), honestly scoped to template-instantiation — rig-graph authoring is an engine ceiling. — `(package_path, name, mesh_path, template:Literal) -> {path, note:'graph authoring not scriptable'}` |
| `control_rig_hierarchy_edit` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (ControlRig hierarchy_controller) | net-new moat | Add/remove controls, nulls, and bones in a ControlRig hierarchy and set control settings — the part of ControlRig that IS Python-scriptable. — `(path, ops:[{op:Literal, name, type, parent?, settings?}]) -> {applied_count, hierarchy[]}` |
| `control_rig_bake_to_sequence` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary) via job-envelope | GenOrca (ControlRig bake) | Bake a ControlRig's animation in a level sequence down to a new AnimSequence — the production path for hand-keyed/procedural rig output. Job-envelope. — `(level_sequence, control_rig_binding, dest_path, frame_range?) -> {job_id}\|{anim_path}` |
| `sequence_bake_to_control_rig` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary.bake_to_control_rig) | net-new moat | Bake an existing AnimSequence onto an FK ControlRig track in a level sequence (so animators can tweak captured/retargeted anim) — reverse of the above. — `(level_sequence, mesh_binding, anim_path, control_rig_class?) -> {ok, control_rig_binding}` |
| `anim_preview_render` | P1 | python_run-backed TS + editor_capture_viewport | GenOrca vision overlay / Roblox capture; reuses our strongest moat | Vision verify: spawn/pose a preview SkeletalMeshActor with a sequence/montage at time t, auto-frame on bounds, capture viewport PNG (image content block) — before/after the agent's edit. — `(mesh_path, anim_path, time_s?, orbit_angles?) -> {images[] (PNG blocks)}` |
| `anim_play_in_editor_test` | P2 | python_run-backed TS + editor_start_pie + capture | Flop pie_test / remiphilippe PIE | Spawn a preview actor playing an anim/montage in PIE, sample N screenshots across the timeline, and report (loop/blend sanity) — gameplay verify without leaving the loop. — `(mesh_path, anim_path, samples=4, duration_s?) -> {frames[] (PNG blocks), pie_log_tail}` |
| `anim_validate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (validator_run + checks) | net-new moat (PLUMB depth none match) | Run PLUMB/validator + structural checks on an anim asset (skeleton-compat, missing curves, broken montage slot, retarget root unset) before/after mutate — the closed-loop correctness gate. — `(asset_path, profile?) -> {ok, issues[], severity}` |
| `anim_checkpoint` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (FScopedTransaction bundle) — cross-cuts 4C | net-new moat (Epic per-call undo only) | Domain dry-run/checkpoint+restore wrapper: snapshot an anim asset (or AnimBP) before a risky multi-step edit and roll back as ONE undo if validate fails — generalizes world_generate's dry_run→commit. — `(asset_path, action:Literal['begin'\|'commit'\|'rollback'], checkpoint_id?) -> {checkpoint_id, ok}` |

**Dependencies:** UE 5.7 Python: unreal.AnimationLibrary (notifies/curves/sync/virtual-bones/retarget-options), unreal.IKRigController + IKRetargeterController + IKRetargetBatchOperation, unreal.ControlRigBlueprint hierarchy_controller + ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary, BlendSpaceFactory1D/BlendSpaceFactory, AnimBlueprintFactory/AnimMontageFactory/ControlRigBlueprintFactory, AssetTools/AssetRegistry, SequencerScripting; Engine plugins enabled at load: IKRig, ControlRig + ControlRigDeveloper, SequencerScripting, AnimationModifierLibrary; Existing C++ FHaybaMCPAnimationHandler (anim_blueprint_get_info/add_state/add_transition/set_condition/compile) — needs first-class TS wrappers + sidecar.json entries; Theme 0 job-envelope (async command conversion in docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md) for batch retarget + bakes; RunSync seam (FHaybaGameThread) for AnimBP compile; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor: pyTemplate descriptor factory + auto-collected descriptors[] so the python-backed tools ship with no rebuild; Theme 4C transaction/undo C++ handler (FScopedTransaction) for anim_checkpoint and to transaction-wrap the C++ state-machine + set_animation handlers; Vision loop tools (editor_capture_viewport, render_camera, auto-frame) for preview/PIE verify; PLUMB validator (validator_run) for anim_validate

**Risks:** ControlRig rig-GRAPH (RigVM) authoring is NOT exposed to UE Python — only hierarchy + bake are scriptable. Must be flagged as engine limitation, never advertised as full ControlRig authoring (honesty invariant).; BlendSpace per-sample graph editing is partly C++-only in UE Python; blendspace_add_sample may need a C++ fallback in AnimationHandler — design returns 'deferred' rather than silently failing.; Long ops (anim_retarget_batch, control_rig_bake_to_sequence) are game-thread-heavy and WILL freeze the editor if synchronous — they MUST go through the job-envelope; do not ship them as blocking python_run calls.; AnimBP compile blocks the game thread; route via RunSync seam + align executor timeout to UE ceiling, else the Theme-0 freeze bug recurs.; Skeleton mismatch (sequence vs target skeleton, retarget root unset, name-mismatched chains) causes silent no-ops in UE — anim_validate must catch these before mutate, and every write tool returns a readback.; Montage section/next-link editing and state BoundGraph anim assignment have thin/absent Python setters; anim_state_set_animation is correctly placed in C++.; BoundGraph/state-graph edits via C++ FGraphNodeCreator can crash on malformed graphs — keep them under the ProcessCommand SEH guard (Theme 0) and transaction-wrap.; UE 5.7 enum churn already bit this handler (EAnimStateType::AST_State removed); ControlRig/IKRig Python API also drifts across 5.x — pin tested API and degrade gracefully across versions.

**Live-editor test plan:** "Use a project with the UE5 Mannequin set (SK_Mannequin/SK_Quinn, ABP_Manny, IK_Mannequin + RTG_Manny-to-Quinn). 1) Read path: anim_blueprint_inspect(ABP_Manny), anim_sequence_inspect(MM_Run), skeleton_inspect(SK_Mannequin), ik_retargeter_inspect(RTG_*) — confirm states/curves/chains print and match the editor. 2) AnimBP edits: anim_blueprint_create a fresh ABP for SK_Mannequin, anim_state_add Idle+Run, anim_transition_add Idle->Run, anim_transition_set_condition true, anim_blueprint_compile -> assert error_count==0; re-inspect to confirm states persisted (readback). 3) Pure-python edits: blendspace_create 2D Speed/Direction, montage_create from MM_Attack on slot DefaultSlot, anim_notify_add a 'footstep' at 0.3s on MM_Run then anim_sequence_inspect to confirm the notify; anim_curve_add an IKWeight curve; skeleton_add_socket weapon_r on hand_r. 4) Retarget moat: ik_rig_create + ik_rig_add_chain on a custom mesh OR use stock rigs; anim_retarget_batch RTG_Manny-to-Quinn over [MM_Run,MM_Idle] dry_run=true (inspect plan), then commit -> assert new AnimSequences exist under dest and play on Quinn. 5) ControlRig: control_rig_create_from_template FK rig for SK_Mannequin; control_rig_bake_to_sequence on a small level sequence -> assert AnimSequence written. 6) Verify/vision: anim_preview_render(SK_Quinn, retargeted MM_Run, orbit) returns PNG blocks showing correct pose; anim_play_in_editor_test samples 4 frames in PIE. 7) Safety: anim_checkpoint begin -> make a bad edit -> anim_validate flags it -> rollback restores in ONE undo (Ctrl+Z parity). Watch editor_stream_log + hayba_journal_tail throughout to confirm no game-thread freeze on batch/bake ops and that SEH guard catches any malformed-graph crash."


<a id="domain-13"></a>
## 13. audio-and-metasounds

*32 tools — P0:12 P1:11 P2:6 P3:3*

No UE MCP competitor ships real audio authoring depth, so almost everything here is a net-new moat; the cheapest, highest-ROI strategy is python_run-backed wrappers (USoundCue/USoundClass/USoundMix/USoundSubmix/USoundAttenuation/UReverbEffect are all freely authorable via UE Editor-Scripting with zero plugin rebuild). The two existing C++ surfaces (HaybaMCPAudioHandler 3 cmds, HaybaMCPMetaSound create/list) get thin first-class wrappers, and the deferred MetaSound graph-mutation gap is honestly scoped to template-instantiation + default-input setting + interface introspection rather than fabricating unstable FrontendDocumentBuilder calls. Every write domain ships a paired read tool (audio_inspect) and a soundcue/submix dry-run, and all mutations route through a C++ FScopedTransaction seam so a multi-node SoundCue build is ONE Ctrl+Z, the named differentiator vs Epic's per-call undo. We lean into PLUMB-style audio_validate (no dangling nodes, 3D sounds require attenuation, submix loops detected) as the closed-loop correctness engine no audio competitor has.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `audio_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/inspect.ts) | net-new moat (no UE MCP introspects audio assets) | Universal read-back for any sound asset (SoundWave/Cue/MetaSound/Class/Mix/Submix/Attenuation/Concurrency/ReverbEffect): resolved properties, cue/submix graph summary, MetaSound interface, the inspection-first paired read tool that makes every write verifiable. — `(asset_path, include_graph=true) -> {class, properties, graph_nodes[], interface{inputs[],outputs[]}, referenced_assets[]}` |
| `audio_list` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (audio_list) via TS wrapper | self (C++ audio_list exists, no TS wrapper) | Paginated list of sound assets filtered by class (SoundWave/SoundCue/MetaSoundSource/SoundClass/Submix/...) and path prefix with duration/sample-rate tags, first-class wrapper over the existing C++ audio_list extended with class filter + pagination. — `(class_filter?, path_prefix?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {sounds[], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `sound_cue_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/cue.ts) | net-new moat | Create a USoundCue from one or more SoundWaves with a chosen template (single/looping/random/mixer/randomized-pitch) wired in one shot, the core authoring outcome-verb that turns raw waves into game-ready cues. — `(package_path, name, waves[], template='single', looping=false, dry_run=false) -> {path, nodes_created, ok}` |
| `sound_cue_edit_graph` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/cue.ts) | net-new moat (GenOrca has material-graph, none have cue-graph) | Add / connect / set / remove SoundCue graph nodes (Mixer, Random, Looping, Modulator, Concatenator, Attenuation, Delay, WavePlayer) on an existing cue, fine-grained graph authoring beyond the create templates. — `(cue_path, ops[{op:add\|connect\|set\|remove, node_type?, node_id?, to_id?, props?}], dry_run=false) -> {applied, graph_nodes[], ok}` |
| `soundclass_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/class.ts) | net-new moat | Create a USoundClass, set its parent in the mixing hierarchy, and apply default properties (volume, pitch, output bus, LFE), the routing backbone for music/sfx/ui/voice channel control. — `(package_path, name, parent_class?, volume=1.0, pitch=1.0) -> {path, ok}` |
| `soundmix_set_adjuster` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/mix.ts) | net-new moat (improves on audio_set_volume stub) | Create/edit a USoundMix and set per-SoundClass volume/pitch/EQ adjusters (the data behind ducking, stealth-muffle, menu-dim), replaces the C++ stub that only supports 'master' transient volume. — `(mix_path, name, adjusters[{sound_class, vol, pitch, apply_to_children}], dry_run=false) -> {path, adjuster_count, ok}` |
| `submix_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/submix.ts) | net-new moat | Create a USoundSubmix and set its parent submix routing, establishes the DSP bus topology (Reverb bus, Music bus, Master) that effect chains attach to. — `(package_path, name, parent_submix?, output_volume_db=0) -> {path, ok}` |
| `submix_add_effect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/submix.ts) | net-new moat | Append/insert a submix effect preset (Reverb, Submix EQ, Dynamics/Compressor, Filter, Convolution-reverb, Tap-delay) into a submix effect chain with typed params, productionizing the DSP layer competitors don't touch. — `(submix_path, effect_type, params{}, index=-1, dry_run=false) -> {effect_preset_path, chain[], ok}` |
| `attenuation_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/attenuation.ts) | net-new moat | Create a USoundAttenuation asset with spatialization, distance-falloff curve, air-absorption, occlusion and focus settings, required correctness data for any 3D positioned sound. — `(package_path, name, falloff_min, falloff_max, shape='sphere', enable_occlusion=false, enable_spatialization=true) -> {path, ok}` |
| `metasound_create` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound (metasound_create) via TS wrapper | self (C++ exists, no TS wrapper) | Create a blank UMetaSoundSource asset, first-class TS wrapper over the working C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound create command. — `(package_path, name) -> {path, class, ok}` |
| `metasound_instantiate_template` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound (new metasound_instantiate) | net-new moat (steal: Maya parametric-prefab pattern) | Duplicate a known-good template MetaSoundSource/Patch and rename it, the honest ceiling-aware way to ship MetaSound authoring given UE 5.7's unstable FrontendDocumentBuilder instead of fabricating graph-mutation calls. — `(template_path, package_path, name) -> {path, interface{inputs[]}, ok}` |
| `metasound_set_default_input` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound (new metasound_set_input real impl) | net-new moat | Set the default value of a named graph input / interface member on a MetaSoundSource (param packs), the stable mutation surface that survives the 5.7 builder churn, enabling parameterized sound design. — `(metasound_path, input_name, value, type='float') -> {input_name, old_value, new_value, ok}` |
| `metasound_inspect` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound (new metasound_inspect) | net-new moat | Read a MetaSound's interface (inputs/outputs with types+defaults), declared interfaces, and referenced patches/graphs, paired read tool so agents can discover settable params before mutating. — `(metasound_path) -> {inputs[{name,type,default}], outputs[], interfaces[], referenced[]}` |
| `metasound_list` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPMetaSound (metasound_list) via TS wrapper | self | Paginated list of MetaSoundSource/Patch assets under a path, first-class wrapper over the working C++ metasound_list. — `(path_prefix='/Game', limit=50, offset=0) -> {metasounds[], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `audio_transaction_begin` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (transaction seam, shared) | net-new moat (vs Epic per-call undo) | Open a named FScopedTransaction so a multi-step audio authoring bundle (cue + class + submix wiring) collapses into ONE Ctrl+Z, the named safety differentiator vs Epic's per-call undo. — `(label) -> {transaction_id, ok}` |
| `audio_transaction_commit` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (transaction seam, shared) | net-new moat | Close the open audio transaction, finalizing all wrapped mutations as a single undo unit. — `(transaction_id) -> {ok}` |
| `audio_undo` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (transaction seam, shared) | parity (Unity/Roblox have undo/redo) | Undo the last audio authoring transaction (and redo) via the editor transactor, safe rollback for agent mistakes. — `(steps=1, direction='undo') -> {undone, current_label, ok}` |
