| `audio_validate` | P0 | python_run-backed TS + validator hook (src/tools/audio/validate.ts) | net-new moat (beyond Flop auto-repair) | PLUMB-style quantified check on a cue/submix/MetaSound: dangling/unreachable nodes, 3D sound missing attenuation, submix parent cycles, missing wave references, clipping gain sums, closed-loop validate-before/after-mutate no audio competitor has. — `(asset_path) -> {ok, violations[{code,severity,node,message}], score}` |
| `assign_sound_to_class` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/class.ts) | net-new moat | Set the SoundClassOverride on one SoundBase (cue/wave/metasound) so it inherits the right mixing channel, the connective tissue between assets and the class hierarchy. — `(sound_path, sound_class_path) -> {sound, sound_class, ok}` |
| `attenuation_assign` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/attenuation.ts) | net-new moat | Attach a SoundAttenuation (and optional concurrency) to a cue/wave/metasound default or to an AmbientSound actor, makes a sound spatial without manual editor clicks. — `(target_path, attenuation_path, concurrency_path?) -> {target, ok}` |
| `concurrency_create` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/concurrency.ts) | net-new moat | Create a USoundConcurrency asset (max-count, resolution rule, volume-scale duck), voice-limiting data that prevents audio-channel exhaustion in dense scenes. — `(package_path, name, max_count=16, resolution='StopOldest', volume_scale=1.0) -> {path, ok}` |
| `reverb_effect_create` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/reverb.ts) | net-new moat | Create a UReverbEffect asset with density/decay/diffusion/gain params for use by audio volumes or the reverb submix, environmental DSP authoring. — `(package_path, name, decay_time=1.5, density=1.0, gain=0.32, params{}) -> {path, ok}` |
| `ambient_sound_spawn` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/place.ts) | net-new moat | Spawn an AAmbientSound actor at a world location wired to a cue/metasound + attenuation with autoplay flag, places diegetic sound in the level in one outcome-verb. — `(sound_path, location[x,y,z], attenuation_path?, auto_activate=true) -> {actor_path, ok}` |
| `audio_component_add` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/place.ts) | net-new moat | Add a UAudioComponent to an existing actor with sound/volume/pitch/auto-activate set, attaches sound to gameplay objects (engines, doors, props). — `(actor_path, sound_path, volume=1.0, pitch=1.0, auto_activate=false) -> {component_name, ok}` |
| `soundwave_import` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/import.ts) | parity (Blender asset-import pattern) | Import wav/ogg/flac files into the project via Interchange/AssetImportTask with target package path and basic compression settings, feeds raw audio into the authoring pipeline. — `(file_paths[], dest_path, compression_quality=40, looping=false) -> {imported[], failed[], ok}` |
| `soundwave_set_properties` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/batch.ts) | parity (Roblox mass_set_property) | Batch-set properties on many SoundWaves (looping, compression quality, streaming, force-mono, volume/pitch multipliers), bulk asset hygiene over a selection or query. — `(wave_paths[]\|query, props{looping?,compression?,streaming?,mono?}, dry_run=false) -> {updated, skipped, ok}` |
| `audio_batch_assign` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/batch.ts) | parity (Roblox mass_set_property; net-new for audio) | Batch-assign SoundClass / Attenuation / Concurrency across many sounds matched by class+path query, outcome-verb for wiring a whole folder of SFX to the right mixing setup at once. — `(query{class,path_prefix}, sound_class?, attenuation?, concurrency?, dry_run=false) -> {matched, updated, sample[], ok}` |
| `submix_send_set` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/submix.ts) | net-new moat | Set a sound's base submix and submix sends (with send level), routes individual sounds into reverb/music/master buses completing the DSP graph wiring. — `(sound_path, base_submix?, sends[{submix, level_db}], dry_run=false) -> {sound, sends[], ok}` |
| `audio_play` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (audio_play) via TS wrapper | self (C++ exists) | Preview-play a cue/wave/metasound 2D in the editor at volume/pitch, first-class wrapper over the existing C++ audio_play for the audition feedback loop. — `(path, volume=1.0, pitch=1.0) -> {playing, ok}` |
| `audio_stop_all` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (new audio_stop) | net-new moat | Stop all active editor preview audio voices, cleanup verb so chained auditions don't pile up. — `() -> {stopped_count, ok}` |
| `submix_bounce_to_wav` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/bounce.ts) PIE-only | net-new moat (steal: remiphilippe headless render) | Record a submix's output to a wav file during PIE (StartRecordingOutput/FinishRecordingOutput), offline audition/diff artifact for the feedback loop, flagged PIE-dependent. — `(submix_path, duration_s, out_file, sound_to_play?) -> {wav_path, samples, ok}` |
| `modulation_control_bus_create` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (src/tools/audio/modulation.ts) | net-new moat | Create an Audio Modulation control bus + modulator (LFO/envelope-follower) for parameter-driven mixing, advanced runtime control requiring the AudioModulation plugin. — `(package_path, name, param_class='Volume', default=1.0) -> {bus_path, ok}` |

**Dependencies:** UE Editor-Scripting (unreal Python): EditorAssetLibrary, AssetToolsHelpers, NewObject for USoundCue/USoundClass/USoundMix/USoundSubmix/USoundAttenuation/USoundConcurrency/UReverbEffect; Existing C++ HaybaMCPAudioHandler (audio_play, audio_list, audio_set_volume), extend not rewrite; Existing HaybaMCPMetaSound plugin (metasound_create, metasound_list working; add_node/connect/set_input/compile deferred); Hardened python_run handler (SEH + 3-tier gate + dangling-delegate gate) as dispatch vehicle for all TS wrappers; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor: ToolDescriptor with pyTemplate factory so these land with zero per-tool 7-file churn; C++ FScopedTransaction seam (Theme 4C) shared across handlers for audio_transaction_*/audio_undo; PLUMB validator hook (validator_run / plumb_validate) for audio_validate integration; Interchange / AssetImportTask for soundwave_import; AudioMixerBlueprintLibrary + PIE for submix_bounce_to_wav; AudioModulation plugin for modulation_control_bus_create; MetasoundEngine/MetasoundFrontend modules already linked in HaybaMCPMetaSound.Build.cs for metasound_inspect/set_default_input

**Risks:** MetaSound graph mutation (add_node/connect/compile) is genuinely unstable across UE 5.3-5.7 FrontendDocumentBuilder; DO NOT fabricate, honestly scope to template-instantiation + set_default_input + inspect, flagged engine limitation (matches existing deferred-stub decision in HaybaMCPMetaSoundHandler.cpp); metasound_set_default_input/inspect need verification that 5.7 frontend exposes stable member-default getters/setters; may need C++ and could itself be partially deferred; SoundCue graph construction via Python (construct_sound_node / first_node) is known but node-class names + pin wiring are version-sensitive; sound_cue_edit_graph needs per-version smoke tests; submix_bounce_to_wav requires active PIE and writes to disk; PIE is itself a gap area and offline audio render can hang the game thread; keep duration short, run under wait_for_idle, P3 only; audio_play uses GWorld + PlaySound2D which only audibly works with an editor audio device / PIE; in -nullrhi or headless CI it silently no-ops, return a clear 'no audio device' code not false success; Audio Modulation is a plugin that may not be enabled; modulation tool must detect-and-degrade gracefully; Transaction-wrapping Python-side mutations won't be captured by C++ FScopedTransaction (per brief 4C); audio_undo reliably covers only C++ handler mutations, Python authoring needs unreal.ScopedEditorTransaction inside the pyTemplate, document the split; SetTransientPrimaryVolume (existing audio_set_volume 'master') is transient and lost on PIE restart; soundmix_set_adjuster is the durable path, deprecate the stub category arg honestly

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live UE 5.7 editor with HaybaMCPToolkit + HaybaMCPMetaSound loaded and a project containing a few SoundWaves: (1) audio_list class_filter=SoundWave to confirm read path + pagination. (2) sound_cue_create template='random' over 3 waves, then audio_inspect the new cue to confirm graph_nodes match, then audio_validate for 0 violations; deliberately create a 3D cue without attenuation and confirm audio_validate flags it. (3) soundclass_create music with parent, soundmix_set_adjuster ducking music -6dB, assign_sound_to_class the cue, audio_inspect to verify SoundClassOverride. (4) submix_create Reverb under Master, submix_add_effect type='reverb', submix_send_set the cue to it, audio_inspect submix to confirm effect chain + send. (5) attenuation_create + attenuation_assign + ambient_sound_spawn at a location; editor_capture_viewport to confirm the AmbientSound actor placed. (6) Wrap steps 2-5 in audio_transaction_begin/commit, then audio_undo(1) and re-inspect to confirm the whole bundle reverted in one step (Ctrl+Z parity proof). (7) metasound_create, metasound_instantiate_template from a known template, metasound_inspect to list interface inputs, metasound_set_default_input on a float param, re-inspect to confirm new_value; if set_default_input returns the deferred error log it honestly as engine-limited. (8) Run every write tool once with dry_run=true and diff against the committed result to verify dry-run fidelity. (9) Start PIE, audio_play the cue + submix_bounce_to_wav 2s, confirm a non-empty wav on disk; in headless/-nullrhi confirm audio_play returns 'no audio device' rather than false success.


<a id="domain-14"></a>
## 14. world-partition-streaming-datalayers

*31 tools — P0:7 P1:12 P2:8 P3:4*

World Partition, runtime streaming, Data Layers, and Level Instances are the connective tissue of any large UE5 open world and a hard gap versus every competitor (Epic baseline, Aura, Flop, GenOrca ship essentially nothing here). Today Hayba has only 3 thin C++ commands (wp_get_cells, wp_load_cell stubbed, wp_get_streaming_state stubbed) and no Data Layer or Level Instance surface at all. The strategy is python_run-first: UE's DataLayerEditorSubsystem, WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary, LevelInstanceSubsystem, and EditorLevelUtils cover ~90% of the authoring loop with zero plugin rebuild, so we ship breadth fast and reserve C++ for the two existing stubs and a fast cell-aggregation read path. Every write tool gets a paired *_inspect read tool, a dry_run preview, query-based batch selection (matching Roblox peer mass_* verbs), and FScopedTransaction/undo grouping so a multi-actor data-layer assignment is one Ctrl+Z. This domain is a net-new moat: spatial/streaming worldbuilding correctness nobody else automates, feeding PLUMB validation (e.g. no gameplay actor outside a runtime data layer).

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `wp_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary + UWorldPartition); hot-path upgrade into C++ HaybaMCPWorldPartitionHandler | net-new moat (no UE MCP has WP introspection) | One-call world overview (WP enabled, runtime grids, cell size, streaming-source count, loaded/unloaded actors, data-layer + level-instance counts) so every other tool can read-first and branch. — `() -> {enabled, grids[], cell_size, streaming_sources, actors:{loaded,unloaded,total}, data_layers, level_instances}` |
| `wp_list_cells` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPWorldPartitionHandler (extend wp_get_cells with pagination + loaded flag + real cell size) | net-new moat | Paginated list of runtime/streaming cells with bounds, actor count, top classes, loaded state; replaces the 100-cap C++ wp_get_cells with honest pagination so agents can reason about spatial layout. — `(grid?, limit=50, offset=0, only_nonempty?) -> {cells:[{cell_id,bounds,actor_count,top_classes[],loaded}], total, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `wp_get_streaming_state` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPWorldPartitionHandler (replace stubbed WpGetStreamingState body) | net-new moat | Finish the stubbed handler: report active streaming sources (location/radius/priority), loaded cells, and pending load/unload so agents can verify what the runtime would stream. — `() -> {enabled, sources:[{name,location,radius,priority}], loaded_cells[], pending[]}` |
| `wp_load_region` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary.load_actors / GetEditorLocationVolume) | net-new moat | Load all WP actors whose bounds intersect a world-space box/sphere into the editor (the real fix for stubbed wp_load_cell) so later edits/captures actually see them; without this, edits silently no-op on unloaded actors. — `(center, extent \| radius, dry_run?) -> {loaded_actor_count, would_load?}` |
| `wp_unload_region` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary.unload_actors) | net-new moat | Unload editor-loaded WP actors in a region to free memory and restore a clean streaming state after batch edits; inverse of wp_load_region, key for keeping large worlds responsive. — `(center, extent \| radius, dry_run?) -> {unloaded_actor_count}` |
| `wp_load_actors_by_query` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (actor-desc iteration + load_actors) | net-new moat | Load WP actors matching a class/tag/name/data-layer query (not just spatial) so an agent can pull e.g. all foliage or all of DataLayer 'Combat' into the editor before mutating them. — `(class?, tag?, name_contains?, data_layer?, limit=200, dry_run?) -> {matched, loaded, actor_paths[]}` |
| `wp_pin_actors` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary.pin_actors) | net-new moat | Pin specific WP actors so they stay loaded regardless of streaming-source position; needed for stable multi-step authoring on a known actor set. — `(actor_paths[], dry_run?) -> {pinned[]}` |
| `wp_unpin_actors` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionBlueprintLibrary.unpin_actors) | net-new moat | Release pinned WP actors so normal streaming resumes; paired inverse of wp_pin_actors to avoid leaking forced-loaded actors. — `(actor_paths[] \| all?) -> {unpinned[]}` |
| `wp_simulate_streaming_at` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartitionStreamingSource via UWorldPartitionSubsystem) | net-new moat | Add a temporary editor streaming source at a world position and report which cells/actors would load: a 'what would the player see here' dry-run, the killer verify tool for streaming correctness. — `(location, radius, dry_run=true) -> {cells_loaded[], actor_count, est_memory_mb?}` |
| `wp_set_runtime_grid` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (set_actor_runtime_grid / property set) | net-new moat | Set an actor's RuntimeGrid placement (coarse grid for large landmarks, fine for gameplay props) so streaming granularity matches design intent. — `(actor_paths[], grid_name, dry_run?) -> {updated[], invalid[]}` |
| `wp_enable` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (WorldPartition conversion utils); commandlet fallback if Python path missing | net-new moat | Enable World Partition on the current/specified level (or report why not), Plan-Mode gated since it is a destructive level conversion; the on-ramp for non-WP worlds. — `(level_path?, confirm=false, dry_run=true) -> {enabled, requires_restart?, warnings[]}` |
| `wp_build_hlod` | P2 | python_run-backed TS dispatch + job-envelope (mirror Theme 0 build-handler async pattern) | remiphilippe (headless build) partial; net-new for WP HLOD | Kick the WP HLOD build as a job-envelope async command (not a blocking game-thread call) and poll status; regenerate HLOD proxies for distant cells without freezing the editor. — `(grids?, force?) -> {job_id}; poll -> {state,progress,built_cells,errors[]}` |
| `datalayer_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.create_data_layer / DataLayerAsset) | net-new moat | Create a DataLayerAsset + instance with name, type (Editor/Runtime), and initial state; foundational Data Layer authoring tool no competitor offers. — `(name, type=Runtime, parent?, initial_runtime_state=Unloaded, dry_run?) -> {asset_path, instance_id}` |
| `datalayer_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerManager / DataLayerEditorSubsystem iteration) | net-new moat | List all data layers with type, runtime state, editor loaded/visible flags, parent, and assigned-actor count; inspect-first companion for the whole data-layer surface. — `(limit=50, offset=0) -> {layers:[{name,asset_path,type,runtime_state,is_loaded_editor,is_visible,parent,actor_count}], total, has_more}` |
| `datalayer_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.get_actors_from_data_layer) | net-new moat | Deep read of one data layer: hierarchy, every assigned actor (paginated), effective runtime/editor state; drill-down to verify assignments before/after a mutation. — `(name \| asset_path, limit=50, offset=0) -> {layer{...}, actors[], children[], has_more}` |
| `datalayer_delete` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.delete_data_layer) | net-new moat | Delete a data layer (optionally reassigning its actors), transaction-wrapped and Plan-Mode gated; clean removal without orphaning actor references. — `(name \| asset_path, reassign_to?, confirm=false, dry_run=true) -> {deleted, affected_actors}` |
| `datalayer_rename` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (asset rename + DataLayerEditorSubsystem) | net-new moat | Rename/relabel a data layer asset and fix up references; unglamorous but needed for iterative worldbuilding cleanup. — `(name \| asset_path, new_name) -> {ok, asset_path}` |
| `datalayer_assign_actors` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.add_actor_to_data_layer) | net-new moat (cf. Roblox add_tag/bulk_set_attributes) | Add a list of actors to a data layer in ONE transaction (one Ctrl+Z) with membership read-back; core authoring verb for organizing a world into streaming groups. — `(name \| asset_path, actor_paths[], dry_run?) -> {assigned[], already_member[], invalid[]}` |
| `datalayer_remove_actors` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.remove_actor_from_data_layer) | net-new moat | Remove actors from a data layer in one transaction with read-back; paired inverse so membership edits are reversible and verifiable. — `(name \| asset_path, actor_paths[], dry_run?) -> {removed[], not_member[]}` |
| `datalayer_assign_by_query` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (EditorActorSubsystem query + DataLayerEditorSubsystem) | net-new moat (cf. Roblox search_by_property+mass_set) | Assign every actor matching a class/tag/name/region query to a data layer (outcome-verb over primitives), e.g. put all Light actors in DataLayer Lighting, in one batched dry-run-able call. — `(name, class?, tag?, name_contains?, region?, dry_run=true, limit=1000) -> {matched, assigned, sample_paths[]}` |
| `datalayer_set_runtime_state` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.set_data_layer_runtime_state) | net-new moat | Set a data layer's runtime state (Unloaded/Loaded/Activated) to simulate/preview gameplay streaming behavior in-editor; verify which content gates on which layer. — `(name \| asset_path, state, include_children?) -> {state}` |
| `datalayer_set_editor_loaded` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.set_data_layer_is_loaded_in_editor) | net-new moat | Load/unload a data layer's actors in the editor so the agent (and vision capture) can see or hide a content group while authoring. — `(name \| asset_path, loaded) -> {is_loaded}` |
| `datalayer_set_visible` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.set_data_layer_is_visible) | net-new moat | Toggle a data layer's editor visibility without unloading; fast isolate/hide for capture and review workflows. — `(name \| asset_path, visible) -> {is_visible}` |
| `datalayer_actor_membership` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (DataLayerEditorSubsystem.get_data_layer_instances_from_actor) | net-new moat | Read all data layers a given actor belongs to; actor-centric inverse lookup to debug 'why is this actor not streaming'. — `(actor_path) -> {layers:[{name,type,runtime_state}]}` |
| `level_instance_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (LevelInstanceSubsystem / EditorActorSubsystem) | net-new moat | List Level Instance / Packed Level Actor instances with source level, transform, loaded/edit state; inspect-first for the level-instance loop. — `(limit=50, offset=0) -> {instances:[{actor_path,level_asset,transform,is_editing,loaded}], total, has_more}` |
| `level_instance_create_from_actors` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (EditorLevelUtils / new_level_instance) | net-new moat | Pack selected actors into a new Level Instance asset (transaction-wrapped, dry-run preview); the reuse/streaming-unit verb for repeating world chunks. — `(actor_paths[], level_asset_path, pivot?, packed?, dry_run=true) -> {instance_actor, level_asset}` |
| `level_instance_spawn` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (LevelInstanceSubsystem) | net-new moat | Spawn an existing Level Instance asset into the world at a transform; place reusable streaming sub-levels programmatically. — `(level_asset_path, location, rotation?, scale?, data_layer?) -> {actor_path}` |
| `level_instance_set_edit` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (LevelInstanceSubsystem.edit_level_instance / commit) | net-new moat | Enter or commit/discard edit mode on a Level Instance so its contents can be mutated and saved back; gated since commit writes the asset. — `(actor_path, mode=enter\|commit\|discard, confirm_on_commit=false) -> {is_editing, saved?}` |
| `level_instance_break` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (LevelInstanceSubsystem.break_level_instance) | net-new moat | Break a Level Instance back into loose actors in the current level (transaction-wrapped, Plan-Mode gated); inverse of pack, for de-instancing. — `(actor_path, confirm=false, dry_run=true) -> {released_actors[]}` |
| `wp_validate_streaming` | P1 | python_run-backed TS feeding validator_run / PLUMB constraints | net-new moat (PLUMB validator extension; beyond Flop auto-repair) | PLUMB-fed correctness pass: flag gameplay actors with no data layer, actors outside any cell, empty/oversized cells, layers with no runtime state, dangling level-instance refs; closed-loop validate-before/after-mutate, our moat applied to WP. — `(rules?, region?) -> {issues:[{code,severity,actor_or_layer,message,fix_hint}], counts}` |
| `wp_transaction_group` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit transaction seam (FScopedTransaction; reuse Theme 4C begin/end-transaction) | net-new moat (Epic = per-call undo only) | Begin/end a named transaction around a batch of WP/data-layer mutations so multi-tool sequences collapse into ONE Ctrl+Z; the cross-cutting safety differentiator vs Epic per-call undo, applied here. — `(action=begin\|end\|cancel, label?) -> {tx_id, open}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 0 job-envelope async conversion must land before wp_build_hlod (HLOD build is long-running and would otherwise freeze the game thread, same bug class as build_*/test_run).; Theme 4C transaction seam (FScopedTransaction begin/end-transaction in C++): wp_transaction_group and full transaction-wrapping of mutating tools depend on it; Python ScopedEditorTransaction does NOT capture C++ handler mutations but DOES capture python_run actor edits, so python tools can self-wrap in the interim.; PLUMB validator + constraint language (wp_validate_streaming registers WP-specific rules into validator_run).; A World-Partition-enabled test map with multiple runtime grids + existing data layers for live validation; most tools degrade to clear 'WP not enabled' / 'no data layers' errors otherwise.; ue-python.js helpers (runUePythonJson, pyStr, _emit/_err harness) and the hardened python_run handler back all python wrappers.; Add-a-tool refactor (Theme 1) descriptor + pyTemplate factory so these ~30 wrappers ship as data, not 30x7-file churn.

**Risks:** UE Python WP coverage is uneven in 5.7: load_actors/unload_actors/pin_actors exist, but editor streaming-source injection (wp_simulate_streaming_at) and HLOD build may need C++ or a commandlet fallback; flag as engine-limitation if the Python path is missing rather than shipping a stub (honesty invariant).; Loading large regions (wp_load_region / load_by_query) can spike memory and stall the editor on big worlds; enforce limit caps, return est counts in dry_run first, and pair every load with an unload.; wp_enable / level_instance commit / datalayer_delete are genuinely destructive (level conversion, asset writes, reference fixup): strictly Plan-Mode + confirm + dry_run gated; a bad convert can require an editor restart.; MEMORY: no TCP sockets from UE Python to plugin ports and no AsyncTask(GameThread)+Wait inside handlers; all python tools run via python_run on the game thread, so keep each call short to avoid the HaybaMCP late-response/UAF crash class.; Data Layer API churned across UE versions (legacy ActorDataLayers vs DataLayerAsset/Instance in 5.7); pin to the 5.7 DataLayerEditorSubsystem surface and detect-and-error on older shapes rather than guessing.; Existing C++ wp_list_cells buckets by a hardcoded 51200 cell size with a 100 cap; must read the real per-grid cell size and paginate or counts will mislead the agent.

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live editor with a World-Partition map (multiple runtime grids + 2-3 existing data layers): 1) Read path first: wp_inspect, wp_list_cells (verify pagination has_more/next_offset), wp_get_streaming_state (confirm the previously-stubbed handler now returns real sources/loaded cells), datalayer_list, level_instance_list; cross-check counts against the WP editor outliner. 2) Data-layer round-trip: datalayer_create('Test_DL', Runtime) -> datalayer_assign_by_query(class='StaticMeshActor', dry_run=true) inspect matched -> run for real -> datalayer_inspect to confirm membership -> datalayer_set_editor_loaded(false) + editor_capture_viewport to visually confirm actors vanish -> set_editor_loaded(true) -> datalayer_remove_actors -> datalayer_delete; verify a SINGLE Ctrl+Z (via wp_transaction_group) reverts the assign batch. 3) Streaming: wp_load_region(dry_run) compare would_load vs real loaded_actor_count via wp_inspect delta, then wp_unload_region restores; wp_simulate_streaming_at over a known landmark and confirm reported cells match wp_list_cells bounds. 4) Validation: introduce a gameplay actor with no data layer, run wp_validate_streaming, confirm it is flagged with a fix_hint, assign it, re-run, confirm clean. 5) Safety: confirm wp_enable / datalayer_delete / level_instance_break refuse without confirm=true and emit a dry_run preview; confirm wp_build_hlod returns a job_id immediately (editor stays responsive) and polls to completion. Capture before/after viewport PNGs for each visible change rather than trusting counts alone.


<a id="domain-15"></a>
## 15. asset-import-and-sources

*30 tools — P0:7 P1:13 P2:10 P3:0*

Today this domain is a thin shim: four HTTP connectors (PolyHaven/AmbientCG/Sketchfab/Fab) that pipe files through one undifferentiated python_run AssetImportTask loop (save=False, no options, no read-back of what was created), plus a single-file C++ asset_import/asset_duplicate and the project asset_retriever index. The author loop is invisible: you cannot say "import this FBX with lightmap UVs, dry-run first, then verify tri counts and save." Strategy: (1) replace the dumb import with outcome-verb Interchange wrappers (mesh/texture/HDRI/USD/Datasmith/folder) carrying real options plus a dry_run->commit and read-back pair, all python_run-backed so they ship with zero plugin rebuilds; (2) collapse the four connectors behind unified asset_source_search/download verbs and add the cross-DCC moat -- async AI-3D/texture generation (generate->poll->import, stolen from Blender Hyper3D); (3) fill the unglamorous gaps competitors skip -- dependency reports, asset validation, redirector fixup, save-dirty, source-repoint reimport, paginated catalog browse, docs-as-a-tool. Every write tool is transaction-tagged and Plan-Mode-gated with structured outputs + machine error codes so the agent never guesses whether an import silently SyntaxError'd.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `asset_import_mesh` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (refactor shared.ts importIntoUe to carry pipeline options + return manifest) | GenOrca/Flop have FBX import; net-new = options-rich Interchange + structured created-asset manifest | Import an FBX/OBJ/glTF/Alembic static or skeletal mesh with real Interchange options (combine meshes, lightmap UVs, import materials/textures, LOD group, build nanite) -- the outcome verb that replaces the optionless single-file asset_import. — `(source_file, dest_path, mesh_type='static'\|'skeletal', combine_meshes=true, generate_lightmap_uvs=false, import_materials=true, import_textures=true, build_nanite=false, lod_group?, replace_existing=true) -> {created_assets[{path,class,tris,material_slots}], skipped[], verified, dest_path}` |
| `asset_import_texture` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca has texture import; net-new = first-class compression/flip-green/VT flags | Import a texture (PNG/TGA/EXR/PSD) with sRGB, compression setting, virtual-texturing, flip-green-channel, and address mode -- so normal maps and masks land correct without a python detour. — `(source_file, dest_path, srgb?, compression='default'\|'normalmap'\|'masks'\|'hdr', flip_green=false, virtual_texture=false, replace_existing=true) -> {path, width, height, format, srgb, compression, verified}` |
| `asset_import_hdri` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | PolyHaven connector downloads HDRIs but no first-class HDRI import verb -- net-new | Import an .hdr/.exr as a long-lat or cubemap and optionally wire it to a SkyLight/skybox -- one outcome verb for lighting-from-image instead of texture+manual setup. — `(source_file, dest_path, as='cubemap'\|'longlat', apply_to_skylight=false, intensity?) -> {texture_path, skylight_actor?, verified}` |
| `asset_import_usd` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (requires USDImporter plugin; probe first) | net-new moat (Blender DCC peer has USD; no UE MCP competitor does) | Import a USD/USDZ stage either as a live USD Stage Actor or flattened to UE assets -- covers the Blender/Houdini USD interop story no UE MCP competitor has. — `(source_file, dest_path, mode='stage_actor'\|'to_assets', materials='import'\|'ignore', merge_meshes=false) -> {mode, actors_or_assets[], prim_count, verified}` |
| `asset_import_datasmith` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (requires DatasmithImporter; returns not_callable if absent) | net-new moat | Import a .udatasmith / CAD scene via the Datasmith importer -- archviz/CAD ingestion that is entirely absent from every competitor. — `(source_file, dest_path, import_metadata=true, collision='none'\|'auto') -> {root_actor?, created_assets[], verified, plugin_available}` |
| `asset_import_folder` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | batch-import is net-new among UE MCPs; matches Roblox mass_* ergonomics | Batch-import an entire local folder through one pipeline, one AssetImportTask per file (UE5.7-safe), returning a paginated per-file outcome so one bad file never aborts the batch. — `(source_dir, dest_path, recursive=true, file_types?[], limit=50, offset=0) -> {imported[], failed[{file,error_code}], has_more, next_offset, total}` |
| `asset_import_dry_run` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | Flop has bp_dry_run; net-new for import side | Parse a source file and report exactly what WOULD be created (meshes, material slots, textures, skeleton, animations, LODs) without writing anything -- the dry_run->commit differentiator generalized beyond world_generate. — `(source_file, mesh_type?) -> {would_create[{name,class}], material_count, texture_count, has_skeleton, anim_count, lod_count, warnings[]}` |
| `asset_import_inspect_source` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new (inspection-first pattern, Flop *_inspect) | Read a source file's header/metadata (FBX axis+units+scene graph, glTF node/mesh counts, USD layers/prims) without importing -- inspection-first probe so the agent picks correct options before committing. — `(source_file) -> {format, up_axis?, units?, scene_nodes, mesh_count, material_names[], embedded_textures, bone_count?}` |
| `asset_import_verify` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (uses AssetVerifier + describe_assets) | net-new moat (read-back/verify; extends verifyAndMarkDelta) | Read-back after import: confirm the assets exist in the registry and report mesh stats (tris/vertices/material slots/bounds) or texture dims, closing the silent-success hole where python_run not throwing != import landed. — `(dest_path_or_asset, expect_count?) -> {found[{path,class,stats}], missing[], verified, reason?}` |
| `asset_save` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (EditorAssetSubsystem.save_loaded_assets) | net-new (critical: imports default save=False) | Explicitly save dirty assets (or a named set) after import -- a separate idempotent step because save=False on a fresh import burst is load-bearing crash-avoidance and must never be auto-chained. — `(paths?[], only_dirty=true) -> {saved[], failed[], count}` |
| `asset_reimport` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca has reimport | Reimport an existing asset from its remembered source, or from a replacement file -- standard authoring loop when the DCC file changes. — `(asset_path, new_source_file?) -> {asset_path, reimported, source_file, verified}` |
| `asset_set_source` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AssetImportData reflection) | net-new | Repoint an asset's reimport source-file path without reimporting -- fixes broken source links after moving project files. — `(asset_path, source_file) -> {asset_path, old_source?, new_source, ok}` |
| `asset_dependency_report` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry get_dependencies/get_referencers) | net-new moat (no UE MCP has dependency introspection) | Report an asset's hard/soft dependencies and referencers from the asset registry -- the unglamorous safety read that tells you what an import dragged in and what a delete would break. — `(asset_path, direction='dependencies'\|'referencers'\|'both', recursive=false, limit=50, offset=0) -> {dependencies[], referencers[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `asset_validate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Houdini graph-intelligence validate / Flop auto-repair -- net-new for imports | Run UE's EditorValidatorSubsystem on imported assets (missing material refs, no lightmap UVs, nanite eligibility, broken texture refs) and return structured issues -- validate-after-mutate for the import path. — `(paths[], fix_safe=false) -> {results[{path,errors[],warnings[]}], passed, failed}` |
| `asset_catalog_browse` | P1 | python_run-backed TS / asset_retriever | Unity paginated hierarchy; extends asset_retriever browse | Paginated browse of a content directory tree with class/name/tag filters -- the inspection surface for finding what already exists before re-importing duplicates. — `(path='/Game', class_filter?, name_contains?, recursive=false, limit=50, offset=0) -> {entries[{path,class,disk_size}], folders[], has_more, next_offset, total}` |
| `asset_rename` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (EditorAssetSubsystem.rename_asset) | Roblox rename_object; net-new for UE MCP | Rename or move an asset, leaving a redirector by default -- Plan-Mode-gated, transaction-wrapped destructive lifecycle op. — `(asset_path, new_path, leave_redirector=true) -> {old_path, new_path, redirector_left, ok}` |
| `asset_fixup_redirectors` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AssetToolsHelpers.fixup_referencers) | net-new moat | Find and fix up redirectors under a path so cooks and references stay clean after rename/move -- the cleanup step competitors omit and that silently rots projects. — `(path='/Game', dry_run=false) -> {redirectors_found, fixed[], remaining}` |
| `asset_duplicate` | P2 | C++ FHaybaMCPAssetHandler (thin TS wrapper, no rebuild) | GenOrca has duplicate; surfacing existing C++ command | Duplicate an asset to a new path -- thin first-class wrapper over the existing but unwrapped C++ asset_duplicate command (Theme 2 debt surfacing). — `(source_path, dest_path) -> {path}` |
| `asset_thumbnail` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (returns MIME image block) | Blender/Roblox get_asset_thumbnail; ties into vision-loop moat | Generate and return a thumbnail PNG image block for an imported asset -- visual read-back so the agent can confirm an import looks right, not just that it exists. — `(asset_path, size=256) -> ImageContentBlock + {asset_path, generated}` |
| `asset_import_pipeline_preset` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Create/update a reusable named Interchange pipeline preset (default scale, lightmap, material policy) so repeated imports stay consistent and token-cheap. — `(preset_name, settings{...}) -> {preset_path, settings, ok}` |
| `asset_source_search` | P0 | TS (dispatch to existing connector search modules) | net-new (unifies existing 4 connectors; token-economy) | Unified paginated search across all configured external connectors (PolyHaven/AmbientCG/Sketchfab/Fab) behind one outcome verb -- keeps the eager surface small instead of exposing four search tools. — `(query, source='all'\|'polyhaven'\|'ambientcg'\|'sketchfab'\|'fab', asset_kind?='texture'\|'model'\|'hdri', limit=20, offset=0) -> {results[{source,id,name,kind,preview_url}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `asset_source_download` | P0 | TS (dispatch to existing connector download modules) | net-new (unifies existing connectors + verifyAndMarkDelta) | Unified download+import of an external asset by {source,id}, routing to the right connector and running the verified import pipeline -- one verb instead of four download tools. — `(source, id, resolution?, flavour?, dest_path?) -> {cache_path, files[], imported, import_game_path, verified, verify_reason?}` |
| `asset_source_providers` | P1 | TS + python_run probe | net-new (DCC get_*_status gating pattern) | Introspection/status probe: which connectors have tokens configured, which import plugins (USD/Datasmith/Interchange) are available, and which AI-gen providers have keys -- the get_*_status gate before attempting anything. — `() -> {connectors[{name,configured}], import_plugins[{name,available}], ai_providers[{name,configured}]}` |
| `asset_gen_3d_create` | P1 | HTTP/TS (provider key via connector secret store) | Blender Hyper3D/Hunyuan -- net-new for UE MCP | Submit a text or image prompt to an AI 3D-mesh provider (Meshy/Tripo/Rodin/Hyper3D) and return a job handle -- the cross-DCC moat stolen from Blender's generate->poll->import. — `(prompt?, image_url?, provider='meshy'\|'tripo'\|'rodin', mode='text_to_3d'\|'image_to_3d', quality?) -> {job_id, provider, status:'queued'}` |
| `asset_gen_3d_status` | P1 | HTTP/TS | Blender DCC async pattern -- net-new | Poll an AI 3D-gen job without blocking the game thread -- the async middle step that makes minutes-long generation safe. — `(job_id, provider) -> {job_id, status:'queued'\|'running'\|'done'\|'failed', progress?, download_url?, error?}` |
| `asset_gen_3d_import` | P1 | HTTP/PY (download + python_run import) | Blender DCC -- net-new for UE MCP | When a 3D-gen job is done, download the result and run it through the verified import pipeline with scale/pivot normalization -- closes the generate->poll->import loop into UE. — `(job_id, provider, dest_path?, normalize_scale=true, recenter_pivot=true) -> {imported, import_game_path, created_assets[], verified}` |
| `asset_gen_texture_create` | P2 | HTTP/PY | net-new moat | Generate a PBR/material texture set from a text prompt via an AI texture provider and import it as a material-ready texture group -- extends the AI-gen moat to surfacing. — `(prompt, maps?=['basecolor','normal','roughness'], resolution='1k'\|'2k', dest_path?) -> {job_id?, imported?, textures[], verified?}` |
| `asset_source_docs` | P2 | TS (BM25/embedding index over bundled docs) | Houdini/remiphilippe docs-as-a-tool -- net-new for UE MCP | Docs-as-a-tool: indexed search over UE import API + connector/provider docs so the agent looks up correct Interchange/USD/Datasmith options instead of guessing -- also closes the postmortem 'no reflection-introspection' gap. — `(query, scope='import_api'\|'connectors'\|'all', limit=5) -> {snippets[{title,source,text}]}` |
| `asset_batch_relocate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox mass ops -- net-new for UE MCP | Move many assets to a new folder in one transaction with optional redirector fixup -- batch lifecycle outcome verb, Plan-Mode-gated. — `(asset_paths[], dest_folder, fixup_redirectors=true) -> {moved[], failed[], redirectors_fixed}` |
| `asset_import_diff` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (before/after diff) | Capture the registry before/after an import and report exactly which assets were created, modified, or replaced -- before/after verification that turns 'import seemed to work' into a precise manifest. — `(dest_path, since_snapshot_id?) -> {created[], modified[], replaced[], snapshot_id}` |

**Dependencies:** python_run handler (hardened SEH + 3-tier security gate) -- execution vehicle for ~24 of these tools; no plugin rebuild; asset-sources/shared.ts (importIntoUe, verifyAndMarkDelta, DAG sink, cachePathFor, downloadToFile/extractZip) -- refactor importIntoUe to take Interchange pipeline options and return a created-asset manifest instead of a log string; asset_retriever index (browse/search/reindex) + AssetVerifier -- reused by verify/catalog/dependency tools and delta-marked after each import; Transactions/undo domain (Theme 4C) -- asset_import_* emit FScopedTransaction tags so a multi-file burst collapses to one Ctrl+Z; this domain consumes that seam; Add-a-tool refactor (Theme 1) descriptor + pyTemplate factory -- the vehicle that lets these ~30 ship as data-declared tools; Interchange/USD/Datasmith editor plugins must be enabled in the target .uproject (USDImporter, DatasmithImporter, InterchangeEditor); Existing connector secret storage (Project Settings -> Hayba MCP -> Asset Connectors) + env fallback (getTokenWithEnvFallback) for new AI-gen provider keys; Vision-feedback domain (4B) for asset_thumbnail image-block return; PLUMB/validator hooks for validate-before/after on destructive rename/relocate

**Risks:** save=False on import is load-bearing: inline save on a fresh import burst has crashed UE in practice; asset_save must be a separate, explicit, idempotent step never auto-chained into import; UE5.7 chokes on one AssetImportTask with many filenames (per shared.ts comment) -- folder/batch import must stay one-task-per-file and report per-file outcomes, not fail the whole batch; Interchange pipeline options surface differs across UE 5.6/5.7/5.8; python_run reflection of pipeline assets is brittle -- pin to documented InterchangeGenericAssetsPipeline fields and degrade gracefully (flag unsupported options, do not error); asset_rename/relocate generate redirectors; leaving them creates dangling refs and broken cooks -- rename must offer fixup_redirectors and be Plan-Mode-gated + transaction-wrapped; AI-3D-gen providers (Meshy/Tripo/Rodin/Hyper3D) are paid, rate-limited, and return varying topology/scale -- jobs can take minutes; must be async (create/status/import) and never block the game thread; imported gen meshes need scale/pivot normalization; Datasmith/CAD import depends on optional plugins not in every project -- probe availability first (providers/status) and return a not_callable flag rather than a hard crash (honesty invariant); Silent-success hole: python_run not throwing != import landed (it can SyntaxError into a logged no-op) -- every import MUST round-trip through verifyAndMarkDelta + registry read-back before claiming imported:true; Large mesh/texture imports can exceed the 1MB frame cap and executor timeouts -- return job-style handles for heavy folder imports, align timeouts to UE ceilings; USD Stage Actor vs USD-to-assets are different outcomes; conflating them confuses agents -- expose mode enum explicitly

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation against a running UE 5.7 project with Interchange/USD/Datasmith plugins enabled, driven through hayba_invoke + python_run. (1) Connectivity: hayba_check_ue_status green, asset_source_providers reports which connector tokens + import plugins are present. (2) Core import happy path: asset_import_dry_run on a known FBX (verify reported meshes/materials/textures match), then asset_import_mesh with generate_lightmap_uvs=true, then asset_import_verify reads back tri count/material slots/registry path; confirm asset_save persists (.uasset on disk, dirty flag cleared). (3) Texture/HDRI: import a PNG (sRGB on) and an .hdr; verify compression settings + that HDRI drives a SkyLight via render_camera/editor_capture_viewport screenshot. (4) USD/Datasmith: import a small .usd stage (both stage-actor and to-assets modes) and a .udatasmith; assert prim/actor counts; on a project lacking the plugin assert not_callable rather than crash. (5) Folder/batch: point at a 10-file folder, assert one-task-per-file, paginated per-file outcome report, and that a single corrupt file does not abort the rest. (6) Sources + AI-gen: asset_source_search 'rock' across providers (paginated), asset_source_download one PBR set and confirm verify; asset_gen_3d_create a text prompt -> poll asset_gen_3d_status to completion -> asset_gen_3d_import and screenshot. (7) Lifecycle/unglamorous: asset_rename then asset_fixup_redirectors (grep registry for redirectors=0), asset_dependency_report on an imported mesh lists its material+textures, asset_validate flags a missing-lightmap-UV mesh, asset_duplicate round-trips. (8) Safety: every destructive tool refused without bPlanApproved; confirm one import burst is a single Ctrl+Z; confirm imported asset survives editor restart. (9) Failure shaping: feed a nonexistent source_file and a path-mismatch dest, assert machine error codes (source_not_found/import_failed/not_in_registry) not stack traces. Capture before/after via editor_capture_viewport and asset_thumbnail.


<a id="domain-16"></a>
## 16. editor-introspection-and-observability

*41 tools — P0:12 P1:19 P2:9 P3:1*

This domain is the agent's eyes inside the editor: it closes the postmortem's "no UE reflection-introspection tool" gap (which forces ~90% of sessions into hand-rolled python guessing class/property names) and productizes Hayba's crash-resilience and journal moats as readable health/observability probes. Strategy: lead with a full reflection layer (class/property/function/enum/struct/subclass discovery) so agents stop guessing, pair every write-domain mutation with a read-back/diff/verify tool (inspection-first invariant), and ship cheap gating probes (editor_state, log_watch_errors, wait_for_compile, mcp_health) that make unattended loops safe. Almost everything is python_run-backed pyTemplate descriptors (zero plugin rebuild) over UnrealEditor-scripting + AssetRegistry + the reflection system; only the live C++ command-table dump and the hot console/perf paths touch the plugin. We out-cover GenOrca/Roblox on introspection breadth while adding diff/verify/journal depth none of them have, and we speak docs-as-a-tool to match Houdini/remiphilippe.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reflect_class` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate, domain=introspect) | Roblox get_class_info (shallow); net-new moat for UE reflection depth | Describe a UClass: superclass chain, class flags, interfaces, default subobjects/components, and key CDO defaults — so agents stop guessing class shapes before spawning/editing. — `(class_path, include_inherited=true) -> {class, super_chain, flags, interfaces, components, cdo_summary}` |
| `reflect_properties` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (no UE MCP exposes reflected property schemas) | List a class/object's reflected UPROPERTYs with type, category, edit/BP flags, and metadata, filtered+paginated — the single biggest cure for the python-guessing loop. — `(class_or_object, filter?, editable_only?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {properties[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `reflect_search_types` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Fuzzy-search the whole type system (native + asset classes, structs, enums) by name/keyword — turns 'what's the class called?' from a python fishing trip into one call. — `(query, kinds=['class','struct','enum'], limit=30) -> {matches[], has_more}` |
| `reflect_functions` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | chongdashu BP-graph (partial); net-new for call-surface listing | List a class's UFUNCTIONs (BlueprintCallable/Pure/Event) with params, return type, and flags, paginated — needed before any BP-graph or call-function authoring. — `(class_path, callable_only=true, limit=50, offset=0) -> {functions[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `reflect_subclasses` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Enumerate concrete subclasses of a base (e.g. all Actor types, all spawnable components) so spawn/add-component tools can offer real, valid choices. — `(base_class, spawnable_only=false, limit=100, offset=0) -> {subclasses[], has_more}` |
| `reflect_default_object` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Dump the CDO property values for a class (what a fresh instance looks like) — the baseline for object_diff and for predicting spawn results. — `(class_path, filter?, limit=80, offset=0) -> {defaults{}, has_more}` |
| `reflect_enum` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Enumerate a UEnum's entries with raw values + display names — lets agents pass valid enum literals instead of guessing ints. — `(enum_path) -> {entries[{name,value,display}]}` |
| `reflect_struct` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Describe a UStruct/USTRUCT's fields with types and defaults — needed to construct struct-typed property values correctly. — `(struct_path) -> {fields[{name,type,default}]}` |
| `object_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox get_instance_properties | Read the full property tree of a live object/actor (by id or path) with depth, filter, and pagination — the universal read-back primitive paired with every write op. — `(target, depth=1, filter?, limit=80, offset=0) -> {object, properties{}, has_more}` |
| `object_get_property` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox get_attribute; net-new for dotted UE paths | Read one property by dotted/indexed path (e.g. RootComponent.RelativeLocation.X) with typed value — surgical verify after a single mutation. — `(target, property_path) -> {value, type}` |
| `object_diff` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox compare_instances | Property-by-property diff of two objects, or object vs its CDO — the before/after verify that proves a mutation did exactly what was asked and nothing else. — `(target_a, target_b='@cdo', filter?) -> {changed[{path,a,b}], same_count}` |
| `asset_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox get_asset_details | AssetRegistry metadata for one asset: class, registry tags, disk size, dirty flag, dependency/referencer counts — read before edit/delete to avoid surprises. — `(asset_path) -> {class, tags{}, disk_size, dirty, dep_count, ref_count}` |
| `asset_registry_query` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Blender asset libs / GenOrca; net-new for tag queries | Query the AssetRegistry by class/path-prefix/tag, paginated — discovery surface for 'what assets exist that I can use here'. — `(class_filter?, path_prefix?, tag_filter?, recursive=true, limit=50, offset=0) -> {assets[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `asset_dependencies` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Walk the dependency or referencer graph of an asset to a given depth — prevents breaking refs before delete/rename and supports impact analysis. — `(asset_path, direction='deps'\|'refs', depth=1, limit=100, offset=0) -> {edges[], has_more}` |
| `asset_verify_exists` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (read-back/verify) | Batch existence + loadability check for a list of asset paths — the cheap gating read that confirms generated/imported assets actually landed. — `(paths[]) -> {results[{path,exists,loadable}], missing_count}` |
| `outliner_tree` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/SceneGraphHandler (extend scene_export) + TS wrapper | Unity paginated hierarchy / Roblox get_descendants | Paginated world outliner: actors with label, class, folder, and attachment parent/children — the agent's map of the level without dumping the whole world. — `(folder?, class_filter?, name_filter?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {nodes[], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `actor_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Flop *_inspect pattern; Roblox get_instance_properties | Full read-back of one actor: transform, components, material slots, tags, layers, data-layers — the inspection-first pair for the actor write domain. — `(actor_id) -> {transform, components[], materials[], tags[], layers[]}` |
| `actor_components` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | List an actor's components with class, attach parent, and a few key props, paginated — needed before component-level edits or transform fixes. — `(actor_id, limit=50, offset=0) -> {components[], has_more}` |
| `selection_get` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox get_selection | Return the current editor selection (level actors, selected components, content-browser assets) — context probe so 'apply to selection' tools know their target. — `() -> {actors[], components[], assets[]}` |
| `scene_stats` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/SceneGraphHandler (mesh_audit) + TS wrapper | net-new moat (extends mesh_audit) | Aggregate scene budget: actor counts by class, total/instanced primitives, triangle estimate, missing-LOD hotspots — observability for perf-driven worldbuilding. — `(group_by='class') -> {total_actors, by_class{}, triangles_est, instanced, hotspots[]}` |
| `editor_state` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat (gating) | One-shot consolidated editor status: active mode, current map, PIE/play-world state, blocking-modal flag, dirty-package count — the master gating probe before any action loop. — `() -> {mode, map, pie_running, play_world, modal_blocking, dirty_packages}` |
| `dirty_packages` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (verify) | List unsaved/dirty packages and assets — verify what would be written before a save/build/cook, and confirm a save actually cleaned them. — `(limit=50, offset=0) -> {packages[], count, has_more}` |
| `viewport_state` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/EditorHandler (extend editor_set_camera readback) + TS wrapper | net-new | Read the active level viewport: camera transform, FOV, view mode, realtime flag, and enabled show-flags — context for capture/render tools and for reproducing a view. — `() -> {camera, fov, view_mode, realtime, show_flags{}}` |
| `level_info` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) / WorldPartitionHandler readback | net-new moat | Current persistent level plus sublevels, streaming/data-layer state, and World Partition loaded-cell summary — observability for WP/data-layer worldbuilding. — `() -> {persistent_level, sublevels[], data_layers[], wp_cells_loaded}` |
| `log_tail` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/EditorHandler (editor_get_output_log) + TS wrapper | Roblox get_output_log; wraps editor_get_output_log | Tail recent editor log lines with severity + category filter and grep, cursor-paginated — the core observability read for diagnosing what the editor just did. — `(severity?, category?, grep?, since_line?, limit=100) -> {lines[], next_line}` |
| `log_watch_errors` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) over editor_get_output_log | net-new moat (unattended-loop safety) | Return only Warnings/Errors emitted since a cursor — the cheap poll an unattended agent runs after each mutation to catch silent failures without paging full logs. — `(since_line?, min_severity='warning') -> {events[], next_line, error_count, warning_count}` |
| `message_log_get` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Read paginated pages of the UE Message Log (PIE, Compiler, MapCheck, AssetCheck) — structured editor diagnostics that never reach the raw output log. — `(channel, limit=50, offset=0) -> {messages[], has_more}` |
| `compile_errors` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Aura self-correcting compile (read side) | Return the last Blueprint/Material/shader compile errors with asset + node/pin context — feeds the self-correcting authoring loop Aura markets. — `(scope='all'\|'blueprint'\|'material'\|'shader', limit=30) -> {errors[{asset,node,message}], has_more}` |
| `wait_for_compile` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) + idle-handler poll | net-new (mirrors wait_for_shaders/wait_for_idle) | Block (job-envelope, polled) until pending Blueprint/material/shader compilation settles, then report success/error counts — gating so reads don't race a half-compiled asset. — `(scope='all', timeout_s=120) -> {settled, errors, warnings}` |
| `perf_stats` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/PerfHandler (editor_get_perf_stats) + TS wrapper | remiphilippe visual/perf tests; wraps editor_get_perf_stats | Snapshot FPS, frame/game/render/GPU ms, draw calls, primitives, and memory — observability that lets the agent catch perf regressions it introduced. — `(deep=false) -> {fps, frame_ms, game_ms, render_ms, gpu_ms, draw_calls, primitives, memory_mb}` |
| `memory_report` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) over console memreport | net-new moat | Engine memory breakdown: UObject count, GC stats, texture/mesh/RT pool sizes (parsed memreport) — diagnoses the leaks and bloat that crash long sessions. — `() -> {uobject_count, gc_ms, texture_mb, mesh_mb, rt_mb, total_mb}` |
| `stat_command` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Toggle and read back a 'stat' group (unit/fps/gpu/rhi/streaming), returning parsed values rather than just flipping the on-screen overlay. — `(group, enable=true) -> {group, values{}}` |
| `gpu_profile` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Capture a single-frame GPU timing breakdown (profilegpu) parsed into named passes with ms — deep perf observability for render-heavy scenes. — `() -> {total_ms, passes[{name,ms}]}` |
| `cvar_get` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Read a console variable's current value, default, and flags — diagnostics and gating (e.g. confirm Lumen/Nanite cvars are in the expected state). — `(name) -> {name, value, default, flags}` |
| `cvar_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new (with verify) | Set a console variable and read back the applied value — verified config change instead of a fire-and-forget console command. — `(name, value) -> {name, requested, applied}` |
| `cvar_list` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new | Search/list console variables by prefix with help text, paginated — discovery so agents find the right knob instead of guessing cvar names. — `(prefix?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {cvars[{name,value,help}], has_more}` |
| `console_exec` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/EditorHandler (editor_run_console_command) + TS wrapper | runreal/remiphilippe console; wraps editor_run_console_command | Run an arbitrary console command and capture the log lines it produced in-window — observable console execution (vs blind editor_run_console_command). — `(command, capture_log=true) -> {executed, output_lines[]}` |
| `undo_history` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | Roblox undo/redo (read side); net-new for UE | Read the editor transaction (undo/redo) stack — labels and current index — so the agent can see what a Ctrl+Z would revert before invoking the transaction domain. — `(limit=30) -> {undo_stack[], redo_stack[], current_index}` |
| `mcp_health` | P0 | TS (extends check-ue-status.ts; no plugin call beyond ping) | net-new moat (extends check_ue_status) | Consolidated server+plugin health: TCP connect, plugin/protocol version, sidecar status, idle/shader state, in-flight command count — surfaces the crash-resilience moat as one probe. — `() -> {connected, plugin_version, sidecar, idle, shaders_ready, inflight}` |
| `mcp_describe_commands` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit/CommandHandler (new mcp_describe_commands) + TS wrapper | net-new moat (Section 5 #5) | Enumerate the running plugin's live C++ command table with param schemas and destructive flags — closes field-level schema drift and makes the sidecar generatable from the editor. — `(domain?, limit=100, offset=0) -> {commands[{name,params[],destructive}], has_more}` |
| `action_journal` | P1 | TS (wraps dag/journal-tail.ts + dag/status.ts) | net-new moat; wraps journal_tail/dag_status | Query the MCP action journal/DAG: recent tool calls with args, result status, duration, and errors, paginated — observability of what the agent itself did, productizing the journal moat. — `(status_filter?, tool_filter?, limit=30, offset=0) -> {entries[], has_more, next_offset}` |

**Dependencies:** python_run handler (SEH-guarded) — backs ~32 of these descriptors with zero plugin rebuild; reflection/AssetRegistry/EditorActorSubsystem all run through it; Add-a-tool refactor (Theme 1): single ToolDescriptor with pyTemplate factory so these land as data, not 7-file churn; sidecar generated from descriptors; Code-Mode / deferred routing + packs: all 40 land behind the introspect pack so they never bloat the eager surface; Existing C++ handlers to extend (no new module needed for most): EditorHandler (log/console/camera readback), PerfHandler (perf_stats), SceneGraphHandler (outliner_tree/scene_stats), CommandHandler (new mcp_describe_commands); Existing TS to wrap: check-ue-status.ts (mcp_health), dag/journal-tail.ts + dag/status.ts (action_journal), wait_for_idle/wait_for_shaders (wait_for_compile pattern); Pagination + error-envelope conventions (limit/has_more/next_offset, machine codes) from the global design rules; DAG/journal store + hayba-memory.db for action_journal; AssetRegistryModule and the UE reflection system (UClass/FProperty/UFunction/UEnum/UStruct iteration) available in-editor

**Risks:** Reflection iteration over huge classes/worlds can blow the 1MB TCP frame cap and waste context — every list tool MUST paginate hard (default 50) and filter server-side, not client-side; CDO access and force-loading assets during reflect_default_object / asset_registry_query can trigger sync loads or GC stalls on the game thread; keep reads shallow and prefer AssetRegistry tags over loading the asset; Property values that are object pointers/structs/arrays serialize poorly — need a typed, bounded value encoder with depth limits or object_inspect returns unreadable blobs; memreport/profilegpu/stat parsing is brittle across UE versions (pinned 5.7.0) — string-scraping console output may break on 5.8; flag gpu_profile/memory_report as best-effort; console_exec output capture races the async log sink; may miss lines emitted after the call returns — needs a short drain window or cursor read via log_tail; mcp_describe_commands requires a C++ rebuild and an honest command table; if it drifts from reality it reintroduces the false-advertising problem it was meant to kill; Some 'read' tools (cvar_set, stat_command, console_exec) are actually mutating and must respect Plan-Mode / not be classified as alwaysOn read-only despite living in this domain; editor_state / message_log_get during PIE or a blocking modal may itself hang the game thread — must go through the RunSync seam and short timeouts, never AsyncTask+Wait

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation against a running UnrealEditor with HaybaMCPToolkit on the pinned 5.7 project: (1) Reflection — call reflect_search_types('StaticMeshActor'), then reflect_class / reflect_properties / reflect_functions on the result; assert StaticMeshComponent appears and property types/flags match the editor Details panel; reflect_enum on a known UEnum (e.g. ECollisionEnabled) and check entries. (2) Round-trip verify — spawn a StaticMeshActor (actor domain), object_inspect it, mutate RelativeLocation, object_get_property to confirm the new X, object_diff vs '@cdo' to confirm exactly one changed field. (3) Asset — asset_registry_query for /Game StaticMesh, asset_inspect one result, asset_dependencies(direction='refs'), asset_verify_exists on a mix of real+fake paths; confirm missing_count is right. (4) Scene/state — outliner_tree paginated (verify has_more/next_offset stitch a full list), selection_get after selecting actors in the viewport, editor_state before/after entering PIE and with a modal open, dirty_packages before+after a save. (5) Logs/diag — emit a known UE_LOG via console_exec, then log_tail(grep=...) and log_watch_errors(since_line) to confirm cursor advance; force a deliberate BP compile error and check compile_errors + message_log_get('Compiler'). (6) Perf/cvar — perf_stats sanity (fps>0, draw_calls>0), cvar_set('r.ScreenPercentage',50) then cvar_get to confirm applied==50, stat_command('unit'). (7) Meta — mcp_health asserts connected+plugin_version, action_journal shows the calls just made with durations, mcp_describe_commands lists the introspect commands with destructive flags; undo_history reflects the spawn/mutate transactions. Pagination, 1MB-frame, and timeout behavior checked by running outliner_tree/asset_registry_query on a large World Partition map. Each tool also gets a disconnected-editor run to confirm graceful error envelopes (machine codes, no hang).


<a id="domain-17"></a>
## 17. gameplay-pie-and-testing

*35 tools — P0:11 P1:11 P2:11 P3:2*

Today this domain is 8 thin commands (start/stop PIE, 4 pie_* runtime primitives, 3 test_* commands) carrying a P0 freeze: test_run blocks the game thread up to 120s/test and PIE wait-loops pump the ticker synchronously. Strategy is three-layered. (1) THEME-0 fix: convert test_run and long PIE waits to the job-envelope pattern (start -> poll) so the whole domain is non-blocking and unattended-safe, a reliability moat nobody documents. (2) Close parity fast by surfacing ~25 missing runtime-control verbs (input action/axis/mouse, possess/teleport/spawn, line-trace/overlap, stats/screenshot-diff) mostly as python_run wrappers with zero plugin rebuild. (3) Own the high ground with outcome verbs (pie_run_scenario drives a whole playtest with assertions + before/after vision diff in one journaled call) and declarative dry-run test plans validated through PLUMB. Every write tool ships a paired read/inspect tool (pie_status, pie_get_player_state, pie_get_actors) per the inspection-first rule.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pie_start` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPEditorHandler (wrap editor_start_pie) + job envelope in HaybaMCPToolkit | Epic/Flop/remiphilippe have PIE start; net-new = non-blocking job envelope | Start Play-In-Editor as a non-blocking job and return a session_id; entry point for every gameplay test and the fix for unattended use. — `(mode='play'\|'simulate', num_players?, dedicated_server?, start_actor_path?, single_step?) -> {session_id, started, world}` |
| `pie_stop` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPEditorHandler (wrap editor_stop_pie) | Epic/Flop | Stop the active PIE session and return a teardown summary (errors scraped, duration, frames); safe teardown competitors omit. — `() -> {stopped, duration_s, error_count, frames}` |
| `pie_status` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (most expose no PIE introspection) | Read PIE state without mutating: running/paused, world type, num_players, elapsed, current map; inspection-first probe before any action. — `() -> {running, paused, mode, world, num_players, elapsed_s, map}` |
| `pie_job_poll` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit job envelope | net-new moat (async/non-blocking) | Poll a long-running PIE/test job (started, running, done, failed) and fetch results when complete; the read half of the job envelope that ends game-thread freezes. — `(job_id, wait_ms?) -> {status, progress, result?, error?}` |
| `test_run_async` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPTestHandler (job-envelope conversion of test_run) | remiphilippe/Flop run tests; net-new = non-blocking | Run automation tests as a polled job instead of blocking the game thread; the THEME-0 fix that makes the test runner production-safe. — `(test_names[]\|'all', smoke_only?, per_test_timeout_s?) -> {job_id}` |
| `test_list` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPTestHandler (wrap existing) | remiphilippe/GenOrca | List discovered automation tests with category/path/source filters, paginated; the inspect tool for the test runner. — `(filter_pattern?, category?, limit?, offset?) -> {tests[], count, total, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `test_get_log` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPTestHandler (wrap existing) | Flop pie_test_bp assertions / Roblox get_playtest_output | Return per-test errors/warnings/duration from the last run; the read-back that turns a pass/fail into actionable diagnostics. — `() -> {tests:[{name,success,duration_s,errors[],warnings[]}], count}` |
| `pie_assert_property` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler (wrap editor_pie_assert) | Flop pie_test_bp / GenOrca | Assert a reflected actor property equals an expected value (with tolerance), waiting up to a timeout; core gameplay correctness check. — `(actor_path\|actor_label, property_name, expected_value, tolerance?, timeout_ms?) -> {matched, observed_value, frame, elapsed_ms}` |
| `pie_wait_for` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler (wrap editor_pie_wait_for) | remiphilippe player control; net-new comparator set | Wait until an actor property satisfies a comparator (eq/neq/gt/lt/gte/lte) or timeout; the async-condition primitive for event-driven tests. — `(actor_path\|actor_label, property_name, comparator, expected_value, tolerance?, timeout_ms?) -> {matched, observed_value, frames}` |
| `pie_capture_screenshot` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler (wrap editor_pie_screenshot, return image block) | Flop/Roblox capture_screenshot; moat = image content blocks | Capture the PIE viewport and return a MIME-typed PNG content block; the runtime half of the vision feedback loop. — `(filename?, return_image?) -> {filename, captured, image_block}` |
| `pie_scrape_log` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (output log filter) | Roblox get_playtest_output / Flop; net-new BP-runtime focus | Return errors/warnings/blueprint-runtime-errors emitted during the current PIE session; turns 'it played' into a real runtime-error gate. — `(severity?='warning'\|'error', since_frame?, limit?) -> {entries[], error_count, warning_count, has_more}` |
| `pie_press_key` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler (wrap editor_pie_press_key) | remiphilippe player control / Roblox simulate_keyboard_input | Inject a key press/release/tap into the PIE viewport; lowest-level input primitive for movement and actions. — `(key, event?='pressed'\|'released'\|'pressed_and_released', held_ms?) -> {dispatched, key, event}` |
| `pie_input_action` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (intent-level input) | Trigger a named Enhanced Input action (or legacy action mapping) on the player controller; tests gameplay by intent, not raw keys. — `(action_name, value?, controller_index?) -> {dispatched, action, resolved_as}` |
| `pie_input_axis` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | remiphilippe player control | Apply a named axis input (e.g. MoveForward=1.0) for a duration; drives continuous movement deterministically. — `(axis_name, value, duration_ms?, controller_index?) -> {dispatched, axis, value, duration_ms}` |
| `pie_mouse` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox simulate_mouse_input | Move/click/drag the mouse in the PIE viewport at screen or world-projected coords; covers aim, look, and UI interaction. — `(op='move'\|'click'\|'drag', x, y, x2?, y2?, button?='left', held_ms?) -> {dispatched, op}` |
| `pie_input_sequence` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (batch input macro) | Replay an ordered macro of timed key/axis/mouse events as one call; the ergonomic vehicle for scripted playthroughs. — `(steps:[{type,name?,value?,at_ms,held_ms?}], controller_index?) -> {executed, step_count, total_ms}` |
| `pie_get_player_state` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | remiphilippe; net-new structured readback | Read the player pawn/controller: transform, velocity, possessed class, and selected gameplay props; inspection before/after input. — `(controller_index?, props?[]) -> {pawn_path, location, rotation, velocity, controller, props{}}` |
| `pie_get_actors` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Unity paginated hierarchy / Roblox get_descendants | Query actors in the PIE world by class/tag/name with transforms, paginated; the runtime hierarchy read that mirrors editor introspection. — `(class_filter?, tag?, name_contains?, limit?, offset?) -> {actors[], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `pie_spawn_actor` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | GenOrca/Flop spawn; net-new PIE-world scoping | Spawn an actor into the transient PIE world at a transform; sets up test fixtures (triggers, targets, props) at runtime. — `(class_path\|blueprint_path, location, rotation?, scale?, label?) -> {spawned, actor_path}` |
| `pie_teleport_player` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Move the player pawn to a location or onto a named actor; positions the player for a specific test without manual driving. — `(location?\|target_actor, controller_index?, snap_to_ground?) -> {teleported, new_location}` |
| `pie_possess` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Possess an existing pawn or spawn-and-possess a pawn class for the player controller; swaps the test subject mid-session. — `(pawn_path?\|pawn_class, location?, controller_index?) -> {possessed, pawn_path}` |
| `pie_line_trace` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (runtime collision query) | Cast a ray in the PIE world and return the first hit (actor/component/location/normal); verifies ground, line-of-sight, weapon hits. — `(start, end\|direction+length, channel?, complex?) -> {hit, actor_path?, location?, normal?, distance?}` |
| `pie_overlap_query` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Sphere/box overlap test at a point and return overlapping actors; confirms triggers fired and spawns landed correctly. — `(shape='sphere'\|'box', center, radius?\|extent?, channel?) -> {overlaps[], count}` |
| `pie_capture_stats` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | remiphilippe CI/visual; net-new structured stats | Snapshot runtime perf (fps, frame_ms, game/render/gpu ms, memory) from stat unit; turns PIE into a lightweight perf gate. — `(samples?, interval_ms?) -> {fps, frame_ms, game_ms, render_ms, gpu_ms, mem_mb}` |
| `pie_screenshot_diff` | P2 | python_run-backed TS + visual sidecar (pixel-diff/CLIP) | Flop pixel-diff; moat = local + CLIP + image blocks | Capture the PIE viewport and pixel-diff it against a committed baseline, returning a score plus both/diff images; closed-loop visual regression. — `(baseline_path, threshold?, region?) -> {diff_score, passed, baseline_block, actual_block, diff_block}` |
| `pie_step_frames` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler | Epic single-step (internal); net-new = exposed verb | Advance the PIE world by N frames (or seconds) while paused, for deterministic frame-exact assertions; tames real-time flakiness. — `(frames?\|seconds?) -> {advanced_frames, world_time}` |
| `pie_set_time_dilation` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Set global time dilation (slomo/fast-forward) in the PIE world for deterministic or fast test execution. — `(dilation) -> {applied, dilation}` |
| `pie_pause` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new | Pause or resume the PIE simulation so state can be inspected without time advancing; debugging companion to step_frames. — `(paused) -> {paused}` |
| `pie_console_command` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPEditorHandler (wrap editor_run_console_command, PIE-scoped) | GenOrca/runreal; standard escape | Run a console command in the PIE world (stat, show, cheats, ke); reaches gameplay debug surfaces no typed tool covers. — `(command) -> {executed, output?}` |
| `pie_watch_property` | P3 | C++ HaybaMCPPIEHandler | net-new moat | Sample an actor property every frame over a window and return the timeseries; debugs gameplay values that change over time (health drain, charge). — `(actor_path, property_name, duration_ms, sample_hz?) -> {series:[{t,value}], min, max, last}` |
| `pie_eval` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (PIE-world scoped, security-gated) | GenOrca execute_python / Roblox execute_luau | Execute scoped python inside the PIE world context for one-off probes the typed tools miss; the hardened escape hatch. — `(code, plan_approved?) -> {result, stdout, error?}` |
| `functional_test_run` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPTestHandler + job envelope | remiphilippe/Flop gauntlet; matches their DevOps loop | Load a map and run UE FunctionalTest actors (gauntlet-style in-level tests) as a polled job; covers level-driven scenarios beyond unit tests. — `(map_path, test_names?[], timeout_s?) -> {job_id}` |
| `test_plan_dry_run` | P2 | python_run-backed TS + PLUMB validator | Flop bp_dry_run; moat = quantified PLUMB validation | Validate a declarative test plan (steps + assertions) through PLUMB without executing it; the dry-run-before-commit moat applied to testing. — `(plan:{steps[],asserts[]}) -> {valid, issues[], resolved_actors[], estimated_duration_s}` |
| `pie_run_scenario` | P1 | python_run-backed TS orchestrator (composes pie_* + test tools) | Flop pie_test_bp (one-shot); moat = non-blocking + vision diff + journaled | One outcome verb that starts PIE, replays an input macro, evaluates an assertion list, captures before/after vision diff, stops, and returns a single journaled report; the bundled playtest differentiator. — `(scenario:{start,macro[],asserts[],capture?}) -> {passed, asserts:[{name,matched,observed}], screenshots[], report_id}` |
| `test_history` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (journal/DAG read) | net-new moat (journaled history) | Recall the last N test/scenario runs from the journal with pass/fail and timing; lessons-as-memory for flaky-test triage. — `(limit?, kind?='test'\|'scenario') -> {runs[], count, has_more}` |

**Dependencies:** THEME-0 job-envelope conversion (docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md) - hard prerequisite for test_run_async/pie_job_poll; THEME-1 add-a-tool refactor + pyTemplate factory for the python_run-backed wrappers; Hardened python_run handler (SEH + security gate) - vehicle for all PY tools; SEH-wrap ProcessCommand so PIE/test handler crashes are recoverable; Existing PIE world resolver (GetPIEWorld) and reflected-property read/compare in HaybaMCPPIEHandler.cpp; Vision tools (editor_capture_viewport/render_camera) + visual sidecar for screenshot-diff/CLIP; PLUMB validator for dry-run test-plan validation; Enhanced Input plugin (for pie_input_action) - fall back to legacy action/axis mappings if absent; journal + DAG for scenario/test-run history
