**Risks:** PIE runs on the game thread; any synchronous wait re-introduces the freeze - all long waits MUST go through the job envelope, not a blocking loop; PIE world is transient: spawned actors and possessed pawns vanish on stop; tools must not promise persistence and must re-resolve the world each call (GEditor->PlayWorld can be null mid-transition); Input injection differs between Enhanced Input and legacy mappings; action-name lookups can silently no-op - return a dispatched=false + reason rather than fake success; Automation-test API churn (UE 5.7 StartTestByName returns void); pin behavior and flag engine-version sensitivity; Screenshot capture currently waits on file write (PIEScreenshot TODO) - racy; prefer OnScreenshotCaptured buffer path for the diff tool; Determinism: real-time PIE makes frame-exact asserts flaky; pie_step_frames + time-dilation mitigate but can't fully fix physics jitter; pie_eval is an escape hatch inside a live game world - must respect the python security gate and Plan-Mode before mutating; Multiplayer/dedicated-server PIE spawns extra processes - resource and teardown hazards; gate behind P3

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation on a real map with a player pawn. (1) pie_start mode=play, then pie_status to confirm world/num_players; prove non-blocking by polling pie_job_poll while a long pie_wait_for is in flight. (2) Movement: pie_input_axis MoveForward 1.0 for 1s, pie_get_player_state confirms location delta; pie_input_action jump, pie_assert_property velocity.Z spike. (3) pie_spawn_actor a trigger, pie_teleport_player onto it, pie_overlap_query confirms overlap, pie_line_trace down confirms ground hit. (4) pie_capture_screenshot then pie_screenshot_diff vs committed baseline; eyeball the PNG block + diff score. (5) pie_capture_stats during movement confirms fps/frame_ms. (6) test_run_async on a known-pass + known-fail automation test, poll pie_job_poll, verify passed/failed buckets and test_get_log errors. (7) pie_run_scenario end-to-end (start->macro->asserts->diff->stop) returns one report; confirm a single failed assert marks the run failed. (8) pie_stop, then pie_status confirms torn down.


<a id="domain-18"></a>
## 18. project-build-cook-packaging

*36 tools — P0:8 P1:15 P2:11 P3:2*

This domain is where Hayba either matches remiphilippe's full headless DevOps loop or stays an authoring-only toy. The decisive move is to land the planned async-job registry (FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry + a *_job_status reader) FIRST: every long subprocess (UBT compile, cook, UAT BuildCookRun) must return a {job_id, status:"running"} envelope immediately instead of WaitFor-blocking the game thread (the two P0 editor-freeze bugs). Because python_run handlers also run on the game thread, long subprocess launches CANNOT live in python — build/cook/package/launch stay C++ behind the shared registry (this is the crash-resilience moat, productized). python_run is reserved for the no-rebuild config/inspection/validation tools (project settings, maps, asset validation, DDC, Live Coding compile). Every write tool ships a paired *_inspect read-back and honors dry-run + Plan-Mode + validate-before-cook (PLUMB-aligned) so the loop is verifiable, not fire-and-forget.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `build_job_status` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit::BuildHandler + shared FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry (TMap<FString,FJobState>+FCriticalSection) | net-new moat (Aura promises Cancel but documents no job registry; Epic/Flop are game-thread-serial) | Poll a job_id from any build/cook/package/test launch -> status, exit_code, bounded log_excerpt; the read-back half of the async-job seam that stops the editor freezing. — `(job_id, tail_bytes?=16384) -> {ok, status:'running'\|'done'\|'failed'\|'cancelled', exit_code?, log_excerpt, started_at, duration_ms}` |
| `build_job_list` | P0 | C++ BuildHandler + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | net-new moat | List active and recent jobs with status/age/op-name; lets the agent discover orphaned/running jobs after a reconnect instead of guessing job_ids. — `(state?='all'\|'running'\|'done', limit?=20, offset?=0) -> {jobs:[{job_id, op, status, started_at, duration_ms}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `build_job_cancel` | P0 | C++ BuildHandler + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry (FProcHandle terminate) | Aura (Cancel) — we make it real + local | Terminate a running build/cook/package job (kill the child process tree); the Cancel() Aura advertises but Hayba can actually back with a real PID handle. — `(job_id) -> {ok, status:'cancelled', exit_code}` |
| `build_job_tail` | P1 | C++ BuildHandler + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (CI log stream) | Offset-paginated incremental log lines for a running job, so the agent streams progress without re-fetching the whole 16KB excerpt each poll. — `(job_id, offset?=0, max_lines?=200) -> {lines:[str], next_offset, has_more, done}` |
| `build_job_wait` | P2 | C++ BuildHandler (ticker-driven; reuses idle-handler deferred pattern) | net-new moat | Cooperative non-blocking wait: returns the latest job state once status changes or a short deadline elapses (never blocks the game thread); convenience over manual polling. — `(job_id, deadline_sec?=5) -> {ok, status, exit_code?, changed:bool}` |
| `build_project` | P0 | C++ BuildHandler (UBT subprocess) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (headless build); Epic restarts to add C++ | Compile the C++ editor or game target via UnrealBuildTool; honest job-envelope rewrap of the existing freezing build_project (the P0 unfreeze). — `(target?='Editor'\|'Game', configuration?='Development'\|'DebugGame'\|'Shipping', platform?='Win64') -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `cook_content` | P0 | C++ BuildHandler (UnrealEditor-Cmd -run=cook) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (headless cook) | Cook content for a target platform (iterate/unversioned/maps), job-envelope; the honest rename of build_cook with non-freezing semantics. — `(platform?='Windows', iterate?=true, unversioned?=false, maps?=[str]) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `package_project` | P0 | C++ BuildHandler (RunUAT BuildCookRun subprocess) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (full DevOps loop) — net-new for every authoring-only competitor | Full BuildCookRun via RunUAT (build+cook+stage+pak+archive) — the headline shippable-build verb; supports dry_run to echo the resolved UAT command line before committing. — `(platform?='Win64', configuration?='Shipping', archive_dir?, build?=true, cook?=true, stage?=true, pak?=true, dry_run?=false) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'} \| {ok, dry_run:true, command_line}` |
| `project_inspect` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit::ProjectHandler (wraps project_get_info) | chongdashu/Flop (project info) — we add packaging+platform readback | Read project identity in one call: name, version, engine version, enabled plugins, target platforms, default/startup maps; the inspection-first anchor for the whole domain (surfaces tier-2 project_get_info). — `() -> {project_name, version, engine_version, plugins_enabled:int, target_platforms:[str], game_default_map, packaging_config}` |
| `test_run` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit::TestHandler + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | Flop pie_test_bp / remiphilippe (visual+CI tests) | Run automation/functional tests by filter, job-envelope; fixes the 120s/test game-thread freeze and gives the agent a pollable result instead of a frozen editor. — `(filter_pattern?, category?, timeout_sec?=600) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `test_list` | P1 | C++ TestHandler (wraps test_list) | remiphilippe / GenOrca | Enumerate discoverable automation tests with filter + category, paginated; inspection-first pair for test_run so the agent picks real test paths. — `(filter_pattern?, category?, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {tests:[{name, full_test_path, file_name, line_number}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `test_get_log` | P1 | C++ TestHandler (wraps test_get_log) | Flop (assertions readback) | Read structured results of the most recent / a given test job: per-test pass/fail, errors, warnings, durations; the read-back half of test_run. — `(job_id?, include_passing?=false) -> {summary:{passed,failed,warnings}, results:[{name, state, errors:[str], duration_ms}]}` |
| `project_validate_assets` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (UEditorValidatorSubsystem) — no rebuild | net-new moat (validate-before-cook; closest is Flop auto-repair) | Run the Editor Validation subsystem (validate_assets_with_settings) over a path before cook/package — validate-before-mutate, catches missing refs/cook-blockers cheaply; PLUMB-aligned safety gate. — `(path?='/Game', recursive?=true, fix?=false, limit?=100) -> {ok, validated:int, errors:[{asset, message}], warnings:[{asset, message}], has_more}` |
| `cook_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (filesystem scan of Saved/Cooked + AssetRegistry) — no rebuild | remiphilippe (cook status) — most competitors have none | Read cook output without re-cooking: cooked-asset count, failures, per-platform output dir, last cook timestamp/DDC stats; verify a cook actually produced content. — `(platform?='Windows') -> {ok, output_dir, cooked_assets:int, failures:[str], total_size_mb, last_cooked_at}` |
| `package_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (filesystem + pak manifest read) — no rebuild | net-new moat | Read the last packaged build: archive dir, platform, total size, .pak/.utoc manifest summary, executable path; confirms the shippable artifact exists and is sane. — `(archive_dir?) -> {ok, platform, executable, total_size_mb, pak_files:[{name, size_mb}], staged_maps:[str]}` |
| `build_artifacts_inspect` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (filesystem scan) — no rebuild | net-new moat (verify/readback) | Disk-level read-back of all build outputs (Binaries, Intermediate size, Saved/Cooked, Staged, archive) so the agent verifies state and spots stale/oversized artifacts before re-running. — `() -> {binaries:[{name, mtime}], intermediate_mb, cooked_mb, staged_mb, archives:[{path, size_mb}]}` |
| `build_target_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (Source/*.Target.cs parse + unreal.get_target_platforms) — no rebuild | net-new moat | Introspect available build targets, configurations and platforms by parsing *.Target.cs + installed platform SDKs; inspection-first so build/package params are never guessed. — `() -> {targets:[{name, type:'Editor'\|'Game'\|'Server'\|'Client'}], configurations:[str], platforms:[str]}` |
| `build_live_coding` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (console command LiveCoding.Compile) — no rebuild | Aura (live-compile) | Trigger a Live Coding (hot-reload) compile of game modules without a full rebuild — the fast iteration loop Aura sells as self-correcting live-compile, here as a one-liner console invoke. — `() -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'} (streams LiveCoding.Compile result)` |
| `build_generate_project_files` | P1 | C++ BuildHandler (RunUAT) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe | Regenerate IDE/project files via RunUAT GenerateProjectFiles, job-envelope; honest rewrap of the existing freezing command. — `() -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `package_stage` | P1 | C++ BuildHandler (RunUAT BuildCookRun -skipcook -stage) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe | Stage-only step (copy cooked content + binaries to the Staged dir) without re-cook/pak; lets the agent iterate on staging/packaging settings cheaply. — `(platform?='Win64', configuration?='Development') -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `launch_standalone` | P1 | C++ BuildHandler (UnrealEditor-Cmd -game subprocess) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (player control) / Flop | Launch a standalone -game instance of the project for a smoke run (separate process, never blocks the editor); the cheap 'does it boot' check before full packaging. — `(map?, extra_args?, capture_log?=true) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running', pid}` |
| `project_get_setting` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (unreal config / GConfig read) — no rebuild | GenOrca/ChiR24 (config get) | Read a typed config value by ini/section/key (Game/Engine/Editor/Input); inspection-first companion to project_set_setting, surfaces tier-2 project_get_settings. — `(config:'Engine'\|'Game'\|'Editor'\|'Input', section, key) -> {ok, value, type, source_ini}` |
| `project_set_setting` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (GConfig write + flush) — no rebuild | GenOrca/ChiR24 (config set) — we add dry-run+readback | Write a Default*.ini config value with dry_run + read-back + Plan-Mode gate; the safe, verifiable mutation of project config (destructive, restart-flagged). — `(config, section, key, value, dry_run?=false) -> {ok, old_value, new_value, requires_restart:bool}` |
| `project_plugin_list` | P1 | C++ ProjectHandler (wraps project_list_plugins) | chongdashu/Flop | List plugins with enabled state, version, and category, paginated; inspection-first for plugin management (surfaces tier-2 project_list_plugins). — `(enabled_only?=false, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {plugins:[{name, enabled, version, category}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `build_clean` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (filesystem delete) — no rebuild | net-new moat | Delete Intermediate/Binaries for a target to force a clean rebuild; the unglamorous fix for stale-artifact build failures, with dry-run preview of what gets removed. — `(target?='Editor', scope?='intermediate'\|'binaries'\|'both', dry_run?=false) -> {ok, removed:[str], freed_mb}` |
| `cook_validate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry dependency walk) — no rebuild | net-new moat (validate-before-cook) | Dry-run cook check: enumerate MapsToCook + their hard references, flag unreachable/missing assets and cook-blocking errors without doing a real cook; pre-flight before the slow cook. — `(platform?='Windows', maps?=[str]) -> {ok, would_cook:int, missing_refs:[str], warnings:[str]}` |
| `package_pak` | P2 | C++ BuildHandler (UnrealPak / UAT -pak -skipstage) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | net-new moat | Create .pak/.utoc from an existing staged build (pak-only step) with optional compression; lets the agent re-pak without re-stage/cook. — `(platform?='Win64', compression?='Oodle'\|'Zlib'\|'none') -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `package_archive` | P2 | C++ BuildHandler (UAT -archive / subprocess zip) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | net-new moat | Zip/copy a packaged build to a destination directory for distribution; the final hand-off artifact step, separated so re-archiving doesn't rebuild. — `(source_dir, dest_dir, format?='zip'\|'copy') -> {ok, job_id, status:'running', output_path}` |
| `launch_on_device` | P2 | C++ BuildHandler (RunUAT BuildCookRun -deploy -run) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | remiphilippe (deploy/run) — net-new for the rest | Deploy + run a packaged build on a target device/platform (UAT -deploy -run) for real-hardware smoke; the device half of the DevOps loop. — `(platform, device_id?, configuration?='Development') -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `project_plugin_enable` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (.uproject JSON edit + project descriptor save) — no rebuild | net-new moat | Enable/disable a plugin in the .uproject with dry-run + restart-required flag and Plan-Mode gate; destructive config mutation made safe and verifiable. — `(plugin_name, enabled, dry_run?=false) -> {ok, old_state, new_state, requires_restart:true}` |
| `project_maps_list` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry World scan + GConfig) — no rebuild | net-new moat | List project maps plus the configured GameDefaultMap / EditorStartupMap / MapsToCook; inspection-first for packaging-settings work. — `(limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {maps:[str], game_default_map, editor_startup_map, maps_to_cook:[str], has_more}` |
| `project_set_packaging_settings` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (GConfig DefaultGame.ini writes) — no rebuild | net-new moat (outcome-verb over ini primitives) | Outcome-verb to set the common packaging knobs at once (maps-to-cook, build config, dirs-to-package, full-rebuild, pak compression) with dry-run + readback; flattens a dozen ini keys into one safe call. — `(maps_to_cook?=[str], build_config?, dirs_to_package?=[str], use_pak?, compression?, dry_run?=false) -> {ok, changed:[{key, old, new}]}` |
| `functional_test_map` | P2 | C++ TestHandler + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | Flop pie_test / remiphilippe (functional) | Run functional tests contained in a specific map (load map, execute AFunctionalTest actors), job-envelope; targeted gameplay verification beyond unit-style automation. — `(map, timeout_sec?=600) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `ci_build_cook_package` | P2 | C++ BuildHandler (chained UAT stages over FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry) | remiphilippe (CI loop) — net-new as a single tracked verb | One-shot chained pipeline (build -> cook -> package) tracked as a single parent job with per-stage checkpoints and fail-fast; the outcome-verb that turns the whole loop into one tracked, resumable operation. — `(platform?='Win64', configuration?='Shipping', archive_dir?, run_tests?=false, dry_run?=false) -> {ok, job_id, stages:['build','cook','package'], status:'running'}` |
| `package_dlc` | P3 | C++ BuildHandler (RunUAT BuildCookRun -dlcname) + FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry | net-new moat | Cook/package a plugin or DLC as a chunked release on top of a base build; advanced distribution for modular content (low priority, ceiling-limited by project chunking setup). — `(dlc_name, platform?='Win64', base_release_version?) -> {ok, job_id, status:'running'}` |
| `ddc_inspect` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (DDC stats console + optional -fillddc subprocess via job) — no rebuild for read | net-new moat | Report Derived Data Cache status/size and optionally warm it (fill-DDC lite cook) to speed subsequent cooks; the unglamorous perf lever for the iteration loop. — `(warm?=false, platform?='Windows') -> {ok, ddc_size_mb, hit_rate?, warmed:bool, job_id?}` |

**Dependencies:** FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry: a shared C++ async-job registry (TMap<FString,FJobState> + FCriticalSection + FProcHandle ownership) is the hard prerequisite for ALL P0 build/cook/package/test tools — it does not exist yet (planned in docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md). Nothing in this domain ships safely until it lands.; THEME 0 unfreeze: build_project/build_cook/test_run currently WaitFor-block the game thread (HaybaMCPBuildHandler.cpp RunOrBackground ~L248, HaybaMCPTestHandler.cpp ~L221). These must be converted to job-envelope before any net-new tool here.; THEME 1 add-a-tool refactor + pyTemplate factory: required so the ~18 python_run-backed tools land as descriptors without per-tool 7-file churn / plugin rebuilds.; SEH-wrap ProcessCommand: subprocess launch + config writes touch the whole 33-handler surface; the moat depends on the one-seam SEH extension.; Plan-Mode gate (bPlanApproved) + DisabledTools filter for the destructive tools (project_set_setting, project_plugin_enable, project_set_packaging_settings, package_*, build_clean).; UE installed-platform SDKs (Win64 always; Android/iOS/Linux/console SDKs gate build_target_list output and launch_on_device).; RunUAT.bat / UnrealBuildTool.exe / UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe present in EngineDir (already resolved in HaybaMCPBuildHandler.cpp ResolveUBTPath/ResolveRunUATPath/ResolveEditorExe).; python_run hardened gate (SEH + 3-tier security + dangling-delegate gate) for the config/validation/inspection tools.; UEditorValidatorSubsystem availability for project_validate_assets; AssetRegistry fully scanned for cook_inspect/cook_validate/project_maps_list.

**Risks:** Game-thread deadlock: python_run runs on the game thread, so a long subprocess launched from python would freeze the editor exactly like the bugs we are fixing. Mitigation: ALL long subprocess ops (build/cook/package/launch/pak/archive) stay C++ behind FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry; python_run tools are restricted to fast, in-process config/inspection/validation only.; Late-response crash (bug_haybamcp_tcpserver_late_response_crash): if a build returns synchronously and the client disconnects before reply, AV at HaybaMCPTcpServer.cpp:123. The job-envelope pattern (return job_id immediately, poll later) is precisely what avoids this — but build_job_wait must NOT reintroduce a long blocking reply.; Duplicate destructive launches: the executor's blind retry could fire two package/build jobs. build/cook/package/launch must be marked non-idempotent and excluded from auto-retry; job_id dedupe in the registry.; Orphaned child processes: a cancelled or editor-crashed job can leave detached UAT/cook processes. Registry must own FProcHandle and kill the process tree on cancel + on plugin shutdown.; UAT BuildCookRun is slow (minutes-to-tens-of-minutes) and noisy; log_excerpt must stay bounded (16KB) and tail be offset-paginated to avoid blowing the 1MB frame cap / token budget.; Config writes (project_set_setting/plugin_enable/packaging_settings) often require an editor restart to take effect — must be honestly flagged requires_restart, never silently 'applied'.; Cross-platform path/SDK assumptions: ResolveEditorExe is Win64-only today; launch_on_device / non-Win64 builds will fail confusingly without SDK presence checks (surface via build_target_list).; python-based cook_inspect/package_inspect read disk that a concurrent cook is still writing — must tolerate partial/locked files and report 'in_progress' rather than crash.; EngineVersion is pinned 5.7.0 and some handlers include engine-private headers — UAT/UBT CLI flags drift across 5.6-5.8; pin-test the command lines per engine version.

**Live-editor test plan:** "Run against a live editor with a small real project (the worldbuilding sample). 1) Land FHaybaAsyncJobRegistry; smoke build_project -> assert it returns {job_id,status:'running'} in <2s and the editor stays responsive (issue a second cheap command like project_inspect during the build and confirm it returns). 2) Poll build_job_status until status:'done' with exit_code 0; cross-check against hayba_journal_tail. 3) build_job_cancel a fresh build -> confirm child process is gone (no orphan UnrealBuildTool.exe in task list) and status:'cancelled'. 4) build_job_list shows the running then completed job; build_job_tail returns monotonically increasing offsets, no dupes. 5) project_validate_assets on /Game -> seed one broken reference, confirm it appears in errors; cook_validate flags the same missing ref WITHOUT a full cook. 6) cook_content for Windows -> poll to done -> cook_inspect reports cooked_assets>0 and a real output_dir; build_artifacts_inspect shows the cooked_mb delta. 7) package_project dry_run=true first -> eyeball the resolved UAT command line; then real run -> package_inspect confirms executable + .pak manifest + total_size_mb; launch_standalone on the cooked build boots and capture_viewport/editor_stream_log shows it running. 8) project_set_setting with dry_run=true -> assert old/new echoed and nothing written; then real write -> project_get_setting reads back the new value and requires_restart is honest. 9) project_plugin_enable dry_run -> .uproject untouched; real -> descriptor reflects the change. 10) test_run on a known-passing + known-failing automation filter -> test_get_log returns correct pass/fail counts; confirm the editor never freezes for the old 120s window. 11) Negative: kill the editor mid-cook, reconnect, build_job_list surfaces the orphaned job state (or honestly reports it lost) and no zombie process remains. 12) ci_build_cook_package end-to-end with run_tests=true on a clean checkout -> single parent job_id walks build->cook->package stages, fail-fast verified by injecting a compile error."


<a id="domain-19"></a>
## 19. ai-copilot-chat-panel

*34 tools — P0:12 P1:12 P2:9 P3:1*

Revive the BYOK in-editor AI copilot as a thin-client / fat-server split: the agentic tool-calling loop lives in the Node MCP server (new chat-server.ts over the existing Express app in dashboard/api.ts, reusing the live tool registry, gating, PLUMB validator and journal), and C++ becomes a streaming SSE consumer (rework FHaybaMCPClaudeClient + SHaybaMCPChatPanel, reusing the OnToolCallRecorded seam). The surface is three layers: (1) MCP/sidecar tools for provider+key config, the streaming chat loop, plan-mode gating, and session persistence; (2) introspection/verify/dry-run tools (the unglamorous core) so an agent can configure and self-check the panel without guessing; (3) C++ commands/components for the Slate panel, DPAPI-encrypted key vault, and SSE cancel. We restore llm-client.ts from ac46d40 and the 8-provider catalog (anthropic/openai/groq/openrouter/ollama/lmstudio/mock/custom) into one providers.ts, honoring DisabledTools + archetype tool_filter + Plan-Mode before any destructive tool. Moats pressed: token economy (per-session budget accounting + Code-Mode deferred routing feeding the loop), crash-resilience (loop runs server-side, never on the game thread), secure-local BYOK (DPAPI vault vs competitors' plaintext/cloud), and the vision feedback loop (auto-attach viewport/selection context).

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `copilot_provider_list` | P0 | TS sidecar providers.ts (Node MCP server) | Aura (BYOK multi-provider); net-new as a single in-editor catalog tool | List the 8-provider catalog (anthropic/openai/groq/openrouter/ollama/lmstudio/mock/custom) with configured/reachable/default flags so an agent knows which BYOK backends exist before configuring one. — `() -> {providers:[{id,label,baseURLDefault,configured,reachable,isDefault}]}` |
| `copilot_provider_set` | P0 | TS sidecar providers.ts + HaybaMCPSettings (C++ mirror for dropdown) | Aura/ChiR24 partial; net-new unified switch | Select active provider + model + optional baseURL/headers for the chat loop; the single place backend selection is persisted, replacing scattered env vars. — `(provider, model?, base_url?, headers?) -> {ok, active:{provider,model,base_url}}` |
| `copilot_provider_test` | P0 | TS sidecar providers.ts | net-new moat (no peer offers a pre-flight provider probe) | 1-token handshake probe against the chosen provider+key to verify reachability/auth before a real turn; returns latency and a machine error code. Verify tool. — `(provider?, model?) -> {ok, reachable, latency_ms, error_code?}` |
| `copilot_model_list` | P1 | TS sidecar providers.ts | net-new for in-editor UE MCP | List models for the active provider (static catalog + dynamic /models fetch where supported, e.g. openrouter/ollama/lmstudio) so the UI dropdown and agents stay current. — `(provider?) -> {models:[{id,context_window,supports_tools,cost_in,cost_out}], source:'catalog'\|'live'}` |
| `copilot_provider_add_custom` | P2 | TS sidecar providers.ts | net-new moat (air-gap/BYO-gateway) | Register a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint (label+baseURL+auth header name) as a first-class provider for air-gapped/self-hosted gateways. Reinforces local/NDA moat. — `(label, base_url, auth_header?, model_default?) -> {ok, provider_id}` |
| `copilot_key_set` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (HaybaMCPSettings + DPAPI CryptProtectData) | net-new moat (peers store plaintext or require cloud) | Store a BYOK API key for a provider in the DPAPI-encrypted vault (replaces plaintext GEditorPerProjectIni); the sidecar reads it via a local IPC handshake, never embeds it in logs. — `(provider, api_key) -> {ok, stored:true}` |
| `copilot_key_status` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (HaybaMCPSettings) + TS proxy | net-new moat (secure introspection) | Report whether a key is present/decryptable per provider WITHOUT ever echoing the secret (masked last-4 only); the introspection tool that makes config debuggable safely. — `(provider?) -> {providers:[{id,has_key,masked_tail,encrypted:true}]}` |
| `copilot_key_clear` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (HaybaMCPSettings) | net-new | Delete a stored key from the vault (BYOK hygiene / handoff between users). Destructive on secrets, so plan-gated. — `(provider) -> {ok, cleared:true}` |
| `copilot_chat_send` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts POST /chat/stream (Node MCP server) | Aura/Flop (native in-editor agent); we add transactional + PLUMB-validated loop | THE core tool: open a server-side agentic loop for a session, stream tokens + tool-call events over SSE, executing MCP tools through the hardened hayba_invoke path with validate-before/after-mutate; returns the final transcript + usage. — `(session_id, message, attachments?, max_steps?, stream=true) -> SSE{token\|tool_call\|tool_result\|plan_request\|done} ; final {assistant_text, tool_trace[], usage}` |
| `copilot_chat_cancel` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts + C++ FHaybaMCPClaudeClient::Cancel | net-new (most peers cannot interrupt a running agent) | Cancel the in-flight generation/loop for a session, implementing the long-promised Cancel(); aborts the SSE stream and any pending tool call cleanly. Crash-resilience moat. — `(session_id) -> {ok, cancelled:true, partial_text?}` |
| `copilot_plan_preview` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (plan mode) + plumb_validate | Flop bp_dry_run / Aura Sandbox; we add quantified PLUMB prediction | Dry-run the next turn: the loop plans the tool steps WITHOUT executing destructive ops, returning an ordered step list (with destructive flags + PLUMB predicted-validation) for the green/red Plan-Mode overlay. — `(session_id, message) -> {steps:[{tool, args, destructive, predicted_validation}], requires_approval}` |
| `copilot_plan_approve` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts + C++ panel toggle | Aura Sandbox approval; net-new granular per-step gate | Set bPlanApproved for a pending plan (whole-plan or per-step ids) so destructive tools may run; the gate that satisfies the Plan-Mode invariant before any mutation. — `(session_id, approve=true, step_ids?) -> {ok, approved_steps[]}` |
| `copilot_tool_approve_single` | P1 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts | Aura Sandbox Mode (per-action) | Approve or reject one specific pending tool call mid-loop (Sandbox-style human-in-the-loop) without approving the whole plan; lets the user veto a single risky op. — `(session_id, tool_call_id, decision:'approve'\|'reject', edited_args?) -> {ok, resumed}` |
| `copilot_session_new` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store) | chongdashu/Aura have sessions; net-new archetype binding | Start a fresh conversation/session with optional goal, archetype, and tool_filter; returns a session_id the panel and agents share. Lifecycle entry point. — `(goal?, archetype?, tool_filter?) -> {session_id, created_at}` |
| `copilot_session_list` | P1 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store) | Aura (session history) | Paginated list of recent sessions (title, last-message, turn count, total cost) for the recent-sessions menu and resume. Paginated per global rule. — `(limit=20, offset=0) -> {sessions:[{id,title,updated_at,turns,cost_usd}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `copilot_session_get` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store) | net-new depth (tool trace + validation inline) | Fetch a full session: message transcript + interleaved tool-call trace + validator results, for rehydrating the panel or letting an agent inspect prior context. Inspection-first pair to chat_send. — `(session_id, limit?, offset?) -> {messages[], tool_trace[], usage, has_more}` |
| `copilot_session_resume` | P1 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store) | Aura | Re-open a persisted session as the active one so a new turn continues prior context after an editor restart; persistence is a named must-have. — `(session_id) -> {ok, active_session, message_count}` |
| `copilot_session_delete` | P2 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store) | common | Delete a session + its transcript/usage rows (privacy/cleanup). Destructive, plan-gated. — `(session_id) -> {ok, deleted:true}` |
| `copilot_session_export` | P2 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts | net-new | Export a transcript to markdown or JSON (with tool trace) for sharing, bug reports, or PLUMB lessons; feeds the lessons-as-memory moat. — `(session_id, format:'md'\|'json') -> {ok, content, byte_len}` |
| `copilot_transcript_search` | P3 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (session store FTS) | net-new | Full-text search across stored sessions/messages (and tool names) so an agent can recall a prior solution. Token-economy aid (avoid re-deriving). — `(query, limit=20, offset=0) -> {hits:[{session_id, snippet, role, score}], has_more}` |
| `copilot_message_retry` | P1 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts | Aura/Flop | Re-run the last assistant turn (regenerate / redo) using the same or a switched model; powers the panel's Redo affordance. — `(session_id, model_override?) -> SSE stream + {assistant_text, usage}` |
| `copilot_message_edit_resend` | P2 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts | net-new for UE MCP | Edit a prior user message and re-run the conversation from that point (branching), truncating downstream turns; standard agentic chat ergonomics. — `(session_id, message_index, new_text) -> {ok, branched_session_id} + SSE` |
| `copilot_context_attach` | P1 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts + editor_capture_viewport/render_camera | net-new moat (no peer auto-injects viewport+selection) | Attach context to the next turn: current selection, viewport screenshot (editor_capture_viewport), or named asset, returned to the model as image/text content blocks. Vision-loop moat applied to chat. — `(session_id, kinds:['selection'\|'viewport'\|'asset'\|'level'], asset_path?) -> {ok, attached:[{kind, mime, summary}]}` |

Safety + token economy. — `(session_id, archetype?, tool_filter?) -> {ok, allowed_count}` |

USD, by model) surfaced in the footer; productizes the token-economy moot. — `(session_id) -> {tokens_in, tokens_out, cached, cost_usd, by_model[]}` |

| `copilot_panel_open` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (SHaybaMCPChatPanel tab spawner) | net-new | Open/focus the Slate chat tab in the editor (and select a session); the C++ command that lets an agent surface the copilot UI to the user. — `(session_id?) -> {ok, panel_visible:true}` |
| `copilot_health` | P0 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts (aggregates hayba_check_ue_status + provider_test) | remiphilippe/Houdini status probes; net-new aggregate | One-call subsystem health: provider reachable, key present, sidecar up, UE TCP connected, registry size, active session; the get_*_status gating probe for the whole chat stack. — `() -> {provider_ok, key_ok, sidecar_ok, ue_connected, tools_available, active_session?, issues[]}` |
| `copilot_emit_mcp_json` | P2 | TS sidecar chat-server.ts | Epic (auto client-config); we match + interop | Emit an Epic-compatible .mcp.json / client config for the running server (incl. chat toolset) so the panel interops with Epic's Toolset Registry instead of fighting it. Interop, not lock-in. — `(client:'epic'\|'claude'\|'cursor', out_path?) -> {ok, config_json, written_path?}` |

**Dependencies:** llm-client.ts restored from commit ac46d40 (LLMTool/LLMToolCall/LLMResponse, stopReason 'tool_use'\|'end_turn') — only correct tool-calling shape in repo history; Single providers.ts merging the 8-provider catalog from 2849a75 presets + 4d47e58 env/baseURL/model maps: mock, anthropic, groq, openrouter, openai, ollama, lmstudio, custom; npm deps @anthropic-ai/sdk and openai added to mcp-tools/hayba-mcp; SSE/streaming added to llm-client.complete; Existing Express app in src/dashboard/api.ts hosts POST /chat/stream via new src/chat/chat-server.ts (registered alongside registerApiRoutes); Live tool registry + hayba_invoke polymorphic dispatch + Code-Mode/deferred routing — the loop calls tools through the same hardened path, not a parallel one; agents/agent-registry.ts archetype.tool_filter (globToRegex) + DisabledTools for per-session tool scoping; C++ OnToolCallRecorded multicast seam (HaybaMCPModule.h:81) bridged into the SSE trace + panel; HaybaMCPSettings vault: replace plaintext GEditorPerProjectIni ApiKey with Windows DPAPI CryptProtectData/CryptUnprotectData; FHaybaMCPClaudeClient reworked from request/response HTTP to SSE consumer + Cancel(); SHaybaMCPChatPanel wires tool-stream/validator/diff + Preview->Plan panel; PLUMB validator (plumb_validate) + validator_run invoked inside the loop for validate-before/after-mutate; Session/usage persistence store (reuse hayba-memory.db SQLite patterns) for sessions/transcripts/usage; keep DB gitignored; Theme 0/1 gates first: ProcessCommand SEH wrap + add-a-tool descriptor refactor; chat tools ship as descriptors (handler/pyTemplate), no per-tool 7-file churn

**Risks:** Game-thread stall: the loop is server-side (locked) but its tool calls still marshal to the game thread via RunSync; a slow tool can stall UE. Mitigation: align executor timeouts to UE ceilings, gate retries to idempotent tools only, run build/test as job-envelopes (Theme 0).; Secret leakage: keys must never appear in SSE frames, journal, transcripts, or tool args. Mitigation: vault holds the only copy; sidecar reads via local IPC; copilot_key_status returns masked last-4 only.; DPAPI is Windows-only — cross-platform key storage is unsolved (matches plugin's current Win64 pinning); document as a known limitation, fall back to OS keychain later.; Plan-Mode bypass: if any destructive tool runs before bPlanApproved the safety moat is void. Mitigation: gate is enforced server-side in the loop, not in the C++ UI, so it cannot be skipped by a non-UI client.; Streaming/tool-call shape drift across 8 providers (Anthropic blocks vs OpenAI tool_calls vs ollama partial support). Mitigation: normalize in llm-client; copilot_provider_test + model.supports_tools flag gate non-tool models.; Duplicate-server confusion: the chat loop must reuse the single tool registry/gating/validator/journal — re-implementing any of these (as Aura warns) doubles maintenance and risks divergence.; SSE reconnect: editor sleep / port re-discovery can drop the stream mid-loop; client must resume by session_id + last seq, not restart the turn (avoid duplicate destructive ops).; Cost runaway in unattended loops; mitigated by copilot_budget_limit_set halt-on-exceed, but default ceilings must be sane.

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation: (1) Start the MCP server + open the editor with HaybaMCPToolkit; call copilot_health and confirm sidecar_ok + ue_connected + tools_available>0. (2) copilot_key_set('anthropic', <key>) then copilot_key_status — verify has_key true, masked_tail only, and confirm the on-disk GEditorPerProjectIni value is DPAPI ciphertext (not plaintext); copilot_provider_test returns reachable with latency. (3) copilot_provider_list/model_list populate the panel dropdown. (4) copilot_session_new -> copilot_chat_send('spawn a cube and validate naming') over SSE: observe token frames, a tool_call (actor_spawn) frame, tool_result, and a final transcript; confirm the cube exists in the live level and the tool trace appears in the panel via copilot_tool_trace_tail. (5) Plan-Mode: with a destructive prompt, copilot_plan_preview returns steps with destructive flags + predicted PLUMB validation and requires_approval; confirm NO mutation happened; copilot_plan_approve then re-run and confirm exactly one Ctrl+Z reverts the bundle. (6) copilot_chat_cancel mid-stream — verify generation stops, partial_text returned, and no orphaned tool call. (7) Restart editor, copilot_session_resume + copilot_session_get — transcript + tool trace rehydrate; copilot_budget_get shows non-zero token/cost. (8) copilot_toolset_filter_set(['plumb_*']) then ask for an actor op — confirm the loop refuses (tool not in allowed_tools from copilot_toolset_filter_get). (9) copilot_context_attach(['viewport','selection']) — confirm an image content block reaches the model and influences the reply. (10) Loop ollama/lmstudio locally to prove BYOK/local-only path works air-gapped; copilot_emit_mcp_json produces a config Epic's client accepts.


<a id="domain-20"></a>
## 20. UMG / Widget Blueprint / UI authoring

*35 tools — P0:7 P1:14 P2:11 P3:3*

UMG is a top-3 gameplay authoring surface entirely absent from the first-class catalog, yet the plugin already ships a working-but-unsurfaced C++ tier-2 handler (HaybaMCPUIHandler.cpp: ui_create_widget / ui_add_element / ui_query) reachable only via hayba_invoke ue_legacy. Fastest moat: (1) wrap those three as honest first-class tools with zero rebuild, then (2) close the real gaps that handler leaves open — it never compiles or saves the WBP, hardcodes top-left CanvasPanel anchors, and has no widget-property/event/animation/batch/named-slot support. The headline parity play is umg_compose_tree: one transactional outcome-verb that builds an entire hierarchy from a single JSON spec (matching Roblox create_ui_tree), with a paired dry-run validator and a single-undo wrapper that beats Epic's per-call undo. Inspection-first reads plus a vision/PIE preview loop make the domain self-verifying. Tree mutation is C++-favored for robustness (WidgetTree + FScopedTransaction); lifecycle, listing, read-back, style convenience, and validation ride on python_run/TS with no rebuild.</summary>
<parameter name="dependencies">["Existing C++ handler HaybaMCPUIHandler.cpp (ui_create_widget/ui_add_element/ui_query) reachable via hayba_invoke ue_legacy — basis for thin TS wrappers","UMG/UMGEditor + Blueprint/WidgetTree + WidgetBlueprintFactory (already included in the handler)","FBlueprintEditorUtils::MarkBlueprintAsStructurallyModified + FKismetEditorUtilities::CompileBlueprint for the missing compile/save step","FScopedTransaction seam from the cross-cutting Transactions/undo domain (4C) for single-undo bundling","Vision-loop tools (editor_capture_viewport / render_camera) and PIE handler (editor_start_pie) for preview/verify","PLUMB validator_run + validator_rules for umg_validate UI checks","Add-a-tool refactor (Theme 1): one descriptor + pyTemplate factory so the python-backed tools land with no per-tool 7-file churn","Plan-Mode gate (bPlanApproved) before destructive ops (delete/remove/reparent)"]

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `umg_widget_inspect` | P0 | TS wrapper -> ue_legacy ui_query (HaybaMCPUIHandler) | GenOrca UMG inspect / Flop inspect-first | Dump a WBP's full tree (names, classes, slot type + geometry, children) so the agent plans before mutating — the paired read tool for the whole domain. — `(widget_blueprint_path, depth?, include_props?=false) -> {root, tree:[{name,class,slot,children}], count}` |
| `umg_widget_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry) | net-new moat | Paginated list of WidgetBlueprint assets filtered by path/parent-class so the agent discovers existing UI before authoring. — `(path_prefix?, parent_class?, limit=50, offset=0) -> {items:[{path,name,parent_class}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `umg_palette_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (closes reflection-introspection gap) | List constructible widget classes (built-in + project UserWidgets) with child-capacity (none/single/multi) so the agent picks valid classes and panels. — `(filter?, include_user_widgets?=true) -> {classes:[{name,path,child_rule,slot_type}]}` |
| `umg_widget_get_properties` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Flop read-back / Roblox get_instance_properties | Read back all editable properties of a named widget in a WBP — the verify half of every set_* tool. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name) -> {name,class,props:{...},slot:{...}}` |
| `umg_widget_create` | P0 | TS wrapper -> ue_legacy ui_create_widget (HaybaMCPUIHandler) | Roblox create_ui_tree (partial) / GenOrca | Create a Widget Blueprint asset (parent UserWidget or custom) with a root CanvasPanel — the entry point the catalog falsely lacks today. — `(path, name, parent_class?='UserWidget') -> {path, name, parent_class, root}` |
| `umg_compile` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (FKismetEditorUtilities::CompileBlueprint + save) | net-new moat (honesty fix) | Compile + save the WBP and return errors/warnings — critical fix: the existing handler marks dirty but never compiles, so edits are lost on restart. — `(widget_blueprint_path) -> {ok, errors:[], warnings:[], saved}` |
| `umg_add_widget` | P0 | TS wrapper -> ue_legacy ui_add_element (HaybaMCPUIHandler) | GenOrca / chongdashu BP-graph analog | Add a widget of class X under a parent panel (or root) with optional slot props — single-element compose. — `(widget_blueprint_path, child_class, parent_widget_name?, name?, slot_props?) -> {name, class, parent, slot_class}` |
| `umg_compose_tree` | P0 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (new ui_compose_tree in HaybaMCPUIHandler) | Roblox create_ui_tree (net-new for UE) | Build an entire widget hierarchy from one JSON spec in a single transaction — the headline Roblox-parity outcome verb; atomic, rolls back on failure. — `(widget_blueprint_path, spec:{class,name,slot_props,props,children[]}, replace_root?=false) -> {created:[names], root, undo_id}` |
| `umg_compose_dry_run` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | Flop bp_dry_run | Validate a compose_tree spec (class resolution, slot compatibility, name collisions, child-rule limits) without mutating — generalizes the world_generate dry-run pattern to UMG. — `(widget_blueprint_path, spec) -> {ok, resolved:[{name,class}], problems:[{path,code,msg}]}` |
| `umg_remove_widget` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_remove_element) | Roblox delete_object | Remove a widget (and its subtree) from the tree by name — Plan-Mode gated destructive op. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name) -> {removed, parent}` |
| `umg_reparent_widget` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_reparent) | Roblox move_object | Move a widget under a new parent panel, preserving the subtree — restructure without rebuild. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, new_parent_name, index?) -> {widget, new_parent, slot_class}` |
| `umg_rename_widget` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_rename) | Roblox rename_object | Rename a widget in the tree (updates variable name + references) — keeps designer/codegen consistent. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, new_name) -> {old_name, new_name}` |
| `umg_duplicate_widget` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_duplicate) | Roblox clone_object / smart_duplicate | Duplicate a widget (or subtree) within the same WBP under a parent — fast repeat of styled elements. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, new_name?, parent_name?) -> {new_name, class}` |
| `umg_wrap_widget` | P3 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_wrap) | net-new moat | Wrap an existing widget in a new panel (Border/SizeBox/Overlay) in place — non-destructive layout insertion. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, wrapper_class) -> {wrapper, wrapped}` |
| `umg_set_property` | P0 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_property) | Roblox set_property / GenOrca | Set any widget property by reflection (Visibility, ToolTipText, IsEnabled, Padding, etc.) — the generic write primitive over the handler's reflection helper. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, property, value) -> {ok, applied:{property,value}}` |
| `umg_batch_set_property` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_batch_set_property) | Roblox mass_set_property | Set one property across all widgets matching class/name-glob in a WBP — the batch-ops moat for restyling many widgets in one call. — `(widget_blueprint_path, match:{class?,name_glob?}, property, value) -> {changed:[names], count}` |
| `umg_set_text` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Convenience: set text + font size + justification + color on text-bearing widgets (TextBlock/Button label/EditableText) without juggling FSlateFontInfo by hand. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, text, font_size?, justify?, color?) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_set_brush` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_brush) | net-new moat | Set an Image/Border/Button brush: texture or material, tint, draw-as (Box/Border/Image), image size — typed over fragile reflection of FSlateBrush. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, asset_path?, tint?, draw_as?, image_size?) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_set_style` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_style) | net-new moat | Apply a style struct or style asset (button/checkbox/slider/progressbar) — outcome verb for consistent theming. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, style:{...}\|style_asset_path) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_set_slot` | P0 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_slot) | GenOrca UMG slot | Set generic slot props (padding/fill/alignment) across any slot type — first-class over the handler's ApplySlotProps. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, slot_props:{...}) -> {ok, slot_class}` |
| `umg_set_anchors` | P1 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_anchors) | net-new moat (gap fix) | Set true CanvasPanelSlot anchors (min/max), offsets, alignment, autosize — fixes the handler's hardcoded top-left anchors so responsive layouts work. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, anchors_min, anchors_max, offsets, alignment?, autosize?) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_set_grid_slot` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_grid_slot) | net-new moat | Set row/column/row-span/column-span/alignment for Grid and UniformGridPanel slots — covers the layout family the handler ignores. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, row, column, row_span?=1, col_span?=1) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_set_named_slot` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (ui_set_named_slot) | net-new moat | Set the content widget of a NamedSlot (UserWidget templating) — enables composition/override patterns agents otherwise can't reach. — `(widget_blueprint_path, named_slot, content_widget_name) -> {ok}` |
| `umg_add_property_binding` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (HaybaMCPUIHandler + Blueprint module) | net-new moat (engine limit flagged) | Bind a widget property (Text/Visibility/Color) to a BP getter function or variable — template-scoped; flagged engine-limited. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, property, binding_function\|binding_var) -> {ok, kind}` |
| `umg_bind_event` | P2 | HaybaMCPToolkit C++ (HaybaMCPBlueprintHandler assist) | net-new moat (engine limit flagged) | Wire a widget event (OnClicked/OnValueChanged) to a BP custom event/function stub, creating the stub if absent — the K2-graph slice of UMG; low ceiling, flagged. — `(widget_blueprint_path, widget_name, event_name, handler_name?) -> {ok, handler}` |
| `umg_widget_set_parent_class` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Reparent the WBP itself to a different UserWidget base class — restructure inheritance without recreating. — `(widget_blueprint_path, new_parent_class) -> {ok, parent_class}` |
| `umg_widget_duplicate` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | Roblox clone_object | Duplicate a whole WBP asset to a new path/name — fast variant authoring. — `(widget_blueprint_path, dest_path, new_name) -> {path}` |
