**Dependencies:** Theme 0/1 gates: ProcessCommand SEH wrap + add-a-tool descriptor refactor must land first so the ~24 new commands ship as descriptors, not 7-file churn.; UE ISourceControlModule + a configured provider plugin (PerforceSourceControl / GitSourceControl / SubversionSourceControl) enabled in the .uproject; tools must degrade gracefully (sc_provider_info reports disabled) when none is configured.; unreal.SourceControlHelpers Python API for the no-rebuild fast-path tools (status/file_state/checkout/add/delete/revert/sync_file).; New C++ handler module HaybaMCPSourceControlHandler.{h,cpp} under Private/handlers/, wired into HaybaMCPCommandHandler.cpp ProcessCommand, for async ISourceControlProvider ops (connect/submit/changelist/resolve/diff/history/shelve/lock).; Async source control operations must run on the game-thread via FHaybaGameThread::RunSync (per MEMORY: handlers run on game thread; AsyncTask(GameThread)+Wait self-deadlocks) and complete within executor timeout ceilings (submit/sync are long — use job-envelope pattern like build_*).; Existing SHaybaMCPDiffPanel (BuildSourceControlBadge/IsSourceControlEnabled/SourceControlProviderName) reused as the diff/review surface for sc_diff and as the place agent checkouts surface.; Plan-Mode gate (bPlanApproved) + DisabledTools must cover the destructive verbs: sc_submit, sc_revert*, sc_sync(force), sc_mark_for_delete, sc_unlock, sc_disconnect.

**Risks:** Submit/sync/connect are genuinely async and can block (server round-trips, large depots) — naive synchronous waits will hit the same game-thread-freeze class as build_*/test_run; MUST use the job-envelope + RunSync seam and align timeouts, or the moat tool reintroduces a P0 freeze.; Provider behavior is NOT uniform: Perforce has changelists/shelve/exclusive-lock; Git has stash/LFS-lock and no numbered CLs; SVN differs again. Changelist/shelve/lock tools need per-provider capability flags so they fail honestly (capability_unsupported code) instead of silently no-op — violating the honesty invariant otherwise.; Irreversible/destructive ops (sc_submit, sc_revert_all, sc_sync force) can lose human teammates' work or pollute a shared depot; require dry-run-first (sc_submit_preview), Plan-Mode approval, and require_clean default true.; Credential handling for sc_connect: P4 passwords / tokens must not be logged in the journal or echoed in results; reuse the DPAPI-encrypted key-storage pattern from the BYOK panel, never plaintext ini.; Blind-retry of non-idempotent ops would double-submit or double-checkout; sc_submit/sc_mark_for_add/sc_changelist_create must be excluded from the idempotent-retry allowlist (Theme 0 transport fix).; unreal.SourceControlHelpers Python surface is narrower than ISourceControlProvider and varies across 5.x; the python fast-path tools may need fallback to the C++ handler on versions where a helper is missing.; Uasset binary diff is non-trivial — sc_diff structured object diff depends on FAssetToolsModule asset-diff support; for unmergeable binaries fall back to a property/summary diff rather than a misleading unified text diff.; Multi-agent on a shared depot can deadlock on locks/conflicts; sc_lock/sc_resolve must surface other_user and never auto-accept-theirs by default (auto_safe only).

**Live-editor test plan:** "On a live editor connected to a throwaway test depot (spin up a local P4D and a Git+LFS repo; run the full matrix on at least Perforce + Git to prove provider-agnostic behavior): 1) sc_provider_info on a project with SC disabled -> reports enabled=false (honesty); enable a provider, re-run -> reports provider/login. 2) sc_connect with valid then invalid creds -> ok/login true, then clean error_code (no plaintext password in journal — grep the journal). 3) Create a new asset via actor/asset tools, sc_checkout_or_add -> sc_file_state confirms is_added; sc_status shows it; sc_submit_preview lists it with action=add; sc_submit -> sc_history shows the new revision. 4) Edit an existing tracked asset WITHOUT checkout, then sc_reconcile(dry_run) -> lists it under to_edit; sc_checkout -> sc_file_state is_checked_out true. 5) sc_revert on a checked-out unchanged file (only_unchanged) -> reverted; sc_file_state clean. 6) Conflict path: from a second client edit+submit the same file, sc_sync here -> reports conflict; sc_resolve(theirs) -> still_conflicted empty; sc_file_state is_conflicted false. 7) Changelist flow: sc_changelist_create -> sc_changelist_add_files -> sc_changelist_describe shows files -> sc_submit(changelist_id) -> sc_changelist_delete on the now-empty CL. 8) sc_diff a locally-edited uasset -> structured changed_props non-empty and the change appears in SHaybaMCPDiffPanel. 9) Batch: select 3 actors in the viewport, sc_checkout_selected -> all 3 source packages checked out. 10) Perforce-only: sc_lock then attempt edit from second client (denied), sc_unlock; sc_shelve a CL, sc_unshelve restores. 11) Capability honesty: run sc_shelve/sc_lock against the Git provider -> capability_unsupported, not a crash or silent success. 12) Freeze check: run sc_sync on a large path and sc_submit of 50 files while polling editor responsiveness (editor_capture_viewport / wait_for_idle) -> editor stays responsive (job-envelope, no game-thread freeze). 13) Plan-Mode: with bPlanApproved=false, sc_submit/sc_revert_all are refused before execution."


<a id="domain-26"></a>
## 26. Localization — String Tables, culture/locale set, text gather/compile, .po import/export, localization dashboard targets

*36 tools — P0:12 P1:12 P2:11 P3:1*

Localization is a total greenfield: no UE MCP competitor (Epic 5.8, Aura, Flop, GenOrca, UAIP) ships any localization tooling, so nearly every tool here is a net-new moat that ties directly into Hayba's existing PLUMB "validate before/after mutate" engine. The strategy is three layers: (1) cheap, no-rebuild python_run wrappers for the author loop — String Table CRUD, CSV round-trip, culture config, preview-culture switching — which cover ~80% of value with zero plugin churn; (2) job-envelope-wrapped commandlet runs (GatherText/compile/.po export-import) that MUST go through the Theme-0 async infra because the GatherText commandlet is a multi-second-to-minute external process that would freeze the editor exactly like build_*/test_run; (3) quantified-validation tools (placeholder-integrity, .po round-trip safety, namespace/key collision audit, unlocalized-text scan, dashboard completion %) that productize the PLUMB moat into the localization correctness loop. Every write tool ships a paired *_get/*_inspect read-back, is FScopedTransaction- or dry_run-guarded, and surfaces a real per-culture completion dashboard — turning "did my localization actually land?" from a blind commandlet into a closed loop. We deliberately accept that String Table entry mutation and ULocalizationTarget config have version-variable Python coverage, and fall back to CSV-import and direct Config/Localization/*.ini editing where the reflected API is thin.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `loc_string_table_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (AssetTools + StringTableFactory) | net-new moat | Create a UStringTable asset with a namespace at a content path; the foundation asset for all in-game localizable text. — `(package_path, asset_name, namespace) -> {ok, asset_path, namespace}` |
| `loc_string_table_set_entry` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (StringTable mutable, CSV-import fallback) | net-new moat | Add or update a single key->source-string entry (with optional translator metadata/context) in a String Table; the core write op of the author loop. — `(asset_path, key, source_string, metadata?, dry_run?) -> {ok, key, created\|updated, before?}` |
| `loc_string_table_batch_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (FScopedTransaction-wrapped) | net-new moat (cf. Roblox bulk_set_attributes) | Set/update many key->value entries in ONE undo transaction; outcome-verb to avoid N round-trips when seeding a table. — `(asset_path, entries[], dry_run?) -> {ok, set_count, created, updated, conflicts[]}` |
| `loc_string_table_remove_entry` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Remove a key from a String Table; destructive, dry_run-able, returns the removed value for undo/verify. — `(asset_path, key, dry_run?) -> {ok, removed_value}` |
| `loc_string_table_rename_key` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Rename a key while preserving source string and metadata; avoids the delete+recreate footgun that orphans translations. — `(asset_path, old_key, new_key, dry_run?) -> {ok, value_preserved}` |
| `loc_string_table_get` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read back all entries (key, source, metadata) from a String Table, paginated; the mandatory inspect/verify pair for the write tools. — `(asset_path, limit?, offset?, filter?) -> {namespace, entries[], total, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `loc_string_table_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry) | net-new moat | List all UStringTable assets in the project with entry counts and namespaces; project-wide localization inventory. — `(path_filter?, limit?, offset?) -> {tables[], total, has_more}` |
| `loc_string_table_import_csv` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (StringTable CSV import) | net-new moat | Bulk-import entries from a Key,SourceString[,Comment] CSV into a String Table; the reliable high-volume seeding path when the reflected setter is thin. — `(asset_path, csv_path, mode=merge\|replace, dry_run?) -> {ok, imported, skipped, conflicts[]}` |
| `loc_string_table_export_csv` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Export a String Table to CSV for external editing/diff; read-back + interchange tool. — `(asset_path, csv_path) -> {ok, csv_path, row_count}` |
| `loc_culture_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (InternationalizationLibrary) | net-new moat | List ICU cultures available in the engine (code, display name, plural form) with a filter; lets the agent pick valid locale codes instead of guessing. — `(filter?, limit?, offset?) -> {cultures[], total, has_more}` |
| `loc_culture_info` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Get ICU detail for one culture code: native/English name, parent/fallback chain, plural categories, RTL flag; informs translator and plural-form handling. — `(culture_code) -> {code, native_name, english_name, fallbacks[], plural_forms[], is_rtl}` |
| `loc_project_cultures_get` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (read DefaultGame.ini/Engine internationalization) | net-new moat | Read the project's currently configured supported/cooked cultures and default culture; inspect pair before any culture mutation. — `() -> {default_culture, supported[], cooked[]}` |
| `loc_project_culture_add` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (DefaultGame.ini edit) | net-new moat | Add a supported (and optionally cooked) culture to project settings; expands which locales the packaged game ships. — `(culture_code, cook=true, dry_run?) -> {ok, supported[], cooked[]}` |
| `loc_project_culture_remove` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Remove a supported/cooked culture from project settings; destructive, dry_run-able. — `(culture_code, dry_run?) -> {ok, supported[]}` |
| `loc_preview_culture_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (SetCurrentCulture / console culture=) | net-new moat | Switch the live editor/runtime current culture so UI text re-resolves immediately; pairs with vision-capture to visually verify a translation landed. — `(culture_code) -> {ok, previous_culture, current_culture}` |
| `loc_preview_culture_get` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read the current editor/runtime culture and language; read-back for the preview switcher. — `() -> {culture, language, locale}` |
| `loc_preview_culture_reset` | P3 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Reset preview culture back to the editor default; recovers from a preview switch that destabilized the UI. — `() -> {ok, current_culture}` |
| `loc_target_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (LocalizationSettings + Config/Localization scan) | net-new moat | List localization dashboard targets (Game/Engine/plugin) with native culture, supported cultures, and last-gather word counts; the dashboard root view. — `() -> {targets[]}` |
| `loc_target_get` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Get one target's full config: gather search paths, file extensions, native culture, cultures, dependencies, and gather settings; inspect pair for configure. — `(target_name) -> {target, native_culture, cultures[], gather_paths[], dependencies[]}` |
| `loc_target_create` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (Config/Localization/<target>.ini write) | net-new moat | Create a new localization target with native culture and initial supported cultures; net-new target for a feature/plugin. — `(target_name, native_culture, cultures[], gather_paths[], dry_run?) -> {ok, target, config_path}` |
| `loc_target_configure` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Update a target's gather paths, file extensions, native culture, or dependencies in one call; outcome-verb over editing raw .ini. — `(target_name, gather_paths?, extensions?, native_culture?, dependencies?, dry_run?) -> {ok, changed[], before}` |
| `loc_target_add_culture` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Add a translation culture to a target (creates its archive dir); grows coverage without touching unrelated config. — `(target_name, culture_code, dry_run?) -> {ok, cultures[]}` |
| `loc_target_remove_culture` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Remove a culture from a target; destructive, dry_run-able, reports archive files affected. — `(target_name, culture_code, dry_run?) -> {ok, cultures[], removed_files[]}` |
| `loc_target_delete` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Delete a localization target and its config; destructive, Plan-Mode gated, lists data that will be removed. — `(target_name, delete_data=false, dry_run?) -> {ok, removed[]}` |
| `loc_gather_text` | P0 | python_run-backed TS spawning UnrealEditor-Cmd -run=GatherText (job-envelope) | net-new moat (job-envelope, Theme 0) | Run the GatherText commandlet for a target as an async job-envelope (never blocks the game thread, unlike build_*); collects new/changed source text into the manifest/archive. — `(target_name, steps=[gather]) -> {job_id, state}` |
| `loc_compile_text` | P1 | python_run-backed TS spawning compile commandlet (job-envelope) | net-new moat (job-envelope) | Compile a target's archives into .locres (the shipped binary translations) as an async job; the 'make it real in-game' step. — `(target_name, cultures?=all) -> {job_id, state}` |
| `loc_pipeline_run` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (chained job-envelope) | net-new moat | Run the full gather->export->import->compile sequence for a target as one tracked job; one-call end-to-end localization refresh. — `(target_name, cultures?=all) -> {job_id, steps[], state}` |
| `loc_job_status` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (job registry) | net-new moat (cross-DCC status pattern) | Poll a long-running localization job (gather/compile/pipeline) for state, progress, and log tail; the get_*_status gating probe required before consuming results. — `(job_id, tail_lines?) -> {state, progress, exit_code?, log_tail[]}` |
| `loc_po_export` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (GatherText ExportPO step / commandlet, job-envelope if large) | net-new moat | Export a target+culture to a PortableObject .po file for translators; the standard interchange hand-off. — `(target_name, culture_code, po_path) -> {ok, po_path, entry_count, untranslated}` |
| `loc_po_import` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (ImportPO step) | net-new moat | Import a translated .po back into the target's archive for a culture; the return leg of the translator round-trip, dry_run-able. — `(target_name, culture_code, po_path, dry_run?) -> {ok, imported, changed, conflicts[]}` |
| `loc_po_validate` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (PortableObject parser + PLUMB validator) | net-new moat (PLUMB validation) | Validate a .po file pre-import: placeholder/format-arg mismatch vs source, empty msgstr, encoding, duplicate msgid; net-new PLUMB-style quantified gate that stops corrupt translations shipping. — `(po_path, source_po?) -> {ok, errors[], warnings[], stats}` |
| `loc_validate_target` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (manifest/archive parse + validator) | net-new moat | Quantified health check of a target: stale/missing translations, unresolved source changes, placeholder-integrity per culture, untranslated counts; productizes the PLUMB validate-before/after moat for localization. — `(target_name, cultures?=all) -> {ok, per_culture[], conflicts[], placeholder_errors[], score}` |
| `loc_dashboard_report` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Aggregate dashboard across all targets: per-culture word counts, translated %, conflicts, and last-gather timestamp; the single 'where is localization at' read-out competitors have nothing like. — `(targets?=all) -> {summary, targets[{name, cultures[{code, words, translated_pct, conflicts}]}]}` |
| `loc_find_unlocalized_text` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (AssetRegistry + reflection scan) | net-new moat | Scan Blueprints/UMG/DataAssets for hardcoded non-localizable strings and FText literals missing a key; net-new audit that surfaces text the gather would never catch. — `(scan_paths?, limit?, offset?) -> {findings[{asset, property, text}], total, has_more}` |
| `loc_namespace_key_audit` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Audit String Tables and gathered manifest for duplicate keys, namespace collisions, and orphaned keys; net-new correctness tool preventing silent translation clobbering. — `(scope=string_tables\|target, target_name?) -> {duplicates[], collisions[], orphans[]}` |
| `loc_locres_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (locres reader, C++ fallback in HaybaMCPToolkit if API thin) | net-new moat | Read a compiled .locres back into key/value entries; verifies what actually shipped per culture, closing the compile loop. — `(locres_path, limit?, offset?) -> {entries[], total, has_more}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 0 job-envelope async infra (docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md) — REQUIRED for loc_gather_text/loc_compile_text/loc_pipeline_run; the GatherText commandlet is a long external process and would freeze the editor exactly like build_*/test_run if run synchronously; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor + pyTemplate factory — these 36 tools should land as descriptors, not 36x7-file wrappers; Hardened python_run handler (SEH + security gate) for all non-commandlet tools; UE Python reflection coverage: unreal.StringTable mutable API, unreal.LocalizationSettings/LocalizationTarget, unreal.InternationalizationLibrary, AssetTools StringTableFactory (pinned 5.7); UnrealEditor-Cmd binary path + project .uproject resolution for commandlet spawning; PLUMB validator + validator_run seams for loc_po_validate/loc_validate_target; Vision-capture (editor_capture_viewport/render_camera) to visually confirm preview-culture switches; PortableObject (.po) parser library or UE's PortableObjectFormatDOM via commandlet

**Risks:** String Table entry MUTATION from Python is version-variable and may be read-mostly in 5.7; set_entry/batch_set may have to route through CSV-import or a small C++ handler in HaybaMCPToolkit — fallback designed in but adds a possible rebuild; GatherText/compile commandlets are long-running external processes; running them on the game thread WILL freeze the editor (the exact Theme-0 P0 bug) — these tools are blocked until the job-envelope lands; ULocalizationTarget/LocalizationSettings Python surface is incomplete; loc_target_* fall back to direct Config/Localization/*.ini editing, which can desync from in-memory dashboard state until reload; Live preview-culture switching (loc_preview_culture_set) can destabilize editor UI / Slate text; must always offer loc_preview_culture_reset and avoid leaving the editor in a non-default culture; PO round-trip is encoding- and placeholder-fragile; non-UTF-8 or reordered {0}/%s args silently corrupt builds — mitigated by loc_po_validate as a hard pre-import gate; Commandlet spawn must use the correct engine/editor binary and not collide with a running editor instance (file locks on manifest/archive); Word-count/translated-% in dashboard depend on freshly gathered manifest; reporting stale data without a gather warning would mislead — report last-gather timestamp explicitly; Deleting targets/cultures touches on-disk archives; must be Plan-Mode gated and dry_run-first to avoid irreversible translation-data loss

**Live-editor test plan:** "On a live editor with a sample project: (1) loc_string_table_create a table, loc_string_table_set_entry several keys, loc_string_table_get to confirm read-back, loc_string_table_export_csv and diff; loc_string_table_import_csv with mode=merge and re-verify counts. (2) loc_project_cultures_get baseline, loc_project_culture_add 'fr'/'ja', confirm via get. (3) Bind a UMG widget to the String Table, loc_preview_culture_set 'fr', then editor_capture_viewport to visually confirm the text re-resolved; loc_preview_culture_reset and re-capture. (4) loc_target_create a Game target with gather paths, loc_target_get to verify config; loc_gather_text returning a job_id, poll loc_job_status until done, confirm manifest/archive written. (5) loc_po_export fr -> .po, hand-edit a msgstr (and deliberately break one placeholder), loc_po_validate to confirm the placeholder error is caught, fix it, loc_po_import (dry_run then commit), loc_compile_text, loc_locres_inspect the resulting .locres to confirm the translation shipped, then loc_preview_culture_set fr again to see it in-editor. (6) loc_validate_target and loc_dashboard_report to confirm per-culture translated_% moved and no conflicts; loc_namespace_key_audit and loc_find_unlocalized_text on the sample assets to confirm audits return sane findings. Throughout: verify dry_run returns a before-image and no mutation, and that every write was undoable (Ctrl+Z) where transaction-wrapped."


<a id="domain-27"></a>
## 27. Networking / replication / multiplayer testing

*28 tools — P0:11 P1:8 P2:6 P3:3*

UE drives multiplayer testing through ULevelEditorPlaySettings (PlayNumberOfClients, PlayNetMode, RunUnderOneProcess, bLaunchSeparateServer) which is a Python-editable CDO, plus FRequestPlaySessionParams overrides at launch — so the bulk of this domain ships as python_run wrappers with one small C++ extension to the existing StartPIE seam (HaybaMCPEditorHandler.cpp:81 currently builds an empty FRequestPlaySessionParams). The headline moat is a closed-loop RPC/property-replication assertion harness: configure single-process multi-client PIE, mutate on the authority world, tick, and assert convergence on every client world within a latency budget — no competitor (Epic baseline, Aura, Flop pie_test_bp, GenOrca, remiphilippe PIE control) does cross-world replication verification. We pair every write tool with an inspection/read-back tool (Flop pattern), wrap all replication-config mutations in FScopedTransaction, expose a dry-run for launch config, and layer NetEmulation (lag/jitter/loss) so assertions run under degraded network. Single-process (RunUnderOneProcess=true) is the load-bearing constraint: separate-process and true dedicated-server PIE spawn child processes the editor Python cannot introspect, so the harness gates on it and the assert tools refuse to run otherwise. Replication-graph and BP-variable-replication authoring are scoped as lower-priority with explicit engine-ceiling flags.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `net_pie_configure` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (LevelEditorPlaySettings CDO via unreal.get_default_object) | net-new moat (Epic/remiphilippe expose only single-player PIE start) | Set multiplayer PIE config (num clients, net mode, dedicated/listen server, run-under-one-process, server-as-client, window layout) on ULevelEditorPlaySettings — the single setup gate for all multiplayer testing. — `(num_clients:int, net_mode:Literal['standalone','listen_server','client'], dedicated_server:bool=false, run_under_one_process:bool=true, auto_connect:bool=true) -> {ok, resolved_settings}` |
| `net_pie_config_get` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Read back current multiplayer PIE settings (inspection-first pair for net_pie_configure) so the agent verifies state before launch. — `() -> {num_clients, net_mode, dedicated_server, run_under_one_process, auto_connect, separate_server}` |
| `net_pie_config_dry_run` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (Flop has bp_dry_run, none for PIE net config) | Resolve and preview the exact multiplayer launch (worlds that will spawn, roles, ports) WITHOUT launching — extends the dry_run→commit pattern to PIE. — `(overrides?:object) -> {planned_worlds:[{role,index}], warnings, single_process:bool}` |
| `net_pie_start` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (HaybaMCPEditorHandler::StartPIE — set FRequestPlaySessionParams overrides) | net-new moat | Launch multiplayer PIE honoring config, with optional inline net_mode/num_clients overrides — extends the bare StartPIE seam that today builds an empty FRequestPlaySessionParams. — `(num_clients?:int, net_mode?:str, run_under_one_process?:bool) -> {pie_started, world_count, server_role}` |
| `net_pie_stop` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (or reuse editor_stop_pie) | parity: chongdashu/remiphilippe (single-player) — extended to multi-world | Tear down the multiplayer PIE session and confirm all server/client worlds are destroyed — clean lifecycle to avoid orphaned net worlds. — `() -> {pie_stopped, worlds_remaining}` |
| `net_pie_status` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (GEditor world contexts / GetWorldContexts) | net-new moat | Enumerate live PIE worlds with their net role (server / client N), connection counts, current map, and net driver — the situational-awareness read for the harness. — `() -> {worlds:[{index, role:Literal['server','client'], net_mode, connections, map}], is_single_process:bool}` |
| `net_replicated_actors_list` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (no UE MCP enumerates per-world replication state) | List replicated actors in a chosen PIE world with role/remote-role, owner, dormancy, NetUpdateFrequency, and always-relevant flag — paginated discovery of what is actually on the wire. — `(world:Literal['server','client'], index:int=0, class_filter?:str, limit:int=50, offset:int=0) -> {actors:[{name,class,role,remote_role,dormancy,owner}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `net_actor_replication_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (reflection) | net-new moat | Deep-inspect one actor's replication: replicated props with COND_* conditions, RepNotify funcs, RPC list with net flags, NetUpdateFrequency, priority, dormancy, NetGUID, replicate-movement — inspection-first pair for the config setters. — `(actor_path:str, world:Literal['server','client']='server', index:int=0) -> {replicates, replicate_movement, net_update_frequency, dormancy, replicated_properties:[{name,condition,repnotify}], rpcs:[{name,kind,reliable}], net_guid}` |
| `net_class_replication_brief` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (reflection) | net-new moat | Static reflection brief of a Blueprint/class: which properties replicate (+ conditions), and every RPC with kind (Server/Client/NetMulticast) and reliability — no PIE required, answers 'is this class wired for net' offline. — `(class_or_bp_path:str) -> {replicated_properties:[{name,condition}], rpcs:[{name,kind:Literal['server','client','multicast'],reliable:bool}]}` |
| `net_replicated_components_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | List components on an actor with bReplicates set, plus their replicated subobject status — closes the component-replication blind spot. — `(actor_path:str, world:Literal['server','client']='server', index:int=0) -> {components:[{name,class,replicates}]}` |
| `net_assert_property_replicated` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (job-envelope tick loop over server+client worlds) | net-new moat (Flop pie_test_bp asserts single-world only) | THE core moat: set a replicated property on the authority actor, tick, and assert each client copy converges to the value within a latency budget; reports per-client value + ticks/ms to converge. — `(actor_path:str, property:str, value:any, timeout_ms:int=2000) -> {pass:bool, server_value, clients:[{index,value,converged,latency_ms}]}` |
| `net_assert_rpc_received` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (temp delegate/counter injection in receiver world) | net-new moat | Instrument an RPC: install a temp counter/hook on the receiver side, invoke on the caller, and assert the handler fired the expected number of times with the expected net flags. — `(actor_path:str, rpc_name:str, args?:array, expect_calls:int=1, timeout_ms:int=2000) -> {pass:bool, calls_observed, kind, reliable}` |
| `net_assert_authority` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Assert HasAuthority/role expectations for an actor across server and client worlds (e.g. ROLE_Authority on server, ROLE_SimulatedProxy on clients) — catches authority/ownership bugs. — `(actor_path:str, expect_server:str='authority', expect_client:str='simulated_proxy') -> {pass:bool, server_role, client_roles:[{index,role}]}` |
| `net_assert_relevancy` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Assert an actor is (or is not) net-relevant to a given client connection via IsNetRelevantFor — verifies culling/relevancy logic that silently breaks gameplay. — `(actor_path:str, client_index:int, expect_relevant:bool=true) -> {pass:bool, is_relevant, distance}` |
| `net_assert_dormancy` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Assert an actor's NetDormancy state matches expected (Awake/DormantAll/Initial) — verifies dormancy-driven bandwidth optimizations behave. — `(actor_path:str, expect:Literal['awake','dormant_all','dormant_partial','initial']) -> {pass:bool, actual}` |
| `net_replication_harness_run` | P0 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat (parity-plus vs Flop assertion blocks) | Run a declared suite of replication assertions (property/rpc/authority/relevancy) in one call and return a pass/fail report — the outcome verb that composes the asserts for CI-style regression. — `(suite:[{type,actor_path,...}], stop_on_fail:bool=false) -> {passed:int, failed:int, results:[{name,pass,detail}]}` |
| `net_emulation_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (NetEmulation.* console cmds per PIE world) | net-new moat | Apply network emulation (added lag, jitter, packet loss, bandwidth cap) to live PIE connections so replication assertions run under degraded conditions — exposes built-in profiles (Average/Bad) or custom. — `(lag_ms?:int, jitter_ms?:int, packet_loss_pct?:float, incoming?:bool=true, outgoing?:bool=true, profile?:Literal['average','bad','custom']) -> {ok, applied}` |
| `net_emulation_clear` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Reset all network emulation on PIE connections to a clean baseline — pair for net_emulation_set. — `() -> {ok}` |
| `net_connection_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS | parity: remiphilippe (player control) — extended to net connections | List NetConnections on the server PIE world (per-client addr, ping/RTT, state, owning PlayerController) — connection-level telemetry for debugging. — `(index:int=0, limit:int=50, offset:int=0) -> {connections:[{client_index,ping_ms,state,player_controller}], has_more}` |
| `net_actor_set_replication` | P1 | python_run-backed TS + FScopedTransaction (4C seam) | net-new moat | Set actor-level replication knobs (bReplicates, bReplicateMovement, NetUpdateFrequency, NetPriority, NetDormancy, bAlwaysRelevant) on a class/CDO — transaction-wrapped, with read-back. — `(class_or_bp_path:str, replicates?:bool, replicate_movement?:bool, net_update_frequency?:float, dormancy?:str, always_relevant?:bool) -> {ok, applied, undo_id}` |
| `net_component_set_replicates` | P2 | python_run-backed TS + FScopedTransaction | net-new moat | Toggle a component's bReplicates and replicated-subobject registration on a Blueprint/actor — transaction-wrapped. — `(actor_or_bp_path:str, component:str, replicates:bool) -> {ok, undo_id}` |
| `net_property_set_replication` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit blueprint module (FBPVariableDescription edit) — flag engine ceiling | net-new moat | Mark a Blueprint variable as replicated, set its replication condition, and optionally bind a RepNotify — the BP-variable replication authoring gap (engine-limited via Python). — `(bp_path:str, variable:str, replicated:bool, condition?:str, repnotify_func?:str) -> {ok, undo_id, engine_limited?:bool}` |
| `net_simulate_disconnect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (UNetConnection::Close) | net-new moat | Force-close a specific client's NetConnection to exercise reconnection, cleanup, and ownership-transfer logic — fault-injection for resilience tests. — `(client_index:int, graceful:bool=false) -> {ok, connections_remaining}` |
| `net_drive_client_input` | P2 | python_run-backed TS | parity: remiphilippe (PIE player control) — extended to per-client targeting | Drive input / possession on a chosen PIE client (move, fire, custom action) to trigger client-prediction and server-reconciliation paths the harness then asserts on. — `(client_index:int, action:str, params?:object, duration_ms?:int) -> {ok}` |
| `net_stats_capture` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (console stat parsing) | net-new moat | Capture network telemetry (bytes in/out, packets, saturation, per-connection ping) over a sample window via stat net / Net.Stats — quantitative bandwidth read. — `(sample_ms:int=1000) -> {bytes_in, bytes_out, packets_in, packets_out, per_connection:[{client_index,ping_ms,saturation}]}` |
| `net_replication_graph_inspect` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (reflection) — requires project ReplicationGraph enabled | net-new moat | Report the active UReplicationGraph driver: node tree, spatialization grid config, and per-class routing policies — visibility into the replication-graph layer no MCP touches. — `() -> {driver_class, nodes:[{class,policy}], grid:{cell_size,spatial_bias}, class_policies:[{class,policy}]}` |
| `net_replication_graph_set_class_policy` | P3 | python_run-backed TS / C++ HaybaMCPToolkit — flag engine ceiling | net-new moat | Set a class's replication-graph routing policy (spatialize / always-relevant / relevant-to-owner) on the graph settings — config-level graph authoring (engine ceiling). — `(class_path:str, policy:Literal['spatialize','always_relevant','relevant_owner','never']) -> {ok, undo_id, engine_limited?:bool}` |
| `net_seamless_travel_test` | P3 | python_run-backed TS | net-new moat | Trigger ServerTravel/seamless travel and assert all clients follow to the new map with player state intact — verifies the travel path that breaks silently. — `(map:str, seamless:bool=true, timeout_ms:int=5000) -> {pass:bool, clients_followed:int, map_after}` |

**Dependencies:** THEME 0 job-envelope conversion: net_assert_* and net_replication_harness_run run tick loops; they MUST be async job-envelopes, not blocking calls, or they re-introduce the test_run game-thread freeze; Hardened python_run handler (SEH + security gate) — most tools are pyTemplate descriptors dispatched through it; ProcessCommand SEH wrap (Theme 0) so a bad reflection call cannot crash the editor; C++ extension of HaybaMCPEditorHandler::StartPIE (currently builds empty FRequestPlaySessionParams at line 86) for net_pie_start overrides; FScopedTransaction begin/end seam from 4C for the net_*_set_* mutators and undo_id return; Multi-world PIE access from editor Python — requires RunUnderOneProcess=true so server + client worlds coexist in the editor process (GEditor world contexts); UE 4.26+ NetEmulation console subsystem for net_emulation_*; Project-level UReplicationGraph enabled for the two graph tools (otherwise they return engine_limited); Add-a-tool refactor (Theme 1) pyTemplate factory so ~24 of these ship with zero plugin rebuild; Vision loop (4B) optional: PIE screenshot per client world for before/after replication diffs

**Risks:** Single-process gate is load-bearing: separate-process PIE and true dedicated-server PIE spawn CHILD PROCESSES the editor Python cannot introspect — the assertion harness must hard-refuse (clear machine code) when run_under_one_process=false, or assertions silently pass against a non-existent world; RPC interception (net_assert_rpc_received) has no clean Python hook — relies on temp delegate/counter injection or BP-event instrumentation; reliable only for BP-exposed RPCs, native C++ RPCs may be unobservable (flag as limitation); Ticking PIE worlds from a command handler risks game-thread re-entrancy / the test_run freeze class; assertions must yield via the ticker dispatch, never spin-wait; Convergence-window assertions are timing-sensitive: under net_emulation lag the default timeout_ms can false-fail; budgets must scale with applied lag; net_property_set_replication (BP variable replication flags) is genuinely hard via UE Python — likely needs C++ FBPVariableDescription edits + BP recompile; high churn, flag engine ceiling and ship P2; Replication-graph reflection is fragile across engine versions and only valid if the project ships a graph driver — gate behind a get-status probe; Driving client input / possession across worlds can desync the editor selection and PIE focus; isolate to the target client world context

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live editor with a sample replicated project (replicated Character with a Health float + a Server RPC): 1) net_pie_configure(num_clients=2, net_mode='listen_server', run_under_one_process=true); net_pie_config_get to confirm; net_pie_config_dry_run to preview 1 server + 2 client worlds. 2) net_pie_start; net_pie_status shows server + 2 clients in-process. 3) net_replicated_actors_list(world='server') finds the characters; net_actor_replication_inspect on one shows Health replicated + the RPC with kind='server'. 4) net_assert_authority (server=authority, clients=simulated_proxy) passes. 5) net_assert_property_replicated(Health, 50) — clients converge within budget; record latency_ms. 6) net_emulation_set(lag_ms=200, packet_loss_pct=5) then re-run the Health assert with a scaled timeout — still converges. 7) net_assert_rpc_received for the Server RPC after net_drive_client_input fires it. 8) net_assert_relevancy after moving an actor far from a client. 9) net_replication_harness_run with a 4-assert suite, confirm aggregated pass/fail. 10) net_simulate_disconnect(client_index=1); net_connection_list shows one remaining. 11) net_emulation_clear; net_pie_stop; net_pie_status shows zero worlds. Negative test: net_pie_configure(run_under_one_process=false) then net_assert_property_replicated must return a clear 'single_process_required' error, not a false pass.


<a id="domain-28"></a>
## 28. Unreal Insights / Trace Profiling

*34 tools — P0:6 P1:14 P2:12 P3:2*

UE exposes all trace control through console commands (`Trace.*`, `csvProfile`, `stat startfile`, `MemReport`) executed via `GEngine->Exec(..., *GLog)` — which the plugin already does via `editor_run_console_command`. So ~80% of this domain ships as python_run/console-backed TS wrappers with NO plugin rebuild, where the real value is outcome-verbs (one-call "capture 10s of CPU+GPU+frame trace to a .utrace") and readback/analysis (parse the .utrace/.csv/.memreport artifacts in `Saved/Profiling/`) that no competitor offers. Console commands return nothing to the caller, so status/verify is done by log-file scrape or filesystem polling of the growing artifact; for production-grade status/channel enumeration I propose ONE small C++ seam over `FTraceAuxiliary`/`UE::Trace` in HaybaMCPToolkit. This is net-new moat territory: no UE MCP (Epic, Aura, Flop, GenOrca, remiphilippe) ships trace-session capture at all — pairing capture with artifact analysis and the vision loop makes Hayba the only "measure-before/after-you-mutate" UE agent. Everything lands behind Code-Mode/packs with inspection-first read tools and a dry_run on the only destructive op (capture deletion).

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `trace_start` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (editor_run_console_command 'Trace.Start'/'Trace.File'); robust variant FTraceAuxiliary::Start in HaybaMCPToolkit | net-new moat (no UE MCP has trace capture) | Start a trace recording to a .utrace file with a chosen channel set — the core capture entrypoint so an agent can profile a scenario on demand. — `(channels?:string[], preset?:Literal['cpu','gpu','memory','frame','loadtime','full'], dest_file?:string, host?:string) -> {ok, dest, channels, trace_id}` |
| `trace_stop` | P0 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Stop' + Saved/Profiling stat readback) | net-new moat | Stop the active trace session and flush the .utrace to disk — closes a capture so the artifact is openable/analyzable. — `() -> {ok, dest_file, bytes, duration_s}` |
| `trace_status` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (FTraceAuxiliary::GetTraceDestinationString + UE::Trace channel enum); python fallback = run 'Trace.Status' then scrape latest log | net-new moat | Read back whether a trace is active, its dest, enabled channels, bytes-written and connection state — inspection-first verify so the agent knows a capture is really running. — `() -> {active, dest, channels[], bytes, paused, endpoint}` |
| `trace_channels_list` | P0 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (UE::Trace::EnumerateChannels); python fallback = 'Trace.Channels' + log scrape | net-new moat | Enumerate all available trace channels and which are currently enabled — introspection so the agent picks valid channel names instead of guessing. — `(filter?:string, limit?:number, offset?:number) -> {channels:[{name,enabled,description}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `trace_channel_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Enable'/'Trace.Disable' per channel) | net-new moat | Batch enable/disable named trace channels on a live or pre-start session — outcome-verb for tuning capture overhead vs detail. — `(enable?:string[], disable?:string[]) -> {ok, enabled[], disabled[], unknown[]}` |
| `trace_preset_apply` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (curated channel map -> Trace.Enable) | net-new moat | Apply a named channel preset (cpu/gpu/memory/frame/loadtime/contextswitch/full) in one call — outcome-verb that hides channel-name minutiae behind intent. — `(preset:Literal['cpu','gpu','memory','frame','loadtime','contextswitch','full'], replace?:boolean) -> {ok, channels[]}` |
| `trace_snapshot` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.SnapshotFile') | net-new moat | Write the in-memory trace ring buffer to a .utrace snapshot file without stopping the session — captures 'the last N seconds' the moment a hitch is seen. — `(dest_file?:string) -> {ok, dest_file, bytes}` |
| `trace_bookmark` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Bookmark') | net-new moat | Insert a named bookmark into the active trace timeline — lets the agent mark 'spawned 1000 actors here' so Insights regions are navigable. — `(name:string) -> {ok, name}` |
| `trace_region` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (CSV_EVENT / Trace region console hooks) | net-new moat | Open/close a named timing region (begin/end) spanning a block of work — outcome markers for measuring a specific operation's cost in the trace. — `(action:Literal['begin','end'], name:string) -> {ok}` |
| `trace_pause` | P2 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Pause'/'Trace.Resume') | net-new moat | Pause or resume event emission on the active trace — toggle to skip uninteresting spans and keep the .utrace small. — `(resume?:boolean) -> {ok, paused}` |
| `trace_send` | P2 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Send') | net-new moat | Stream trace events live to a running Unreal Insights / trace store over the network instead of a file — for live profiling sessions. — `(host:string, channels?:string[]) -> {ok, endpoint, channels[]}` |
| `trace_screenshot` | P3 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Screenshot') | net-new moat (extends Hayba vision loop) | Attach a viewport screenshot to the current trace timeline — ties the vision-loop moat into the profile so frames are visually identifiable in Insights. — `(name?:string) -> {ok, name}` |
| `frame_trace_quick` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (Trace.Start -> wait_for_idle/sleep -> Trace.Stop) | net-new moat | One-call timed capture: start cpu+gpu+frame trace, run for N seconds, stop, return the .utrace path — the killer convenience verb for 'profile the current scene'. — `(duration_s?:number, channels?:string[], open_after?:boolean) -> {ok, dest_file, duration_s, channels[]}` |
| `perf_capture_pie` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (editor_start_pie + trace_start + csv_profile_analyze) | net-new moat (remiphilippe has PIE, none trace it) | Orchestrated capture during PIE: launch PIE, trace for a duration, stop PIE and trace, return artifact + frame summary — measures real gameplay perf, not editor idle. — `(duration_s:number, preset?:string, map?:string) -> {ok, dest_file, frame_summary}` |
| `trace_open_insights` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (subprocess on EngineDir/Binaries/.../UnrealInsights.exe); or Node child_process in sidecar | net-new moat | Launch UnrealInsights.exe on a .utrace capture (or the latest) so a human can drill in — bridges agent capture to the Insights GUI. — `(utrace_path?:string, latest?:boolean) -> {ok, launched, path}` |
| `trace_list_captures` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (os.scandir on Saved/Profiling) | net-new moat | List .utrace captures in Saved/Profiling with size, mtime and duration — paginated inspection so the agent can pick a capture to open/analyze/delete. — `(limit?:number, offset?:number, sort?:Literal['mtime','size','name']) -> {captures:[{path,bytes,modified,duration_s}], has_more, next_offset}` |
| `trace_capture_get` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (TraceServices header parse) / C++ HaybaMCPToolkit for deep parse | net-new moat | Return metadata for a single .utrace (channels recorded, frame count, duration, platform) — readback before opening/analyzing. — `(utrace_path:string) -> {path, bytes, channels[], frames, duration_s, engine_version}` |
| `trace_delete_captures` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (os.remove guarded by dry_run) | net-new moat | Delete selected or stale .utrace/.csv/.memreport artifacts with a dry_run preview — the one destructive op, gated by Plan-Mode and dry_run. — `(paths?:string[], older_than_days?:number, dry_run?:boolean) -> {deleted[], freed_bytes, would_delete[]}` |
| `trace_analyze` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (unreal Trace Analysis API / trace2csv) or C++ HaybaMCPToolkit TraceServices reader | net-new moat (Houdini graph-intel analog, none in UE) | Parse a captured .utrace and return top CPU timers, GPU pass costs, frame-time stats and hitch list — net-new closed-loop analysis so the agent acts on numbers, not screenshots. — `(utrace_path?:string, top_n?:number, group?:Literal['cpu','gpu','frame']) -> {frame:{avg_ms,p99_ms,max_ms,hitches}, top_timers:[{name,incl_ms,calls}]}` |
| `csv_profile_start` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('csvProfile start'/'startfile') | net-new moat | Start the CSV profiler (optionally to a named file) to record per-frame stat columns — lightweight always-on perf capture across a scenario. — `(dest_file?:string, metadata?:Record<string,string>) -> {ok, dest_file}` |
| `csv_profile_stop` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('csvProfile stop') | net-new moat | Stop the CSV profiler and flush the .csv to Saved/Profiling/CSV — closes a CSV capture for analysis. — `() -> {ok, dest_file, rows}` |
| `csv_profile_event` | P2 | python_run-backed TS ('csvProfile event') | net-new moat | Insert a named CSV event marker into the live profile — annotates the timeline so analysis can segment by operation. — `(name:string) -> {ok, name}` |
| `csv_profile_analyze` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (csv parse + numpy-free percentile) | net-new moat | Parse a CSV-profiler file into frame-time percentiles, hitch count, and worst stat columns — net-new readback that turns raw CSV into an actionable verdict. — `(csv_path?:string, hitch_ms?:number, top_n?:number) -> {frames, avg_ms, p50_ms, p99_ms, max_ms, hitches, worst_columns:[{name,avg,max}]}` |
| `csv_metadata_set` | P3 | python_run-backed TS ('csvProfile metadata') | net-new moat | Set CSV profiler metadata key/values (build, map, settings) attached to the capture — labels runs so A/B perf comparisons are traceable. — `(metadata:Record<string,string>) -> {ok, metadata}` |
| `stat_file_start` | P2 | python_run-backed TS ('stat startfile') | net-new moat | Begin a stats-file (.ue4stats) capture via 'stat startfile' — records the full stat system for offline StatsViewer/Insights import. — `(dest_file?:string) -> {ok, dest_file}` |
| `stat_file_stop` | P2 | python_run-backed TS ('stat stopfile') | net-new moat | Stop the stats-file capture and flush .ue4stats — closes a stat capture. — `() -> {ok, dest_file, bytes}` |
| `stat_named_events_toggle` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('stat namedevents'/'-statnamedevents') | net-new moat | Toggle 'stat namedevents' so stat scopes emit named CPU-trace events — the stat-to-trace bridge that makes timers readable in Insights. — `(enable:boolean, verbose?:boolean) -> {ok, enabled}` |
| `stat_dump_hitches` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (console + log scrape) | net-new moat | Run 'stat dumphitches' and return recent frame hitches with timing — quick hitch readback without a full trace. — `(threshold_ms?:number) -> {hitches:[{frame, ms}], count}` |
| `memtrace_start` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('Trace.Enable memalloc,callstack,module' + Trace.File); note: callstacks need -trace launch arg | net-new moat | Start a memory trace (memalloc + callstack + module channels) to a .utrace — enables MemInsights allocation/leak analysis in one verb. — `(callstacks?:boolean, dest_file?:string) -> {ok, dest_file, channels[]}` |
| `mem_report` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('MemReport -full' + parse .memreport in Saved/Profiling) | net-new moat | Run 'MemReport -full' and parse the summary (total/physical/streaming/texture/mesh pools) — net-new memory readback for budget checks. — `(full?:boolean) -> {report_path, summary:{physical_mb, streaming_mb, textures_mb, meshes_mb, top_classes[]}}` |
| `llm_snapshot` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (console + log scrape; LLM needs -llm launch arg) | net-new moat | Toggle Low-Level Memory Tracker and snapshot category totals ('stat LLM' / 'LLM.DumpAllocs') — per-subsystem memory attribution. — `(action:Literal['enable','snapshot'], tag_set?:string) -> {ok, categories:[{name, mb}]}` |
| `gpu_trace_frame` | P1 | python_run-backed TS ('ProfileGPU' + Trace.Enable gpu + Trace.Snapshot + log scrape) | extends Hayba gpu_profile; net-new for trace | Capture a single-frame GPU profile ('ProfileGPU') plus a gpu-channel trace snapshot — net-new GPU pass-cost readback complementing existing gpu_profile. — `(dest_file?:string) -> {ok, dest_file, passes:[{name, ms}]}` |
| `trace_store_status` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (probe UnrealTraceServer port/dir) / Node sidecar | net-new moat | Check whether UnrealTraceServer (the trace store) is running and list live sessions/stores — environment introspection so the agent knows where captures land. — `() -> {server_running, store_dir, sessions:[{name, bytes, live}]}` |
| `trace_config_get` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (read cmdline + CVars via unreal) / C++ HaybaMCPToolkit | net-new moat | Report current trace configuration: active channels, dest mode (file/network), -trace launch args, and relevant CVars — inspection-first before starting a session. — `() -> {launch_trace_args[], default_channels[], save_dir, cvars:Record<string,string>}` |

**Dependencies:** editor_run_console_command (existing C++ handler — GEngine->Exec into GLog) is the backbone for every Trace.*/csvProfile/stat command; editor_stream_log / GetOutputLog (existing) for scraping console-command output since Exec returns nothing to the caller; Theme 0 job-envelope/async fix: frame_trace_quick & perf_capture_pie run for N seconds and MUST NOT block the game thread — depend on the async command conversion (docs/audit/2026-06-22-mcp-async-command-conversions.md); python_run hardened handler (SEH + security gate) for filesystem ops on Saved/Profiling and subprocess launch of UnrealInsights.exe; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor + pyTemplate factory so these ~34 wrappers ship as descriptors without 7-file churn; editor_start_pie + wait_for_idle (existing) for perf_capture_pie orchestration; ONE optional C++ seam in HaybaMCPToolkit over FTraceAuxiliary / UE::Trace::EnumerateChannels + TraceServices reader for robust trace_status/trace_channels_list/trace_analyze (python fallbacks exist); UnrealInsights.exe + UnrealTraceServer binaries shipped in EngineDir/Binaries/Win64 (path discovery); Code-Mode/deferred routing + a new 'trace' pack in packs.yaml so the 34 tools stay off the eager surface

**Risks:** Console commands return nothing to the caller (Exec writes only to GLog) — every readback (status/channels/hitches/gpu passes) relies on log-file scraping, which is timing/format-fragile across UE versions; mitigate with the C++ FTraceAuxiliary/TraceServices seam for the load-bearing reads; Several deep captures require LAUNCH-TIME args that can't be toggled at runtime: memalloc callstacks need -trace=memory/-statnamedevents, LLM needs -llm — memtrace_start/llm_snapshot must detect-and-warn when the editor wasn't launched with them rather than silently producing empty traces; Timed captures (frame_trace_quick, perf_capture_pie) are exactly the game-thread-blocking pattern Theme 0 bans — they MUST use the async job envelope or they reintroduce editor freezes; Parsing .utrace is non-trivial: the binary format is internal; trace_analyze likely needs the TraceServices C++ analysis API or shelling trace utilities — pure-python parsing may only reach header metadata (flag the analysis depth honestly, no false advertising); Starting a trace while one is already active, or with the network/file dest mismatched, can error or split files — trace_start must check trace_status first and surface a clear machine code; CSV/stat file locations vary (Saved/Profiling vs Saved/Profiling/CSV) and large captures can fill disk — trace_delete_captures and quota awareness needed; dry_run on delete is mandatory; UnrealInsights.exe / UnrealTraceServer may not be present in a packaged/minimal install — trace_open_insights/trace_store_status must degrade gracefully; Profiling overhead itself perturbs measurements (observer effect) — preset choices should warn when 'full' channels are enabled for steady-state perf claims

**Live-editor test plan:** Live-editor validation on the pinned 5.7 build with HaybaMCPToolkit loaded: 1) trace_channels_list -> assert known channels (cpu,gpu,frame,memory,loadtime) appear and 'enabled' flags toggle after trace_channel_set/trace_preset_apply. 2) frame_trace_quick(duration_s=5, open_after=false) on a populated map -> assert a .utrace appears in Saved/Profiling with non-zero bytes and trace_status showed active during the window then inactive after; confirm editor did NOT freeze (concurrent wait_for_idle responds). 3) trace_analyze(latest) -> assert frame.avg_ms/p99_ms are plausible (>0, p99>=avg) and top_timers non-empty; cross-check by trace_open_insights and eyeballing the same timeline in the GUI. 4) csv_profile_start -> spawn/destroy actors via batch tools -> csv_profile_event('spawned') -> csv_profile_stop -> csv_profile_analyze: assert hitch count rises around the event row and worst_columns lists a renderer/gamethread stat. 5) mem_report(full=true) -> assert summary physical_mb matches 'stat memory' order-of-magnitude; memtrace_start with -trace not set -> assert it returns a clear 'launch-arg-missing' warning rather than an empty trace. 6) gpu_trace_frame -> assert passes[] includes BasePass/Lumen/Shadows with ms>0. 7) trace_list_captures pagination (limit=2) -> has_more/next_offset correct; trace_delete_captures(older_than_days=999, dry_run=true) -> would_delete lists files, deletes nothing; dry_run=false removes them and freed_bytes>0. 8) Negative: trace_start while already tracing -> structured error code; trace_open_insights with missing binary -> graceful {launched:false}. Compare a before/after capture across a known-heavy edit to prove the closed measure-mutate-measure loop.


<a id="domain-29"></a>
## 29. Virtual Textures — Runtime Virtual Texture

*32 tools — P0:11 P1:12 P2:8 P3:1*

VT today is a single read (texture_streaming_report) plus generic texture_set_settings that can flip one bool — there is no way for an agent to author the RVT pipeline end-to-end (asset → volume → bounds-align to landscape → producers writing → material sample/output → build streaming mips), nor to safely bulk-convert textures to SVT. This is a net-new moat domain: no competitor (Epic baseline, Aura, Flop, GenOrca, UAIP) ships VT authoring, and it is high-value for the worldbuilding/landscape story. Strategy: ship the whole RVT loop and SVT batch-convert as python_run-backed pyTemplate wrappers (zero plugin rebuild — UE EditorAssetLibrary/MaterialEditingLibrary cover all of it), reserve only the long-running build/streamed-mip generation for a C++ job-envelope so it never freezes the game thread. Lean hard on the moats: every mutator gets a paired *_inspect read, a dry_run preview, transaction-wrapping, and a PLUMB-style vt_validate that quantifies pipeline correctness (content-type ↔ material output match, writers present, bounds aligned, pool sized) before/after mutate, plus vision-loop debug-viz capture so the agent can see the VT actually rendering.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vt_check_support` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; reads DefaultEngine.ini / r.VirtualTextures cvars) | net-new moat (Houdini-style get_*_status gating probe, none in UE peers) | Read whether r.VirtualTextures + RVT support are enabled in project settings (gating probe so agents fail fast before authoring); also reports VT tile/pool config — `() -> {virtual_texture_support:bool, rvt_support:bool, anisotropic:bool, tile_size:int, pool_mb:int, needs_restart:bool}` |
| `vt_enable_project_support` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; writes config + unreal.SystemLibrary) | net-new moat | Enable VirtualTextureSupport / RVT support in project settings (DefaultEngine.ini) — the prerequisite no other tool sets, returns restart-required flag — `(enable_rvt?:bool, enable_anisotropic?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, changed:[str], needs_restart:bool}` |
| `rvt_asset_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; unreal.AssetToolsHelpers + RuntimeVirtualTexture factory) | net-new moat | Create a URuntimeVirtualTexture asset with a chosen material content type — the root object the whole RVT pipeline hangs off — `(path, material_type:Literal[BaseColor\|BaseColor_Normal_Roughness\|BaseColor_Normal_Specular\|BaseColor_Normal_Specular_YCoCg\|BaseColor_Normal_Specular_Mask_YCoCg\|WorldHeight\|Displacement], tile_size?:int=256, tile_count?:int, lossy_compress?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, path, content_type, exists}` |
| `rvt_asset_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; reflective set_editor_property) | net-new moat | Set properties on an existing RVT asset (content type, tile size/count/border, page table format, compress) in one outcome-call, transaction-wrapped — `(path, properties:{...alias map: material_type,tile_size,tile_count,tile_border,lossy_compress,enable_compress_crunch,remove_low_mips}, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, applied:[str], before, after}` |
| `rvt_asset_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; AssetRegistry referencers) | net-new moat (Flop inspection-first pattern) | Read one RVT asset: content type, tile size/count, page table format, est. VRAM footprint, and how many volumes/materials reference it (inspection-first paired read) — `(path) -> {path, content_type, tile_size, tile_count, page_table_format, est_vram_mb, volume_refs:[str], material_refs:[str]}` |
| `rvt_asset_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; AssetRegistry by class) | net-new moat | Enumerate URuntimeVirtualTexture assets under a path prefix with content type + ref counts, paginated — `(path_prefix?, limit?:int=50, offset?:int) -> {items:[{path,content_type,referenced:bool}], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `rvt_asset_delete` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; EditorAssetLibrary.delete_asset) | net-new moat | Delete an RVT asset, refusing (or force) when volumes/materials still reference it to avoid dangling pipeline — `(path, force?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, deleted, blocking_refs:[str]}` |
| `rvt_volume_spawn` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; EditorActorSubsystem.spawn_actor_from_class) | net-new moat | Spawn an ARuntimeVirtualTextureVolume actor and bind it to an RVT asset (and optional bounds-align actor) — places the RVT in the world — `(rvt_asset, name?, bounds_align_actor?, location?, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, actor_path, rvt_asset, aligned:bool}` |
| `rvt_volume_set_bounds` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; URuntimeVirtualTextureComponent.set_bounds / SetRotation+SetBounds) | net-new moat | Align an RVT volume's bounds to its bounds-align actor (usually the Landscape) and snap to tile grid — the single most error-prone manual step, now one call — `(volume, bounds_align_actor?, snap_to_landscape?:bool=true, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, bounds_min, bounds_max, aligned_to}` |
| `rvt_volume_set` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; reflective set on RuntimeVirtualTextureComponent) | net-new moat | Set RVT volume/component properties (virtual_texture asset swap, bounds_align_actor, snap_to_landscape_height_modify, num_streaming_mips, lod, hide in editor) in one outcome-call — `(volume, properties:{...}, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, applied:[str], before, after}` |
| `rvt_volume_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Read one RVT volume: bound RVT asset, bounds box, align actor, streaming-mip count, whether build is stale (paired read) — `(volume) -> {actor_path, rvt_asset, bounds_min, bounds_max, bounds_align_actor, num_streaming_mips, build_stale:bool, writer_count:int}` |
| `rvt_volume_list` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; EditorActorSubsystem.get_all_level_actors filtered) | net-new moat | List all RVT volume actors in the current level with their bound asset + align status, paginated — `(limit?:int=50, offset?:int) -> {items:[{actor_path,rvt_asset,aligned:bool}], count, has_more, next_offset}` |
| `rvt_volume_delete` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate) | net-new moat | Delete RVT volume actor(s) by path/selection, dry-run aware — `(volumes:[str]\|str, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, deleted:[str]}` |
| `rvt_landscape_bind` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; Landscape.runtime_virtual_textures array) | net-new moat | Add an RVT asset to a Landscape's RuntimeVirtualTextures array and set its render-pass type — makes the landscape a producer (the canonical RVT use case) — `(landscape, rvt_asset, render_pass:Literal[Default\|RuntimeVirtualTexture\|None]=RuntimeVirtualTexture, num_lods?:int, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, landscape, bound_rvts:[str]}` |
| `rvt_primitive_enable_write` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; component reflective set, batch over selection) | net-new moat | Add an RVT to a static-mesh/primitive component's RuntimeVirtualTextures array + set VirtualTextureRenderPassType so the mesh writes into the RVT (e.g. roads/decals into terrain blend) — `(actor_or_component:[str]\|str, rvt_asset, pass_type:Literal[Default\|RuntimeVirtualTexture\|None], draw_in_main_pass?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, updated:[str]}` |
| `rvt_audit_writers` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; scan level components) | net-new moat | List every primitive/landscape in the level that writes to a given RVT asset, with pass type — read-back/verify that producers are actually wired — `(rvt_asset) -> {writers:[{actor,component,pass_type,draw_in_main_pass}], count}` |
| `rvt_material_add_output` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; MaterialEditingLibrary.create_material_expression RuntimeVirtualTextureOutput) | net-new moat | Add a RuntimeVirtualTextureOutput expression node to a material and wire base-color/normal/roughness/specular/worldheight pins matching the RVT content type — the write side of RVT materials — `(material, connect:{basecolor?,normal?,roughness?,specular?,mask?,worldheight?}\|auto, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, node_guid, connected_pins:[str], compile_deferred:bool}` |
| `rvt_material_add_sample` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; create RuntimeVirtualTextureSample expression) | net-new moat | Add a RuntimeVirtualTextureSample node bound to an RVT asset and expose its outputs — the read side (e.g. landscape material sampling baked RVT) — `(material, rvt_asset, material_type?:auto, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, node_guid, output_pins:[str]}` |
| `rvt_material_inspect` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; iterate material expressions) | net-new moat | Report RVT-related nodes in a material (sample/output expressions, bound assets, content-type mismatch vs the RVT asset) — inspection paired read before/after material edits — `(material) -> {outputs:[{guid,connected_pins}], samples:[{guid,rvt_asset,type_match:bool}], mismatches:[str]}` |
| `rvt_volume_build_streamed_mips` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (job-envelope, mirrors HaybaMCPBuildHandler async pattern; RuntimeVirtualTextureSetBounds + BuildStreamedMips) | net-new moat (vs Epic's game-thread-serial build that freezes) | Build/refresh the streaming low-mips (SVT) for an RVT volume so distant terrain has data without runtime render — long-running, runs as a non-blocking job-envelope (the freeze-safe path) — `(volumes:[str]\|all, num_mips?:int) -> {job_id, status:Literal[queued\|running\|done\|error], built:[str]}` |
| `rvt_build_status` | P1 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (job-envelope status) | net-new moat | Poll the streamed-mip / SVT build job started by build_streamed_mips or svt_build (get_*_status gate) — `(job_id) -> {status, progress:float, log_tail:[str], error?}` |
| `svt_enable` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; set_editor_property virtual_texture_streaming + UpdateResource) | partial self-overlap (texture_set_settings can flip the bool) — this adds verify+footprint+txn | Enable Streaming Virtual Texture (VirtualTextureStreaming=true) on a single texture, transaction-wrapped with read-back of the resulting format/footprint — `(path, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, path, was, now:true, est_vram_before_mb, est_vram_after_mb}` |
| `svt_batch_convert` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; AssetRegistry filter + loop, batch) | matches Roblox mass_set_property / net-new for UE VT | Bulk-convert all textures above a size threshold under a path to SVT (the real production workflow) — dry-run lists candidates first, then applies with per-asset result — `(path_prefix?, min_size?:int=2048, include_groups?:[str], exclude_normal_maps?:bool, dry_run?:bool=true, limit?:int) -> {candidates\|converted:[{path,size,ok}], count, skipped:[{path,reason}]}` |
| `svt_revert` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; batch reflective set) | net-new moat | Disable VT streaming on texture(s) (revert to regular streaming) — the undo-path for an over-eager batch convert, dry-run aware — `(paths:[str]\|str, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, reverted:[str], failed:[{path,reason}]}` |
| `svt_set_auto_threshold` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; DefaultEngine.ini [/Script/Engine.RendererSettings]) | net-new moat | Set the project Auto-VirtualTexture size threshold + tile size in project settings so new large imports auto-convert (set-and-forget governance) — `(auto_vt_size?:int, vt_tile_size?:int, enable_auto?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, changed:[str], needs_restart:bool}` |
| `svt_build` | P2 | C++ HaybaMCPToolkit (job-envelope; reuses rvt_build_status) | net-new moat | Force-rebuild SVT derived data for converted textures (when DDC is stale) — non-blocking job-envelope — `(paths:[str]\|all_svt) -> {job_id, status, queued:int}` |
| `svt_report` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; AssetRegistry + size tags) | extends existing texture_streaming_report (only prior tool in domain) | Extend the existing texture_streaming_report to VT specifically: count of SVT vs regular, per-group VRAM, oversized non-VT textures recommended for conversion (the actionable read) — `(path_prefix?, top_n?:int=20) -> {svt_count, non_svt_count, total_vt_vram_mb, recommendations:[{path,size,reason}]}` |
| `vt_pool_config_get` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; SystemLibrary console var read + stat) | net-new moat | Read runtime VT physical-pool sizes / residency stats (r.VT.* cvars) so the agent can diagnose VT thrashing — `() -> {pool_size_mb:int, max_upload_per_frame:int, residency_pct:float, num_pages_mapped:int}` |
| `vt_pool_config_set` | P3 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; exec console commands) | net-new moat | Set VT pool / upload-budget cvars at runtime to fix thrashing without restart, dry-run aware — `(pool_size_mb?:int, max_upload_per_frame?:int, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, applied:{...}}` |
| `vt_debug_capture` | P2 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; set cvars) + editor_capture_viewport | net-new moat (extends Hayba vision-loop strongest-in-class) | Enable 'r.VT.Visualize'/RVT debug-viz and capture a viewport screenshot so the agent can SEE the RVT actually rendering (vision feedback loop) — returns image content block — `(mode:Literal[runtime_vt\|svt_residency\|page_table\|none], rvt_asset?, restore?:bool) -> {image: png, mode}` |
| `vt_validate` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate; cross-reads asset+volume+material+config) | net-new moat (PLUMB quantified validation; no competitor) | PLUMB-style quantified pipeline check: RVT volume has bounds-align actor + non-degenerate bounds, material output pins match RVT content type, ≥1 writer wired, streamed mips built, project support on, pool sized — green/red with unsat reasons (validate before/after mutate) — `(scope:Literal[volume\|rvt_asset\|level]=level, target?) -> {pass:bool, checks:[{name,pass,detail,severity}], unsat_core:[str]}` |
| `vt_pipeline_setup` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (pyTemplate orchestration; build step delegates to C++ job-envelope) | net-new moat (DCC outcome-over-operations + run_tool_script one-Ctrl+Z bundling) | One outcome-verb that scaffolds the whole RVT loop for a landscape: create RVT asset → spawn+align volume → bind landscape → optionally add sample node → validate, all as one transaction/undo (the differentiator vs per-call ops) — `(landscape, content_type, rvt_path, tile_size?, add_sample_to_material?, build_streamed_mips?:bool, dry_run?:bool) -> {ok, rvt_asset, volume, validate:{pass}, steps:[{name,ok}]}` |

**Dependencies:** Theme 0 transport/SEH fixes: rvt_volume_build_streamed_mips and svt_build MUST run as job-envelopes (HaybaMCPBuildHandler async pattern) or they re-introduce the build_* 300s game-thread freeze; Theme 1 add-a-tool refactor: all python_run-backed tools ship as pyTemplate descriptors (zero rebuild) — depends on the pyTemplate factory landing; Hardened python_run (SEH + 3-tier gate + dangling-delegate gate) — the execution substrate for ~28 of these 32 tools; Existing texture handler (HaybaMCPTextureHandler.cpp) + texture_streaming_report — svt_report/svt_enable build on its size-tag reads and the virtual_texture_streaming alias; Vision loop (editor_capture_viewport / wait_for_shaders) for vt_debug_capture; PLUMB validator harness for vt_validate (green/red plan-mode overlay, unsat-core surfacing); Transaction seam (FScopedTransaction / shared begin/commit) so vt_pipeline_setup bundles into one Ctrl+Z; UE modules: RuntimeVirtualTexture / VirtualTexturing must be enabled; r.VirtualTextures project setting (vt_check_support gates this)

**Risks:** Streamed-mip / SVT builds are genuinely long and DDC-bound — if not job-enveloped they freeze the editor (the named P0 bug class); must poll via rvt_build_status, never block; Enabling r.VirtualTextures / RVT support is a RESTART-required project setting — vt_enable_project_support can only flag needs_restart, not hot-apply; agents may wrongly assume it took effect; Material content-type ↔ output-pin mismatch silently produces black RVT; vt_validate must catch it but pin-name reflection across UE versions (5.7→5.8) is brittle; RVT volume bounds-align is rotation+bounds sensitive (level-set memory: landscape spawn quirks); set_bounds via Python may not exactly match the editor 'Set Bounds' button — needs live verification against C++ BuildBounds; Bulk svt_batch_convert can balloon DDC/cook time and convert normal maps that shouldn't be VT — dry_run default + exclude_normal_maps mitigations are mandatory, not optional; VirtualTextureStreaming change requires UpdateResource()/recompile; mass conversion may stall the asset registry — chunk the batch and respect frame budget; RuntimeVirtualTextureComponent / material-expression Python APIs are partially editor-only and version-locked (pinned 5.7.0); some setters may need legacy ue_invoke fallback or a thin C++ shim if reflection gaps appear

**Live-editor test plan:** On a live editor with a real ALandscape (per memory, must be a true Landscape, not the spawn placeholder): 1) vt_check_support → confirm support flags; if off, vt_enable_project_support and restart. 2) rvt_asset_create(/Game/VT/RVT_Terrain, BaseColor_Normal_Roughness) then rvt_asset_inspect to verify content_type + zero refs. 3) rvt_volume_spawn bound to the landscape, rvt_volume_set_bounds(snap_to_landscape=true); rvt_volume_inspect → bounds_min/max non-degenerate and aligned_to == landscape. 4) rvt_landscape_bind → rvt_audit_writers shows the landscape as a writer with RuntimeVirtualTexture pass. 5) On the landscape material: rvt_material_add_output(auto) and a consumer rvt_material_add_sample(RVT_Terrain); material_compile; rvt_material_inspect → no type mismatches. 6) rvt_volume_build_streamed_mips → poll rvt_build_status until done. 7) vt_debug_capture(runtime_vt) → screenshot shows the RVT page coverage over the terrain (vision verify), then capture(none) to restore. 8) vt_validate(level) → expect pass:true; deliberately break it (unbind landscape) and confirm unsat_core names the missing writer. 9) SVT side: import/identify a 4K texture, svt_batch_convert(dry_run=true) lists it, run with dry_run=false, svt_report shows svt_count incremented and est VRAM drop; svt_revert one and re-check. 10) Idempotency/safety: re-run rvt_asset_create on same path (exists handling), run a destructive delete with dry_run=true first, confirm one Ctrl+Z undoes a full vt_pipeline_setup run. Cross-check every footprint/format read-back against texture_streaming_report and the editor's own RVT volume details panel.


<a id="domain-30"></a>
## 30. DataTables / Curves / Gameplay Tags / Data Assets

*38 tools — P0:17 P1:12 P2:8 P3:1*

UE structured-data is a high-frequency authoring loop (game balance tables, AI/animation curves, ability tag taxonomies, config DataAssets) that today has only a 3-verb C++ stub (data_create/get/set, UDataAsset-only) and zero DataTable/Curve/GameplayTag coverage — asset-import handles FBX/USD/Datasmith but none of these. Nearly the entire surface is reachable through UE Python (DataTableFunctionLibrary fill/export CSV+JSON, RichCurve key APIs, GameplayTagsManager, AssetTools/AssetRegistry), so I deliver it as python_run-backed TS descriptors with no plugin rebuild, reserving only the existing C++ DataAsset reflection path. The moat plays are inspection-first (struct_inspect, *_inspect read tools so the agent knows columns/rowstruct/tag-tree before mutating), dry-run validation of CSV/JSON against the RowStruct before commit (PLUMB-style validate-before-mutate), and one-undo batch row/property writes. I prioritize the create + import + row-CRUD + introspection core (P0), then curve/curve-table + gameplay-tag management (P1), then batch/query/rename niceties (P2/P3). Every write verb pairs with a read-back verify and respects Plan-Mode for destructive ops.

| Tool | Pri | Module | Parity | Description / signature |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `datatable_create` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (AssetTools.create_asset + DataTableFactory, set row_struct via struct_inspect-resolved UScriptStruct) | GenOrca (datatable create); Flop has table verbs — we add RowStruct validation | Create a DataTable asset bound to a named RowStruct — the entry point for all tabular game data (balance, loot, dialogue). — `(path, name, row_struct, save?=true) -> {asset_path, row_struct, columns[]}` |
| `datatable_import_csv` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataTableFunctionLibrary.fill_data_table_from_csv_string) | GenOrca; net-new dry_run validation moat | Fill/replace a DataTable's rows from a CSV string or file against its RowStruct — bulk authoring path used in ~every balance workflow. — `(asset_path, csv?, csv_file?, dry_run?=false, replace?=true) -> {rows_imported, warnings[], unmatched_columns[]}` |
| `datatable_import_json` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataTableFunctionLibrary.fill_data_table_from_json_string) | GenOrca; net-new dry_run | Fill/replace DataTable rows from a JSON string or file — the lossless round-trip format for nested struct columns CSV can't express. — `(asset_path, json?, json_file?, dry_run?=false, replace?=true) -> {rows_imported, warnings[]}` |
| `datatable_export` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (export_data_table_to_csv_string / export_data_table_to_json_string) | GenOrca | Export a DataTable to a CSV or JSON string (and optionally a file) — read-back/verify after import and source-control snapshots. — `(asset_path, format='json'\|'csv', out_file?) -> {format, text, row_count}` |
| `datatable_inspect` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataTable.get_editor_property row_struct + reflection + get_data_table_row_names) | net-new moat (paired read tool, Flop inspection pattern) | Read a DataTable's RowStruct name, ordered column list with field types, and row count without dumping every row — inspection-first before mutate. — `(asset_path) -> {row_struct, columns:[{name,type}], row_count}` |
| `datatable_list_rows` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (DataTableFunctionLibrary.get_data_table_row_names) | GenOrca | List row names of a DataTable with pagination — cheap key enumeration before targeted row reads. — `(asset_path, limit?=50, offset?=0) -> {rows[], has_more, next_offset, total}` |
| `datatable_row_get` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (export-to-json round-trip, select by key) | net-new moat | Read one row by name as a JSON object — the verify half of single-row CRUD. — `(asset_path, row_name) -> {row_name, fields:{...}}` |
| `datatable_row_set` | P0 | python_run-backed TS (export json -> splice key -> fill_from_json), Modify()+save | GenOrca (coarser); net-new transaction+verify | Add or update a single row by name (JSON fields) in one transaction with read-back — the core incremental edit verb. — `(asset_path, row_name, fields, dry_run?=false) -> {row_name, created\|updated, fields}` |
| `datatable_rows_set_batch` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (single fill_from_json merge), one FScopedTransaction via ScopedEditorTransaction | net-new moat (one-Ctrl+Z batch) | Add/update many rows in ONE undo step — bulk balance edits without N import calls (named differentiator vs per-call undo). — `(asset_path, rows:[{row_name,fields}], dry_run?=false) -> {written, skipped[], warnings[]}` |
| `datatable_row_remove` | P1 | python_run-backed TS (export json -> drop keys -> fill replace) | GenOrca | Delete one or more rows by name — destructive, Plan-Mode gated, returns surviving count. — `(asset_path, row_names[], dry_run?=false) -> {removed[], not_found[], remaining}` |
